        payload.setdefault("_csrftoken", csrf)
        payload.setdefault("_uid", uid)
        payload.setdefault("_uuid", self.s.device.device_id)
        body = json.dumps(payload, separators=(",", ":"))
        if self._is_ios():
            r = await self.s.request(
                "POST", f"{self.BASE}/api/v1/{path}",
                data=body,
                headers={"Content-Type":
                    "application/json; charset=UTF-8"})
        else:
            r = await self.s.request(
                "POST", f"{self.BASE}/api/v1/{path}",
                data={"signed_body": f"SIGNATURE.{body}"},
                headers={"Content-Type":
                    "application/x-www-form-urlencoded; charset=UTF-8"})
        return r

    async def upload_story_photo(self, image_path: str):
        """Публикует СТОРИ из картинки. Возвращает media_id сторис."""
        from pathlib import Path
        if not Path(image_path).exists():
            raise UploadError(f"story image not found: {image_path}")
        with open(image_path, "rb") as f:
            img_bytes = f.read()
        upload_id = str(int(time.time() * 1000))
        dev = self.s.device
        entity_name = f"{upload_id}_0_{random.randint(1_000_000_000, 9_999_999_999)}"
        rupload_params = {
            "media_type": "1", "upload_id": upload_id,
            "for_direct_story": "1",
            "camera_session_id": str(uuid.uuid4()),
            "image_compression": json.dumps(
                {"lib_name": "moz", "lib_version": "3.1.m", "quality": "80"}),
            "retry_context": '{"num_step_auto_retry":0,"num_reupload":0,'
                             '"num_step_manual_retry":0}',
            "xsharing_user_ids": "[]",
        }
        headers = {
            "Offset": "0", "X-Entity-Length": str(len(img_bytes)),
            "X-Entity-Name": entity_name, "X-Entity-Type": "image/jpeg",
            "X-Instagram-Rupload-Params": json.dumps(rupload_params),
            "Content-Type": "application/octet-stream",
        }
        r = await self.s.request("POST", f"{self.BASE}/rupload_igphoto/{entity_name}",
                                 data=img_bytes, headers=headers, timeout=60)
        if r.status_code != 200:
            raise UploadError(f"story rupload: HTTP {r.status_code} {r.text[:200]}")
        await asyncio.sleep(random.uniform(2.0, 4.0))
        csrf, uid = "", ""
        for c in self.s._session.cookies.jar:
            if c.name == "csrftoken" and c.value:
                csrf = c.value
            elif c.name == "ds_user_id" and c.value:
                uid = c.value
        now = int(time.time())
        payload = {
            "_csrftoken": csrf,
            "_uid": uid or str(self.acc.get("uid") or self.acc.get("pk") or dev.device_id[:16]),
            "_uuid": dev.device_id,
            "device_id": dev.android_id or dev.device_id,
            "upload_id": upload_id,
            "caption": "",
            "source_type": "4",
            "configure_mode": "1",
            "capture_type": "normal",
            "story_media_creation_date": str(now - random.randint(11, 30)),
            "client_shared_at": str(now - random.randint(3, 10)),
            "client_timestamp": str(now),
            "create_at": str(now),
        }
        body = json.dumps(payload, separators=(",", ":"))
        attempts = []
        # попытка 1: signed_body form на media/configure_to_story
        # (тот же формат, что у рабочего configure_to_clips — работает на обеих платформах)
        try:
            r = await self.s.request(
                "POST", f"{self.BASE}/api/v1/media/configure_to_story/",
                data={"signed_body": f"SIGNATURE.{body}"},
                headers={"Content-Type":
                    "application/x-www-form-urlencoded; charset=UTF-8"})
            try:
                j = r.json()
            except Exception:
                attempts.append(f"media/signed: HTTP {r.status_code} {r.text[:120]}")
                j = None
            if j and j.get("status") == "ok":
                media = j.get("media") or {}
                return media.get("id") or media.get("pk")
            if j:
                attempts.append(f"media/signed: {str(j)[:150]}")
        except Exception as e:
            attempts.append(f"media/signed: {e}")
        # попытка 2: простой urlencoded (без подписи) на media/configure_to_story
        try:
            r = await self.s.request(
                "POST", f"{self.BASE}/api/v1/media/configure_to_story/",
                data=payload)
            try:
                j = r.json()
            except Exception:
                attempts.append(f"media/form: HTTP {r.status_code} {r.text[:120]}")
                j = None
            if j and j.get("status") == "ok":
                media = j.get("media") or {}
                return media.get("id") or media.get("pk")
            if j:
                attempts.append(f"media/form: {str(j)[:150]}")
        except Exception as e:
            attempts.append(f"media/form: {e}")
        raise UploadError("configure_to_story failed — " + " | ".join(attempts))

    async def share_reel_to_story(self, media_id: str, image_path: str,
                                  reel_url: str = ""):
        """Шарит рилс в стори: нативная карточка через story_feed_media
        (JSON-МАССИВ, как в реальном клиенте). Фолбэк — link-стикер на рилс."""
        from pathlib import Path
        if not image_path or not Path(image_path).exists():
            raise UploadError(f"story reshare: нет картинки-превью: {image_path}")
        reel_pk = str(media_id).split("_")[0]
        dev = self.s.device
        csrf, uid = "", ""
        for c in self.s._session.cookies.jar:
            if c.name == "csrftoken" and c.value:
                csrf = c.value
            elif c.name == "ds_user_id" and c.value:
                uid = c.value
        if not uid:
            uid = str(self.acc.get("uid") or self.acc.get("pk") or "")
        now = int(time.time())
        camera_session = str(uuid.uuid4())
        attempts = []

        async def _rupload_photo():
            with open(image_path, "rb") as f:
                img_bytes = f.read()
            up_id = str(int(time.time() * 1000))
            entity = f"{up_id}_0_{random.randint(1_000_000_000, 9_999_999_999)}"
            rp = {
                "media_type": "1", "upload_id": up_id,
                "for_direct_story": "1",
                "camera_session_id": camera_session,
                "image_compression": json.dumps(
                    {"lib_name": "moz", "lib_version": "3.1.m", "quality": "80"}),
                "retry_context": '{"num_step_auto_retry":0,"num_reupload":0,'
                                 '"num_step_manual_retry":0}',
                "xsharing_user_ids": "[]",
            }
            h = {
                "Offset": "0", "X-Entity-Length": str(len(img_bytes)),
                "X-Entity-Name": entity, "X-Entity-Type": "image/jpeg",
                "X-Instagram-Rupload-Params": json.dumps(rp),
                "Content-Type": "application/octet-stream",
            }
            rr = await self.s.request("POST", f"{self.BASE}/rupload_igphoto/{entity}",
                                      data=img_bytes, headers=h, timeout=60)
            if rr.status_code != 200:
                raise UploadError(f"reshare rupload: HTTP {rr.status_code}")
            await asyncio.sleep(random.uniform(2.0, 4.0))
            return up_id

        def _payload(upload_id, source_type, with_feed_media):
            p = {
                "_csrftoken": csrf, "_uid": uid, "_uuid": dev.device_id,
                "upload_id": upload_id,
                "source_type": source_type,
                "configure_mode": "1",
                "camera_session_id": camera_session,
                "story_media_creation_date": str(now - random.randint(11, 30)),
                "client_shared_at": str(now - random.randint(3, 10)),
                "client_timestamp": str(now),
                "client_context": str(uuid.uuid4()),
                "supported_capabilities_new": json.dumps({}),
                "xsharing_user_ids": "[]",
            }
            if with_feed_media:
                # КРИТИЧНО: story_feed_media — JSON МАССИВ [{...}], не объект.
                # Объект IG молча дропает — сторис публикуется без карточки.
                p["story_feed_media"] = json.dumps([{
                    "x": 0.5, "y": 0.5, "z": 0,
                    "width": 0.6, "height": 0.8,
                    "rotation": 0.0,
                    "is_sticker": True,
                    "is_hidden": False,
                    "media_id": reel_pk,
                    "product_type": "clips",
                    "media_type": 2,
                }])
            return p

        async def _try_configure(p, tag):
            body = json.dumps(p, separators=(",", ":"))
            for label, data in (
                ("signed", {"signed_body": f"SIGNATURE.{body}"}),
                ("form", p),
            ):
                try:
                    r = await self.s.request(
                        "POST", f"{self.BASE}/api/v1/media/configure_to_story/",
                        data=data,
                        headers={"Content-Type":
                            "application/x-www-form-urlencoded; charset=UTF-8"})
                    try:
                        j = r.json()
                    except Exception:
                        attempts.append(f"{tag}/{label}: HTTP {r.status_code}")
                        continue
                    if j.get("status") == "ok":
                        media = j.get("media") or {}
                        sid = media.get("id") or media.get("pk")
                        has_card = bool(media.get("story_feed_media")
                                        or media.get("story_sticker_ids"))
                        print(f"[reshare:{self.acc.get('login')}] "
                              f"{'карточка рилса OK' if has_card else 'сторис без карточки'} "
                              f"({tag}/{label})")
                        return sid, has_card
                    attempts.append(f"{tag}/{label}: {str(j)[:100]}")
                except Exception as e:
                    attempts.append(f"{tag}/{label}: {e}")
            return None, False

        # ── одна сторис с link-стикером на рилс (нативную карточку IG дропает) ──
        up = await _rupload_photo()
        link = reel_url or f"https://www.instagram.com/reel/{reel_pk}/"
        p = _payload(up, "4", False)
        p["story_sticker_ids"] = json.dumps(
            [f"link_sticker_{uuid.uuid4().hex.upper()}"])
        p["link_url"] = link
        p["story_link_url"] = link
        p["story_link_text"] = "Открыть рилс"
        sid, _ = await _try_configure(p, "link")
        if sid:
            return sid
        raise UploadError("share_reel_to_story failed — " + " | ".join(attempts))

    @staticmethod
    def _extract_media_ids(j, limit=40):
        """Универсальный парсер: достаёт media_id из items независимо от формата."""
        ids = []
        for it in (j.get("items") or []):
            m = it.get("media") or it  # item может быть самим медиа
            if not isinstance(m, dict):
                continue
            if m.get("media_type") == 2 or m.get("product_type") == "clips" \
               or "video_versions" in m or "image_versions2" in m:
                mid = m.get("id") or m.get("pk")
                if mid:
                    ids.append(str(mid))
        return ids[:limit]
    async def self_engage(self, media_id: str, like: bool = True):
        """Автор сам смотрит свой рилс (media/seen) и лайкает его."""
        dev = self.s.device
        # 1) просмотр своего рилса
        seen_body = json.dumps({
            "reels": json.dumps({str(media_id): {"skipped_timing": "0"}}),
            "live_reels": "{}",
        }, separators=(",", ":"))
        await self._post_api("media/seen", {
            "_uuid": dev.device_id,
            "reels": seen_body,
            "live_reels": "{}",
        })
        if not like:
            return
        # 2) лайк своего рилса
        csrf, uid = "", ""
        for c in self.s._session.cookies.jar:
            if c.name == "csrftoken" and c.value:
                csrf = c.value
            elif c.name == "ds_user_id" and c.value:
                uid = c.value
        if not uid:
            uid = str(self.acc.get("uid") or self.acc.get("pk") or "")
        payload = {
            "_csrftoken": csrf, "_uid": uid, "_uuid": dev.device_id,
            "media_id": str(media_id),
            "module_name": "reel_feed_timeline",
        }
        body = json.dumps(payload, separators=(",", ":"))
        r = await self.s.request(
            "POST", f"{self.BASE}/api/v1/media/{media_id}/like/",
            data={"signed_body": f"SIGNATURE.{body}"},
            headers={"Content-Type":
                "application/x-www-form-urlencoded; charset=UTF-8"})
        try:
            j = r.json()
        except Exception:
            raise UploadError(f"self_like non-JSON HTTP {r.status_code}: {r.text[:120]}")
        if j.get("status") != "ok":
            raise UploadError(f"self_like failed: {str(j)[:150]}")
    async def watch_reels(self, count: int = 3, query: str = ""):
        """Смотрит N рилсов с паузами. query = тематический поиск."""
        dev = self.s.device
        media_ids = []
        if query:
            r = await self.s.request("GET", f"{self.BASE}/api/v1/fbsearch/top_search/",
                                     params={"q": query, "count": "30"})
            try:
                j = r.json()
            except Exception:
                j = {}
            media_ids += self._extract_media_ids(j)
        if len(media_ids) < count:
            session_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
            r = await self._post_api("clips/feed", {
                "supported_capabilities_new": json.dumps({}),
                "session_id": session_id,
                "page_size": "10",
                "include_feed_video": "true",
            })
            try:
                j = r.json()
            except Exception:
                j = {}
            media_ids += self._extract_media_ids(j)
        # фолбэк: если поиск и лента пустые — берём сторис-трей
        if not media_ids:
            try:
                r = await self.s.request("GET", f"{self.BASE}/api/v1/feed/reels_tray/")
                j = r.json()
                for seg in (j.get("tray") or []):
                    mid = (seg.get("id") or seg.get("media", {}).get("id"))
                    if mid:
                        media_ids.append(str(mid))
            except Exception:
                pass
        random.shuffle(media_ids)
        watched = 0
        for mid in media_ids:
            if watched >= count:
                break
            await asyncio.sleep(random.uniform(4.0, 10.0))
            seen_body = json.dumps({
                "reels": json.dumps({mid: {"skipped_timing": "0"}}),
                "live_reels": "{}",
            }, separators=(",", ":"))
            try:
                await self._post_api("media/seen", {
                    "_uuid": dev.device_id,
                    "reels": seen_body,
                    "live_reels": "{}",
                })
                watched += 1
            except Exception:
                continue
        return watched

    async def follow_by_query(self, query: str, count: int = 3):
        """Ищет юзеров по запросу и подписывается на count штук."""
        r = await self.s.request("GET", f"{self.BASE}/api/v1/users/search/",
                                 params={"q": query, "count": "30"})
        try:
            users = r.json().get("users", [])
        except Exception:
            users = []
        users = [u for u in users if not u.get("is_verified") and not u.get("is_private")]
        random.shuffle(users)
        followed = 0
        for u in users:
            if followed >= count:
                break
            await asyncio.sleep(random.uniform(12.0, 30.0))
            try:
                r = await self._post_api(f"friendships/create/{u.get('pk')}/",
                                         {"user_id": str(u.get("pk"))})
                j = r.json()
            except Exception:
                continue
            if j.get("status") == "ok":
                followed += 1
        return followed

    async def save_story_to_highlight(self, story_media_id: str, title: str = "Reels"):
        """Создаёт актуальное из фотостори. Перебирает source_type: '3' — рабочий."""
        dev = self.s.device
        csrf, uid = "", ""
        for c in self.s._session.cookies.jar:
            if c.name == "csrftoken" and c.value:
                csrf = c.value
            elif c.name == "ds_user_id" and c.value:
                uid = c.value
        if not uid:
            uid = str(self.acc.get("uid") or self.acc.get("pk") or "")
        story_pk = str(story_media_id).split("_")[0]
        attempts = []
        for st in ("3", "1", "self_story"):
            payload = {
                "_uid": uid, "_uuid": dev.device_id, "_csrftoken": csrf,
                "supported_capabilities_new": json.dumps({}),
                "media_ids": json.dumps([story_pk]),
                "title": title[:15],
                "source_type": st,
                "creation_mode": "new",
                "disable_media_id": "",
            }
            body = json.dumps(payload, separators=(",", ":"))
            for label, data in (
                ("signed", {"signed_body": f"SIGNATURE.{body}"}),
                ("form", payload),
            ):
                try:
                    r = await self.s.request(
                        "POST", f"{self.BASE}/api/v1/highlights/create_reel/",
                        data=data,
                        headers={"Content-Type":
                            "application/x-www-form-urlencoded; charset=UTF-8"})
                    try:
                        j = r.json()
                    except Exception:
                        attempts.append(f"st{st}/{label}: HTTP {r.status_code}")
                        continue
                    if j.get("status") == "ok":
                        print(f"[highlight:{self.acc.get('login')}] "
                              f"актуальное создано (source_type={st})")
                        return j
                    attempts.append(f"st{st}/{label}: {str(j)[:100]}")
                except Exception as e:
                    attempts.append(f"st{st}/{label}: {e}")
            await asyncio.sleep(random.uniform(10, 20))
        raise UploadError("highlight failed — " + " | ".join(attempts))

    # ═══════════════ CAPTION FIX / RECOVERY ═══════════════
    async def _edit_media_caption(self, media_id: str, caption: str,
                                    wait_before: float = 60.0) -> bool:
        """Force-обновляет caption. feedback_required и сетевые сбои (включая
        прокси-SSL) считаются ретраебельными — до 3 попыток с нарастающей паузой."""
        if not caption or not media_id:
            return False
        login = self.acc.get("login", "?")
        waits = [wait_before, wait_before + 120, wait_before + 240]
        for attempt, w in enumerate(waits, 1):
            if w > 0:
                print(f"[caption-fix:{login}] попытка #{attempt}, жду {int(w)}с")
                await asyncio.sleep(w + random.uniform(-3, 5))
            dev = self.s.device
            uid, csrf = "", ""
            try:
                for c in self.s._session.cookies.jar:
                    if c.name == "ds_user_id" and c.value:
                        uid = c.value
                    elif c.name == "csrftoken" and c.value:
                        csrf = c.value
            except Exception:
                pass
            payload = {
                "_csrftoken": csrf,
                "_uid": uid or str(self.acc.get("uid") or self.acc.get("pk") or ""),
                "_uuid": dev.device_id,
                "container_module": "edit_media_info_page",
                "device_id": dev.android_id or dev.device_id,
                "caption_text": caption,
            }
            try:
                body = json.dumps(payload, separators=(",", ":"))
                r = await self.s.request(
                    "POST", f"{self.BASE}/api/v1/media/{media_id}/edit_media/",
                    data={"signed_body": f"SIGNATURE.{body}"},
                    headers={"Content-Type":
                        "application/x-www-form-urlencoded; charset=UTF-8"})
                try:
                    j = r.json()
                except Exception:
                    print(f"[caption-fix:{login}] non-JSON HTTP {r.status_code}")
                    continue
                if j.get("status") == "ok":
                    print(f"[caption-fix:{login}] ✓ caption применён (попытка #{attempt})")
                    return True
                msg = str(j.get("message", "")).lower()
                if "feedback_required" in msg:
                    print(f"[caption-fix:{login}] feedback_required — ретрай позже")
                    continue
                print(f"[caption-fix:{login}] edit_media fail: {str(j)[:150]}")
                return False
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"[caption-fix:{login}] сетевой сбой ({type(e).__name__}) — ретрай")
                continue
        print(f"[caption-fix:{login}] все {len(waits)} попыток исчерпаны")
        return False

    async def _find_recently_uploaded_media(self, user_id: str,
                                             upload_id: str,
                                             max_age_sec: int = 300) -> str | None:
        """
        Ищет только что созданный media в feed_user по времени.
        Используется когда configure_to_clips вернул ошибку, но media реально создалась.
        Возвращает media_id или None.
        """
        try:
            r = await self.s.request(
                "GET", f"{self.BASE}/api/v1/feed/user/{user_id}/",
                params={"count": "6"})
            if r.status_code != 200 or not r.text:
                return None
            j = r.json()
            if j.get("status") != "ok":
                return None
            items = j.get("items", []) or []
            import time as _t
            now = _t.time()
            for item in items:
                taken_at = int(item.get("taken_at") or 0)
                if now - taken_at > max_age_sec:
                    continue
                is_reel = (item.get("product_type") == "clips"
                          or item.get("media_type") == 2
                          or "clips_metadata" in item)
                if not is_reel:
                    continue
                mid = item.get("id") or item.get("pk")
                if mid:
                    return str(mid)
            return None
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[media-find:{self.acc.get('login')}] error: {e}")
            return None

    async def _recover_caption_via_feed(self, caption: str,
                                         upload_id: str) -> dict | None:
        """
        Fallback: configure_to_clips завершился ошибкой, но media могла создаться.
        Ищем свежий рилс в ленте юзера и форсим caption.
        Возвращает синтетический result dict {'status': 'ok', 'media': {...}} или None.
        """
        login = self.acc.get("login", "?")
        uid = None
        try:
            for c in self.s._session.cookies.jar:
                if c.name == "ds_user_id" and c.value:
                    uid = c.value
                    break
        except Exception:
            pass
        if not uid:
            print(f"[caption-recover:{login}] no ds_user_id in cookies")
            return None

        await asyncio.sleep(random.uniform(8, 15))

        media_id = await self._find_recently_uploaded_media(uid, upload_id)
        if not media_id:
            print(f"[caption-recover:{login}] media в ленте не найдено — "
                  f"считаем что configure реально провалился")
            return None

        print(f"[caption-recover:{login}] найдено свежее media {media_id[:20]}, "
              f"форсю caption")
        if caption:
            await asyncio.sleep(random.uniform(2, 4))
            ok = await self._edit_media_caption(media_id, caption)
            if not ok:
                print(f"[caption-recover:{login}] edit_media провалился — "
                      f"caption не применится, но рилс жив")

        return {"status": "ok", "media": {"id": media_id, "pk": media_id},
                "_recovered_via_feed": True}

    async def _upload_cover(self, upload_id: str, cover_path: str):
        with open(cover_path, "rb") as f:
            data = f.read()
        entity_name = f"{upload_id}_0_{random.randint(1_000_000_000, 9_999_999_999)}"
        headers = {
            "X-Entity-Length": str(len(data)), "X-Entity-Name": entity_name,
            "X-Entity-Type": "image/jpeg",
            "X-Instagram-Rupload-Params": json.dumps({
                "media_type": "1", "upload_id": upload_id,
                "image_compression": json.dumps({
                    "lib_name": "moz", "lib_version": "3.1.m", "quality": "80"})}),
            "Offset": "0", "Content-Type": "application/octet-stream",
        }
        r = await self.s.request("POST",
            f"{self.BASE}/rupload_igphoto/{entity_name}",
            data=data, headers=headers, timeout=30)
        if r.status_code != 200:
            print(f"[cover] upload returned {r.status_code}: {r.text[:150]}")
        await asyncio.sleep(random.uniform(1.5, 3.0))

    # ═══════════════ STATS — БЕЗОПАСНЫЙ РЕЖИМ ═══════════════
    async def get_self_stats(self, user_id: str, mode: str = "light") -> dict:
        """
        mode:
          'light'  — ТОЛЬКО /users/{id}/info/. Followers/following/media_count.
                     Никаких просмотров рилсов. Максимально безопасно.
          'combo'  — light + просмотры одного последнего рилса через
                     /media/{id}/info/. Средне-безопасно.
          'deep'   — старое поведение: /feed/user/ + /clips/user/. ОПАСНО —
                     используй только для 1-2% выборки на A/B тестах.
        """
        # 1) profile info — единственный обязательный запрос
        r = await self.s.request("GET", f"{self.BASE}/api/v1/users/{user_id}/info/")
        if r.status_code == 429:
            raise RateLimitError("429 on user_info")
        if 500 <= r.status_code < 600:
            raise TransientError(f"HTTP {r.status_code} on user_info")
        try:
            j = r.json()
        except Exception:
            raise TransientError(f"stats non-JSON HTTP {r.status_code}")

        if j.get("status") != "ok" or not j.get("user"):
            msg = str(j.get("message", ""))
            if "user_not_found" in msg or "does not exist" in msg:
                raise SuspendedError("user_not_found — акк деактивирован/удалён")
            raise TransientError(f"stats fail: {msg[:150]}")

        info = j["user"]
        result = {
            "followers":      int(info.get("follower_count") or 0),
            "following":      int(info.get("following_count") or 0),
            "media_count":    int(info.get("media_count") or 0),
            "reels_count":    0,
            "total_views":    0,
            "total_likes":    0,
            "total_comments": 0,
            "total_reposts":  0,
            "username":       info.get("username"),
            "is_private":     bool(info.get("is_private", False)),
            "is_suspended":   False,
            "stats_mode":     mode,
        }

        if mode == "light":
            return result

        if mode == "combo":
            try:
                if info.get("media_count", 0) > 0:
                    r2 = await self.s.request(
                        "GET",
                        f"{self.BASE}/api/v1/highlights/{user_id}/highlights_tray/",
                    )
                    pass
            except Exception:
                pass
            return result

        return await self._get_stats_deep(user_id, result)

    async def get_media_info(self, media_id: str) -> dict:
        """
        Безопасно получает статистику ОДНОГО рилса через /media/{id}/info/.
        Не палит акк — этот эндпоинт вызывается когда пользователь смотрит
        свой же рилс. Один запрос, минимальный footprint.
        Возвращает: {play_count, like_count, comment_count, reshare_count, code}
        """
        r = await self.s.request(
            "GET", f"{self.BASE}/api/v1/media/{media_id}/info/")

        if r.status_code == 429:
            raise RateLimitError("429 on media/info")
        if 500 <= r.status_code < 600:
            raise TransientError(f"HTTP {r.status_code} on media/info")
        if r.status_code == 404:
            return {"deleted": True}

        try:
            j = r.json()
        except Exception:
            raise TransientError(f"media/info non-JSON HTTP {r.status_code}")

        items = j.get("items") or []
        if not items:
            return {"deleted": True, "raw": j}

        item = items[0]
        return {
            "media_id":      item.get("id") or item.get("pk"),
            "code":          item.get("code"),
            "play_count":    int(item.get("play_count") or item.get("view_count") or 0),
            "like_count":    int(item.get("like_count") or 0),
            "comment_count": int(item.get("comment_count") or 0),
            "reshare_count": int(item.get("reshare_count") or item.get("share_count") or 0),
            "taken_at":      item.get("taken_at"),
            "caption":       (item.get("caption") or {}).get("text", "")[:200] if item.get("caption") else "",
            "product_type":  item.get("product_type"),
            "deleted":       False,
        }

    async def _get_stats_deep(self, user_id: str, base: dict) -> dict:
        """Опасный режим — оставлен для A/B тестов и одноразовых аудитов."""
        seen_ids = set()
        views = likes = comments = reposts = 0
        reels_count = 0

        try:
            r = await self.s.request("GET",
                f"{self.BASE}/api/v1/feed/user/{user_id}/",
                params={"count": "50"})
            if r.text and r.text.strip():
                feed_j = r.json()
                for item in feed_j.get("items", []):
                    is_reel = (item.get("product_type") == "clips"
                               or item.get("media_type") == 2
                               or "clips_metadata" in item)
                    if not is_reel: continue
                    mid = item.get("id") or item.get("pk")
                    if mid in seen_ids: continue
                    seen_ids.add(mid)
                    reels_count += 1
                    views    += int(item.get("play_count", 0) or item.get("view_count", 0) or 0)
                    likes    += int(item.get("like_count", 0) or 0)
                    comments += int(item.get("comment_count", 0) or 0)
                    reposts  += int(item.get("reshare_count", 0) or item.get("share_count", 0) or 0)
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[stats deep] feed error: {e}")

        base.update({
            "reels_count":    reels_count,
            "total_views":    views,
            "total_likes":    likes,
            "total_comments": comments,
            "total_reposts":  reposts,
            "stats_mode":     "deep",
        })
        return base

    # ═══════════════ utils ═══════════════
    async def _pause(self, lo: float, hi: float):
        mu = (lo + hi) / 2
        val = random.lognormvariate(mu * 0.6, 0.35)
        delay = max(lo, min(hi * 1.5, val))
        loop = asyncio.get_event_loop()
        await loop.run_in_executor(None, time.sleep, delay)

    async def _probe_duration(self, path: str) -> int:
        try:
            out = subprocess.check_output([
                "ffprobe", "-v", "error", "-show_entries", "format=duration",
                "-of", "default=nw=1:nk=1", path,
            ]).decode().strip()
            return int(float(out) * 1000)
        except Exception as e:
            raise UploadError(f"ffprobe duration failed: {e}. Ставим ffmpeg/ffprobe в PATH или в ./bin/")

    async def _extract_frame(self, video_path: str) -> str:
        tmp = tempfile.mktemp(suffix=".jpg")
        try:
            subprocess.run(
                ["ffmpeg", "-y", "-i", video_path, "-vframes", "1",
                 "-q:v", "2", tmp], check=True, capture_output=True)
            return tmp
        except Exception as e:
            raise UploadError(
                f"ffmpeg frame extraction failed: {e}. "
                f"Не заливаем чёрный cover — IG считает это low-quality.")
core/image_uniquifier.py
"""
Уникализатор картинок для аватарок / covers.
Ломает pHash + PhotoDNA + чистит EXIF + меняет file-level сигнатуру.

Что делаем:
  1. Открываем через PIL → полностью пересобираем pixel-data (ломает CRC/hash файла)
  2. Убираем ВСЕ EXIF/ICC (Meta не любит пустой EXIF, поэтому ставим фейковый iPhone/Samsung)
  3. Micro-crop 1-4px по краям → сдвиг pHash-грида
  4. Резайз ±1-3% → ломает DCT-коэффициенты
  5. Micro-rotation ±0.3° → ломает симметричные фингерпринты
  6. Легкая коррекция brightness/contrast/saturation ±1-2%
  7. Добавляем PIL-noise на 1-2% пикселей (imperceptible visually)
  8. JPEG re-encode с случайным quality 82-91 → ломает JPEG DCT-hash
  9. Случайные размеры финального файла (padding до 320/640/720/1080 px)

Не искажает картинку заметно для глаза, но каждый выход = уникальный fingerprint.
"""
import io
import random
import tempfile
import asyncio
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime, timedelta

from PIL import Image, ImageEnhance, ImageOps


# фейковые EXIF-профили (подставляются вместо пустого/палевного EXIF)
_FAKE_EXIF_PROFILES = [
    {
        "Make": "Apple",
        "Model": "iPhone 15 Pro",
        "Software": "17.5.1",
        "LensModel": "iPhone 15 Pro back triple camera 6.86mm f/1.78",
    },
    {
        "Make": "Apple",
        "Model": "iPhone 14",
        "Software": "17.4",
        "LensModel": "iPhone 14 back dual wide camera 5.7mm f/1.5",
    },
    {
        "Make": "samsung",
        "Model": "SM-S928B",
        "Software": "S928BXXU5CXIA",
    },
    {
        "Make": "samsung",
        "Model": "SM-S918B",
        "Software": "S918BXXUAFXIA",
    },
    {
        "Make": "Google",
        "Model": "Pixel 8 Pro",
        "Software": "HG4ADB.240705.001",
    },
    {
        "Make": "Xiaomi",
        "Model": "23117RA68G",
        "Software": "V816.0.9.0.UNRMIXM",
    },
]


class ImageUniquifier:

    @staticmethod
    def unique(src: str,
               dst: str | None = None,
               target_size: int | None = None,
               is_avatar: bool = False) -> str:
        """
        Обрабатывает картинку.
        :param src: путь к оригиналу
        :param dst: куда сохранить (по умолчанию — tmp)
        :param target_size: если задан — финальный размер (квадрат) в px
        :param is_avatar: аватары IG хочет квадратные, cover'ы 9:16
        :return: путь к уникализированной картинке
        """
        dst = dst or tempfile.mktemp(suffix=".jpg")

        img = Image.open(src).convert("RGB")

        # 1. Micro-crop 1-4 px по каждой стороне (rand на каждую сторону отдельно)
        w, h = img.size
        left = random.randint(1, 4)
        top = random.randint(1, 4)
        right = w - random.randint(1, 4)
        bottom = h - random.randint(1, 4)
        img = img.crop((left, top, right, bottom))

        # 2. Micro-rotation ±0.3° (imperceptible)
        angle = random.uniform(-0.3, 0.3)
        img = img.rotate(angle, resample=Image.BICUBIC, fillcolor=(0, 0, 0))

        # 3. Финальный размер — под целевой формат
        if is_avatar:
            # аватарка IG — квадрат
            side = target_size or random.choice([320, 360, 400, 480, 512, 640, 720, 800])
            img = ImageOps.fit(img, (side, side), method=Image.LANCZOS)
        else:
            # cover — вертикаль 9:16
            base_w = target_size or random.choice([720, 810, 900, 1080])
            base_h = int(base_w * 16 / 9)
            img = ImageOps.fit(img, (base_w, base_h), method=Image.LANCZOS)

        # 4. Резайз ±1-3% и обратно к целевому — ломает DCT
        w, h = img.size
        drift = random.uniform(0.98, 1.03)
        img = img.resize(
            (int(w * drift), int(h * drift)),
            resample=Image.LANCZOS
        )
        img = img.resize((w, h), resample=Image.LANCZOS)

        # 5. Легкая коррекция цвета
        img = ImageEnhance.Brightness(img).enhance(random.uniform(0.98, 1.02))
        img = ImageEnhance.Contrast(img).enhance(random.uniform(0.98, 1.02))
        img = ImageEnhance.Color(img).enhance(random.uniform(0.97, 1.03))
        img = ImageEnhance.Sharpness(img).enhance(random.uniform(0.95, 1.05))

        # 6. Micro-noise на случайных пикселях (~0.5-1% пикселей)
        # ломает pixel-hash не касаясь визуального восприятия
        pixels = img.load()
        w, h = img.size
        noise_count = int(w * h * random.uniform(0.005, 0.010))
        for _ in range(noise_count):
            x = random.randint(0, w - 1)
            y = random.randint(0, h - 1)
            r, g, b = pixels[x, y]
            dr = random.randint(-3, 3)
            dg = random.randint(-3, 3)
            db = random.randint(-3, 3)
            pixels[x, y] = (
                max(0, min(255, r + dr)),
                max(0, min(255, g + dg)),
                max(0, min(255, b + db)),
            )

        # 7. JPEG re-encode с random quality + фейковый EXIF
        quality = random.randint(82, 91)
        exif_bytes = ImageUniquifier._build_fake_exif()

        img.save(
            dst,
            "JPEG",
            quality=quality,
            optimize=True,
            progressive=random.choice([True, False]),
            exif=exif_bytes,
            subsampling=random.choice([0, 1, 2]),  # 4:4:4 / 4:2:2 / 4:2:0
        )
        return dst
# ================================================================
    @staticmethod
    async def unique_async(src: str,
                           dst: str | None = None,
                           target_size: int | None = None,
                           is_avatar: bool = False) -> str:
        """Асинхронная обёртка — запускает unique() в thread pool."""
        loop = asyncio.get_event_loop()
        return await loop.run_in_executor(
            None,  # используем default executor
            ImageUniquifier.unique,
            src, dst, target_size, is_avatar
        )
    # ================================================================
    @staticmethod
    def _build_fake_exif() -> bytes:
        """Собирает минимальный, но реалистичный EXIF-блок под iPhone/Samsung/Pixel."""
        try:
            import piexif
        except ImportError:
            return b""

        profile = random.choice(_FAKE_EXIF_PROFILES)

        # случайная дата съёмки в пределах последних 60 дней
        dt = datetime.now() - timedelta(
            days=random.randint(1, 60),
            hours=random.randint(0, 23),
            minutes=random.randint(0, 59),
        )
        dt_str = dt.strftime("%Y:%m:%d %H:%M:%S")

        zeroth = {
            piexif.ImageIFD.Make: profile["Make"].encode(),
            piexif.ImageIFD.Model: profile["Model"].encode(),
            piexif.ImageIFD.Software: profile["Software"].encode(),
            piexif.ImageIFD.DateTime: dt_str.encode(),
            piexif.ImageIFD.Orientation: 1,
            piexif.ImageIFD.XResolution: (72, 1),
            piexif.ImageIFD.YResolution: (72, 1),
            piexif.ImageIFD.ResolutionUnit: 2,
        }
        exif = {
            piexif.ExifIFD.DateTimeOriginal: dt_str.encode(),
            piexif.ExifIFD.DateTimeDigitized: dt_str.encode(),
            piexif.ExifIFD.ExposureTime: (1, random.randint(30, 250)),
            piexif.ExifIFD.FNumber: (random.choice([15, 18, 22, 28]), 10),
            piexif.ExifIFD.ISOSpeedRatings: random.choice([32, 50, 100, 200, 400]),
            piexif.ExifIFD.FocalLength: (random.choice([26, 28, 35, 50, 57]), 10),
            piexif.ExifIFD.LensModel: profile.get("LensModel", "").encode(),
            piexif.ExifIFD.ColorSpace: 1,
        }

        exif_dict = {
            "0th": zeroth,
            "Exif": exif,
            "1st": {},
            "thumbnail": None,
            "GPS": {},   # намеренно пусто — многие юзеры отключают геометки
        }
        try:
            return piexif.dump(exif_dict)
        except Exception:
            return b""
core/orchestrator.py
"""
Orchestrator v2 — точная классификация ошибок + fresh-acc soft flow +
A/B experiment tracking.
"""
import asyncio
import random
import hashlib
import json
import time
from asyncio import Semaphore
from pathlib import Path

from core.asset_picker import AssetPicker
from core.image_uniquifier import ImageUniquifier
from core.device import DeviceProfile
from core.session import IGSession
from core.geo import GeoResolver
from core.uniquifier import ReelUniquifier
from core.ig_client import (
    IGClient, UploadError,
    SuspendedError, CheckpointError, TwoFactorRequired,
    SessionExpiredError, RateLimitError, TransientError, LoginError,
)
from core.checkpoint import CheckpointSolver


class Orchestrator:
    _running_logins: set = set()
    _running_lock = None

    def __init__(self, threads: int, on_status, db=None, settings: dict = None,
                 stats_sink=None, geo: GeoResolver = None,
                 experiment_variant: str = None):
        self.sem = Semaphore(threads)
        self.on_status = on_status
        self.db = db
        self._caption_queue = asyncio.Queue()
        self._caption_worker_started = False
        self.settings = settings or {}
        self.stats_sink = stats_sink
        self.geo = geo or GeoResolver(db_path="data/geo_cache.db")
        self.experiment_variant = experiment_variant

    @classmethod
    def _get_lock(cls):
        if cls._running_lock is None:
            cls._running_lock = asyncio.Lock()
        return cls._running_lock

    async def _sleep(self, sec: float):
        if sec <= 0: return
        loop = asyncio.get_event_loop()
        await loop.run_in_executor(None, time.sleep, sec)

    async def run_batch(self, accounts: list, action: str, params: dict):
        async with self._get_lock():
            skipped_logins = []
            actual_accounts = []
            actual_indices = []
            for i, a in enumerate(accounts):
                login = a.get("login", "")
                if login in self._running_logins:
                    skipped_logins.append(login)
                else:
                    self._running_logins.add(login)
                    actual_accounts.append(a)
                    actual_indices.append(i)

        if skipped_logins:
            print(f"[orch] пропущено {len(skipped_logins)} акков — уже в работе")
        if not actual_accounts:
            return

        try:
            tasks = [asyncio.create_task(
                        self._run_one(actual_indices[k], a, action, params))
                     for k, a in enumerate(actual_accounts)]
            await asyncio.gather(*tasks, return_exceptions=True)
        finally:
            async with self._get_lock():
                for a in actual_accounts:
                    self._running_logins.discard(a.get("login", ""))

    # ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    def _is_fresh_account(self, acc: dict) -> bool:
        """Fresh = 0 рилсов И нет last_error И импорт < 24 часов назад."""
        if int(acc.get("reels_count") or 0) > 0:
            return False
        if acc.get("last_error"):
            return False
        # если stats_updated_at недавно и там 0 рилсов — тоже fresh
        return True

    def _adjust_params_for_fresh(self, acc: dict, action: str,
                                 params: dict) -> dict:
        """
        Если акк fresh — смягчаем параметры чтобы не спалить его сразу.
        Не модифицирует params inplace.
        """
        if not self._is_fresh_account(acc):
            return params
        soft = dict(params)
        # задержки х2
        soft["delay_min"] = int(soft.get("delay_min", 25) * 2)
        soft["delay_max"] = int(soft.get("delay_max", 90) * 2)
        # warmup минимальный (много запросов на fresh акк = флаг)
        soft["warmup"] = "min"
        # ensure_public выключаем на первом заливе — сначала пусть просто зальётся
        if action == "upload_reel":
            soft["ensure_public_override"] = False
        # первый setup — если акк реально свежий, ставим bio/avatar ОДНИМ большим
        # блоком с большими паузами
        soft["_fresh"] = True
        return soft

    # ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    async def _run_one(self, row, acc, action, params):
        async with self.sem:
            # ── fresh-account адаптация ──
            effective_params = self._adjust_params_for_fresh(acc, action, params)

            spread = min(60.0, effective_params.get("delay_max", 90))
            initial_delay = random.uniform(0.3, spread)
            if effective_params.get("_fresh"):
                # добавляем ещё до 30 сек джиттера чтобы fresh не били в одну минуту
                initial_delay += random.uniform(0, 30)
            await self._sleep(initial_delay)

            self.on_status(row, "running", "")

            retry = int(effective_params.get("retry", 1))
            for attempt in range(retry + 1):
                try:
                    result = await self._do_action(acc, action, effective_params)
                    self.on_status(row, "success", "")
                    if self.db:
                        try:
                            self.db.set_status(acc["login"], "success", "")
                            self.db.log_task_run(acc["login"], action, "success")
                            # A/B tracking
                            self._log_experiment(acc, action, "success", None,
                                                 effective_params)
                        except Exception:
                            pass
                    if action == "collect_stats" and result:
                        if self.db:
                            try: self.db.update_stats(acc["login"], result)
                            except Exception: pass
                        if self.stats_sink:
                            self.stats_sink(acc["login"], result)
                    return result

                # ── 1. РЕАЛЬНАЯ смерть акка ──
                except SuspendedError as e:
                    self.on_status(row, "dead", f"suspended: {str(e)[:120]}")
                    if self.db:
                        self.db.set_status(acc["login"], "dead", f"suspended: {str(e)[:200]}")
                        self._log_experiment(acc, action, "dead", "suspended",
                                             effective_params)
                    return

                except LoginError as e:
                    # ТОЛЬКО реально плохой пароль/креденшелы
                    msg = str(e).lower()
                    if "bad_password" in msg or "incorrect" in msg:
                        self.on_status(row, "error", f"bad_creds: {str(e)[:100]}")
                        if self.db:
                            self.db.set_status(acc["login"], "error",
                                              f"bad_creds: {str(e)[:200]}")
                        return
                    # прочий LoginError — тоже не бан, ретраим
                    if attempt < retry:
                        await self._sleep(random.uniform(5, 15))
                        continue
                    self.on_status(row, "error", f"login: {str(e)[:120]}")
                    if self.db:
                        self.db.set_status(acc["login"], "error", str(e)[:200])
                    return

                # ── 2. Session/Bearer протух — НЕ БАН ──
                except SessionExpiredError as e:
                    if acc.get("is_ios_iam"):
                        # iOS IAM: Bearer сдох. Помечаем специальным статусом,
                        # не запускаем password-flow (иначе checkpoint 100%).
                        self.on_status(row, "session_expired",
                                      "iOS IAM Bearer expired — нужен ре-импорт из IAM")
                        if self.db:
                            self.db.set_status(acc["login"], "session_expired",
                                              "iOS IAM Bearer expired")
                        return
                    # Android: cookies протухли, следующий retry попробует password-flow.
                    # На этот раз для password нужен свежий session — сбрасываем cookies
                    acc["cookies"] = None
                    if attempt < retry:
                        await self._sleep(random.uniform(3, 8))
                        continue
                    self.on_status(row, "error", "session_expired retries exhausted")
                    if self.db:
                        self.db.set_status(acc["login"], "error", "session_expired")
                    return

                # ── 3. Rate limit / transient — retry с бэкоффом, БЕЗ пометки ──
                except RateLimitError as e:
                    if attempt < retry:
                        backoff = random.uniform(30, 90) * (2 ** attempt)
                        await self._sleep(min(backoff, 300))
                        continue
                    # даже после retry — не банить, помечаем soft-status
                    self.on_status(row, "rate_limited", f"429: {str(e)[:100]}")
                    if self.db:
                        # оставляем 'idle' в БД — акк живой, просто устал
                        self.db.set_status(acc["login"], "rate_limited", f"rate_limited: {str(e)[:200]}")
                    return

                except TransientError as e:
                    if attempt < retry:
                        await self._sleep(random.uniform(8, 20))
                        continue
                    self.on_status(row, "error", f"transient: {str(e)[:100]}")
                    if self.db:
                        # transient после всех retry — тоже НЕ dead. Просто error.
                        self.db.set_status(acc["login"], "error", f"transient: {str(e)[:200]}")
                    return

                # ── 4. 2FA — нужен totp secret ──
                except TwoFactorRequired:
                    self.on_status(row, "error", "2FA required, no totp_secret")
                    if self.db:
                        self.db.set_status(acc["login"], "error", "2FA no secret")
                    return

                # ── 5. Checkpoint — пробуем решить ──
                except CheckpointError as e:
                    self.on_status(row, "checkpoint", f"solving: {str(e)[:80]}")
                    # попытка решения — в _do_action уже интегрирована,
                    # если сюда пришло — значит solver сдался
                    if self.db:
                        self.db.set_status(acc["login"], "checkpoint",
                                          f"unsolved: {str(e)[:200]}")
                    if effective_params.get("rotate_proxy_on_ban") and self.db:
                        new_proxy = self.db.get_free_proxy()
                        if new_proxy and attempt < retry:
                            old_proxy = acc.get("proxy")
                            if old_proxy:
                                self.db.conn.execute(
                                    "UPDATE proxies SET bound_to=NULL WHERE proxy=?",
                                    (old_proxy,))
                            acc["proxy"] = new_proxy
                            self.db.upsert_account(acc)
                            self.db.bind_proxy(new_proxy, acc["login"])
                            await self._sleep(random.uniform(10, 25))
                            continue
                    return

                # ── 6. Другие ошибки: классифицируем по тексту ──
                except Exception as e:
                    msg = str(e).lower()

                    hard_signals = (
                        "feedback_required", "spam", "action_blocked",
                        "we restrict certain", "biography_length",
                        "url_missing", "cannot_be_uploaded",
                        "not_allowed",
                    )
                    is_hard = any(sig in msg for sig in hard_signals)

                    is_proxy = ("407" in msg or "connect tunnel" in msg
                                or "curl: (28)" in msg or "curl: (7)" in msg
                                or "curl: (56)" in msg or "curl: (55)" in msg
                                or "curl: (35)" in msg or "timed out" in msg
                                or "connection reset" in msg)

                    is_soft = ("transcode" in msg or "mismatch" in msg
                               or "server error" in msg or "500" in msg
                               or "502" in msg or "503" in msg or "504" in msg)

                    if is_hard:
                        self.on_status(row, "error", f"hard: {str(e)[:120]}")
                        if self.db:
                            self.db.set_status(acc["login"], "error",
                                              f"hard: {str(e)[:200]}")
                        return

                    # авто-ротация прокси при traffic error
                    if is_proxy and effective_params.get("rotate_proxy_on_traffic", True) \
                       and self.db:
                        old_proxy = acc.get("proxy")
                        new_proxy = self.db.get_free_proxy()
                        if new_proxy and new_proxy != old_proxy:
                            self.db.conn.execute(
                                "UPDATE proxies SET status='bad', bound_to=NULL WHERE proxy=?",
                                (old_proxy,))
                            acc["proxy"] = new_proxy
                            self.db.upsert_account(acc)
                            self.db.bind_proxy(new_proxy, acc["login"])
                            self.db.conn.commit()
                            print(f"[proxy-rotate:{acc['login']}] "
                                  f"{str(old_proxy)[:25]}... → {new_proxy[:25]}...")
                            await self._sleep(random.uniform(3, 8))
                            if attempt < retry: continue

                    if attempt < retry:
                        if is_proxy:  await self._sleep(random.uniform(15, 45))
                        elif is_soft: await self._sleep(random.uniform(10, 25))
                        else:         await self._sleep(random.uniform(3, 8))
                        continue

                    self.on_status(row, "error", str(e)[:200])
                    if self.db:
                        self.db.set_status(acc["login"], "error", str(e)[:200])
                    return

    # ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    def _log_experiment(self, acc, action, outcome, err_type, params):
        """Записывает исход в experiments таблицу — для A/B аналитики."""
        if not self.db or not hasattr(self.db, "log_experiment"):
            return
        try:
            self.db.log_experiment({
                "login":     acc["login"],
                "action":    action,
                "outcome":   outcome,      # success | dead | error | rate_limited
                "err_type":  err_type or "",
                "variant":   self.experiment_variant or "default",
                "platform":  acc.get("platform") or "android",
                "is_fresh":  int(self._is_fresh_account(acc)),
                "params_hash": hashlib.md5(json.dumps({
                    "u_level":  params.get("uniquify_level"),
                    "u_covers": params.get("uniquify_covers"),
                    "pre_story": params.get("pre_story"),
                    "share_story": params.get("share_reel_to_story"),
                    "highlight_story": params.get("highlight_story"),
                    "watch_reels": params.get("watch_reels"),
                    "watch_count": params.get("watch_count"),
                    "follow_users": params.get("follow_users"),
                    "follow_count": params.get("follow_count"),
                    "self_engage": params.get("self_engage"),
                    "self_like": params.get("self_like"),
                    "warmup":   params.get("warmup"),
                    "d_min":    params.get("delay_min"),
                    "d_max":    params.get("delay_max"),
                    "first_setup": params.get("first_setup_enable"),
                    "consume_covers": params.get("consume_covers"),
                }, sort_keys=True).encode()).hexdigest()[:16],
                "params_json": json.dumps({
                    "u_level":  params.get("uniquify_level"),
                    "u_covers": params.get("uniquify_covers"),
                    "warmup":   params.get("warmup"),
                    "pre_story": params.get("pre_story"),
                    "share_story": params.get("share_reel_to_story"),
                    "highlight_story": params.get("highlight_story"),
                    "watch_reels": params.get("watch_reels"),
                    "watch_count": params.get("watch_count"),
                    "self_engage": params.get("self_engage"),
                    "self_like": params.get("self_like"),
                    "follow_users": params.get("follow_users"),
                    "follow_count": params.get("follow_count"),
                    "d_min":    params.get("delay_min"),
                    "d_max":    params.get("delay_max"),
                    "first_setup": params.get("first_setup_enable"),
                    "consume_covers": params.get("consume_covers"),
                    "stats_mode": params.get("stats_mode"),
                }, ensure_ascii=False),
            })
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[exp] log fail: {e}")

    # ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    async def _do_action(self, acc, action, params):
        if not acc.get("proxy"):
            raise ValueError("no proxy assigned to account")

        country, tz_offset = await self.geo.resolve(acc["proxy"])

        has_full_device = (
            acc.get("device_id") and acc.get("model") and
            acc.get("ig_version") and acc.get("os_release"))
        device = None
        if has_full_device:
            try:
                device = DeviceProfile.from_saved(acc)
            except Exception:
                device = None

        if device is None:
            device = DeviceProfile.generate(
                seed=acc["login"],
                platform=acc.get("platform") or params.get("platform"),
                country_hint=country, tz_offset_hint=tz_offset)
            if self.db:
                saved = device.to_saved()
                acc.update({k: v for k, v in saved.items()
                           if k not in ("seed", "waterfall_id", "pigeon_session_id",
                                        "session_id", "mid")})
                self.db.upsert_account(acc)

        # для iOS IAM НЕ трогаем country/tz — они уже настоящие из IAM-дампа
        if not acc.get("is_ios_iam") and country and country != device.country:
            device.country = country
            from assets.locale_map import pick_timezone_for_locale
            device.timezone_offset = pick_timezone_for_locale(device.locale, tz_offset)

        cookies = acc.get("cookies")
        if not cookies and acc.get("cookies_json"):
            try:
                cookies = json.loads(acc["cookies_json"])
                acc["cookies"] = cookies
            except Exception:
                cookies = None

        sess = IGSession(device, acc["proxy"], cookies)
        cli = IGClient(sess, acc)
        cli.on_caption_failed = lambda mid, cap: self._queue_caption_fix(cli, acc, mid, cap)

        try:
            # Login (с обработкой чекпоинта)
            try:
                uid = await cli.login()
            except CheckpointError as ce:
                solver = CheckpointSolver(cli, acc, self.settings)
                payload = ce.args[0] if ce.args else {}
                if await solver.solve(payload):
                    # после решения — БОЛЬШАЯ пауза, IG не любит immediate retry
                    await self._sleep(random.uniform(20, 45))
                    uid = await cli.login()
                else:
                    raise

            # ── warmup только для password-flow и НЕ для iOS IAM ──
            if not acc.get("is_ios_iam") and not acc.get("cookies"):
                try:
                    warmup_level = params.get("warmup", "medium")
                    await cli.warm_postlogin(str(uid), intensity=warmup_level)
                except Exception:
                    pass
            # ═══ SAFE MODE: для Android откладываем bio/avatar ═══
            if action == "upload_reel" and params.get("safe_mode") \
               and not acc.get("is_ios_iam"):
                try:
                    cur = self.db.conn.cursor()
                    reels_done = cur.execute(
                        "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM reels_uploads WHERE login=?",
                        (acc["login"],)
                    ).fetchone()[0]
                except Exception:
                    reels_done = 0

                min_bio = int(params.get("safe_min_reels_for_bio", 2))
                min_avatar = int(params.get("safe_min_reels_for_avatar", 3))

                orig_bio = params.get("first_setup_enable", False)
                orig_avatar = params.get("first_setup_avatar", False)
                should_bio = orig_bio and reels_done >= min_bio
                should_avatar = orig_avatar and reels_done >= min_avatar

                if orig_bio and not should_bio:
                    print(f"[safe_mode:{acc['login']}] bio ОТЛОЖЕН — "
                          f"залито {reels_done}, порог {min_bio}")
                if orig_avatar and not should_avatar:
                    print(f"[safe_mode:{acc['login']}] avatar ОТЛОЖЕН — "
                          f"залито {reels_done}, порог {min_avatar}")
                if (orig_bio and should_bio) or (orig_avatar and should_avatar):
                    print(f"[safe_mode:{acc['login']}] порог пройден "
                          f"({reels_done} рилсов), ставим bio={should_bio} "
                          f"avatar={should_avatar}")

                # переопределяем в локальной копии params
                params = {**params,
                          "first_setup_enable": should_bio,
                          "first_setup_avatar": should_avatar}
            # ── целевое действие ──
            # ── целевое действие ──
            if action == "upload_reel":
                ensure_pub = params.get("ensure_public", True)
                if params.get("ensure_public_override") is False:
                    ensure_pub = False   # fresh акк — пропускаем на первом заливе

                if ensure_pub and not acc.get("is_public_verified"):
                    try:
                        status = await cli.ensure_public(str(uid))
                        if status == "made_public":
                            print(f"[privacy:{acc['login']}] был приватным — открыт")
                            await self._sleep(random.uniform(3, 7))
                        if status in ("was_public", "made_public") and self.db:
                            self.db.mark_public_verified(acc["login"])
                            acc["is_public_verified"] = 1
                    except Exception as e:
                        print(f"[privacy:{acc['login']}] fail: {e}")

                need_setup = (params.get("first_setup_enable")
                              or params.get("first_setup_avatar"))
                if need_setup and not acc.get("first_setup_done"):
                    await self._do_first_setup(cli, acc, params)

                # ── pre_story: сторис ПЕРЕД рилсом (S3/S4/S9) ──
                if params.get("pre_story"):
                    try:
                        story_folder = params.get("cover_folder") or params.get("avatar_folder")
                        if story_folder and Path(story_folder).is_dir():
                            img = await AssetPicker.pick(story_folder)
                            if img:
                                try:
                                    if params.get("uniquify_covers", True):
                                        tmp = await ImageUniquifier.unique_async(img, is_avatar=False)
                                        sid = await cli.upload_story_photo(tmp)
                                        try: Path(tmp).unlink(missing_ok=True)
                                        except Exception: pass
                                    else:
                                        sid = await cli.upload_story_photo(img)
                                    if sid:
                                        acc["_last_story_media_id"] = str(sid)
                                        print(f"[story:{acc['login']}] SUCCESS: сторис опубликована "
                                              f"(media_id={sid}), пауза перед рилсом")
                                        await self._sleep(random.uniform(60, 150))
                                    else:
                                        print(f"[story:{acc['login']}] FAIL: сторис не вернула media_id")
                                finally:
                                    AssetPicker.release(img)
                        else:
                            print(f"[story:{acc['login']}] FAIL: папка для сторис не найдена "
                                  f"({story_folder})")
                    except Exception as e:
                        print(f"[story:{acc['login']}] FAIL: {e}")

                # ── сам рилс ──
                result = await self._upload(cli, params)
                # ── пост-действия: шер в стори / актуальное / watch / follow ──
                await self._post_reel_engagement(cli, acc, params, result)
                return result

            if action == "set_avatar":
                if params.get("ensure_public", False) and not acc.get("is_public_verified"):
                    try:
                        status = await cli.ensure_public(str(uid))
                        if status in ("was_public", "made_public") and self.db:
                            self.db.mark_public_verified(acc["login"])
                            acc["is_public_verified"] = 1
                    except Exception:
                        pass
                return await self._do_avatar_only(cli, acc, params)

            if action == "collect_stats":
                stats_mode = params.get("stats_mode", "light")

                # ═══ базовая инфа профиля (всегда) ═══
                result = await cli.get_self_stats(str(uid), mode="light")

                # ═══ self_reels: собираем views последних N рилсов ═══
                if stats_mode == "self_reels":
                    reels_limit = int(params.get("reels_limit", 5))
                    login = acc["login"]

                    try:
                        cur = self.db.conn.cursor()
                        rows = cur.execute(
                            "SELECT media_id FROM reels_uploads "
                            "WHERE login=? AND media_id IS NOT NULL AND media_id != '' "
                            "ORDER BY uploaded_at DESC LIMIT ?",
                            (login, reels_limit)
                        ).fetchall()
                    except Exception as e:
                        print(f"[stats:{login}] db error: {e}")
                        rows = []

                    if not rows:
                        # у акка нет залитых рилсов в БД — только базовая инфа
                        print(f"[stats:{login}] нет media_id в reels_uploads — только profile info")
                        return result

                    total_views = total_likes = total_comments = total_reposts = 0
                    reels_ok = 0

                    for row in rows:
                        media_id = row[0] if not hasattr(row, "keys") else row["media_id"]
                        try:
                            info = await cli.get_media_info(str(media_id))
                            if not info.get("deleted"):
                                total_views    += int(info.get("play_count", 0))
                                total_likes    += int(info.get("like_count", 0))
                                total_comments += int(info.get("comment_count", 0))
                                total_reposts  += int(info.get("reshare_count", 0))
                                reels_ok += 1
                            # пауза между media-запросами чтобы не палить
                            await self._sleep(random.uniform(1.5, 3.5))
                        except Exception as e:
                            print(f"[stats:{login}] media {media_id[:12]}...: {type(e).__name__}")

                    result["total_views"]    = total_views
                    result["total_likes"]    = total_likes
                    result["total_comments"] = total_comments
                    result["total_reposts"]  = total_reposts
                    result["reels_count"]    = reels_ok
                    result["stats_mode"]     = "self_reels"

                    print(f"[stats:{login}] собрано {reels_ok}/{len(rows)} рилсов, "
                          f"views={total_views}, likes={total_likes}")

                return result
            if action == "noop_warm":
                if not acc.get("is_ios_iam"):
                    try:
                        await cli.warm_postlogin(str(uid), intensity=params.get("warmup", "min"))
                    except Exception:
                        pass
                stats = await cli.get_self_stats(str(uid), mode="light")
                return {"alive": True, "followers": stats.get("followers", 0),
                        "username": stats.get("username")}
            if action == "health_check":
                # Новая задача: только login + info/. Не пишет ничего кроме статуса.
                # Используется для рефреша статуса всех "banned" акков — узнать
                # кто реально мертвый а кто просто rate_limited был.
                stats = await cli.get_self_stats(str(uid), mode="light")
                return {"alive": True, "followers": stats.get("followers", 0),
                        "media_count": stats.get("media_count", 0),
                        "username": stats.get("username")}

            raise ValueError(f"unknown action: {action}")

        except Exception as e:
            # судебный лог: пишем причину смерти в БД
            detail = f"{type(e).__name__}: {e}"[:500]
            resp = getattr(e, "response", None)
            if resp is not None:
                try:
                    detail = (f"HTTP {resp.status_code}: "
                              f"{str(resp.text)[:400]} | {detail}")[:500]
                except Exception:
                    pass
            if self.db:
                try:
                    with self.db._cursor() as c:
                        c.execute("UPDATE accounts SET last_error_detail=? "
                                  "WHERE login=?",
                                  (detail, acc["login"]))
                except Exception:
                    pass
            raise  # пробрасываем дальше — оркестратор сам поставит статус

        finally:
            try:
                if self.db:
                    fresh_cookies = {c.name: c.value for c in sess._session.cookies.jar}
                    if sess._auth_header:
                        fresh_cookies["Authorization"] = sess._auth_header
                    if sess.mid:
                        fresh_cookies["X-MID"] = sess.mid
                    if fresh_cookies.get("sessionid") or fresh_cookies.get("Authorization"):
                        self.db.save_cookies(acc["login"], fresh_cookies)
            except Exception:
                pass
            try:
                await sess.close()
            except Exception:
                pass

    # ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    # first_setup / avatar_only / upload / bio-splitter — БЕЗ ИЗМЕНЕНИЙ
    # (скопируй методы _do_first_setup, _do_avatar_only, _upload,
    #  _spin, _split_variants, _pick_bio из твоего оригинала — они рабочие)
    # ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

    async def _do_first_setup(self, cli, acc, params):
        login = acc.get("login", "?")
        do_bio = params.get("first_setup_enable", False)
        do_avatar = params.get("first_setup_avatar", False)
        consume_used = params.get("consume_used_assets", True)

        is_fresh = self._is_fresh_account(acc)
        if is_fresh:
            avatar_delay = (10, 25)      # ещё осторожнее
            bio_delay = (12, 30)
        else:
            avatar_delay = (4, 10)
            bio_delay = (5, 12)

        # ─── Аватар ───
        if do_avatar:
            avatar_folder = params.get("avatar_folder", "")
            if avatar_folder and Path(avatar_folder).is_dir():
                reserved_path = None
                picked_path = None
                if consume_used:
                    reserved_path = await AssetPicker.pick(avatar_folder)
                    picked_path = reserved_path
                else:
                    avatars = []
                    for ext in ("*.jpg", "*.jpeg", "*.png", "*.webp",
                                "*.JPG", "*.JPEG", "*.PNG", "*.WEBP"):
                        avatars.extend(Path(avatar_folder).glob(ext))
                    avatars = [a for a in avatars if not a.name.endswith(".taken")]
                    if avatars:
                        picked_path = str(random.choice(avatars))

                if picked_path:
                    try:
                        with open(picked_path, "rb") as f:
                            new_avatar_hash = hashlib.md5(f.read()).hexdigest()
                        if acc.get("avatar_hash") == new_avatar_hash:
                            if reserved_path:
                                AssetPicker.release(reserved_path)
                        else:
                            upload_path = picked_path
                            temp_uniq = None
                            if params.get("uniquify_avatars", True):
                                temp_uniq = ImageUniquifier.unique(
                                    picked_path, is_avatar=True)
                                upload_path = temp_uniq
                            try:
                                await cli.change_profile_picture(upload_path)
                            finally:
                                if temp_uniq:
                                    try: Path(temp_uniq).unlink(missing_ok=True)
                                    except Exception: pass
                            if self.db:
                                self.db.conn.execute(
                                    "UPDATE accounts SET avatar_hash=?, "
                                    "avatar_updated_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP WHERE login=?",
                                    (new_avatar_hash, login))
                                self.db.conn.commit()
                            if reserved_path and consume_used:
                                AssetPicker.consume(reserved_path)
                            elif reserved_path:
                                AssetPicker.release(reserved_path)
                            await self._sleep(random.uniform(*avatar_delay))
                    except Exception as e:
                        if reserved_path: AssetPicker.release(reserved_path)
                        print(f"[first_setup:{login}] avatar fail: {e}")

        # ─── Био ───
        if do_bio:
            try:
                bio_path = Path("data/bio_settings.json")
                if bio_path.exists():
                    bio_cfg = json.loads(bio_path.read_text("utf-8"))
                    bio_text = bio_cfg.get("bio", "").strip()
                    if bio_text:
                        if params.get("skip_if_ever_set_bio", True) and acc.get("bio_hash"):
                            print(f"[first_setup:{login}] bio уже ставили — пропуск")
                        else:
                            variants = self._split_variants(bio_text)
                            if len(variants) > 1:
                                idx = int(hashlib.md5(login.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()[:8], 16) % len(variants)
                                chosen_bio = variants[idx]
                            elif variants:
                                chosen_bio = variants[0]
                            else:
                                chosen_bio = bio_text
                            bio_final = self._spin(chosen_bio)
                            if len(bio_final) > 150:
                                bio_final = bio_final[:150]
                            new_bio_hash = hashlib.md5(bio_final.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
                            if acc.get("bio_hash") != new_bio_hash:
                                result = await cli.set_biography(bio_final)
                                if result.get("status") == "ok" and self.db:
                                    self.db.set_bio_hash(login, new_bio_hash)
                                await self._sleep(random.uniform(*bio_delay))
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"[first_setup:{login}] bio fail: {e}")

        if (do_bio or do_avatar) and self.db:
            try:
                self.db.mark_setup_done(login)
                acc["first_setup_done"] = 1
            except Exception: pass

    async def _do_avatar_only(self, cli, acc, params):
        login = acc.get("login", "?")
        avatar_folder = params.get("avatar_folder", "")
        if not avatar_folder or not Path(avatar_folder).is_dir():
            raise ValueError("avatar_folder not set or does not exist")
        consume_used = params.get("consume_used_assets", True)

        if params.get("skip_if_set"):
            uid_str = ""
            try:
                for c in cli.s._session.cookies.jar:
                    if c.name == "ds_user_id":
                        uid_str = c.value; break
                if uid_str:
                    r = await cli.s.request(
                        "GET", f"{cli.BASE}/api/v1/users/{uid_str}/info/")
                    j = r.json()
                    if j.get("status") == "ok" and j.get("user"):
                        has_avatar = not j["user"].get("has_anonymous_profile_picture", True)
                        if has_avatar:
                            return {"status": "skipped", "reason": "already set"}
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"[avatar:{login}] info fetch fail: {e}")

        reserved_path = None
        picked_path = None
        if consume_used:
            reserved_path = await AssetPicker.pick(avatar_folder)
            if reserved_path is None:
                raise ValueError(f"no images in {avatar_folder} (all consumed)")
            picked_path = reserved_path
        else:
            avatars = []
            for ext in ("*.jpg", "*.jpeg", "*.png", "*.webp",
