                        "*.JPG", "*.JPEG", "*.PNG", "*.WEBP"):
                avatars.extend(Path(avatar_folder).glob(ext))
            avatars = [a for a in avatars if not a.name.endswith(".taken")]
            if not avatars: raise ValueError(f"no images in {avatar_folder}")
            picked_path = str(random.choice(avatars))

        try:
            with open(picked_path, "rb") as f:
                new_hash = hashlib.md5(f.read()).hexdigest()
            await self._sleep(random.uniform(
                params.get("delay_min", 15), params.get("delay_max", 60)))
            upload_path = picked_path
            temp_uniq = None
            if params.get("uniquify_avatars", True):
                temp_uniq = ImageUniquifier.unique(picked_path, is_avatar=True)
                upload_path = temp_uniq
            try:
                result = await cli.change_profile_picture(upload_path)
            finally:
                if temp_uniq:
                    try: Path(temp_uniq).unlink(missing_ok=True)
                    except Exception: pass
            if self.db:
                try:
                    self.db.conn.execute(
                        "UPDATE accounts SET avatar_hash=?, avatar_updated_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP "
                        "WHERE login=?", (new_hash, login))
                    if params.get("mark_setup"):
                        self.db.conn.execute(
                            "UPDATE accounts SET first_setup_done=1 WHERE login=?",
                            (login,))
                    self.db.conn.commit()
                except Exception: pass
            if reserved_path and consume_used:
                AssetPicker.consume(reserved_path)
            elif reserved_path:
                AssetPicker.release(reserved_path)
            return result
        except Exception:
            if reserved_path: AssetPicker.release(reserved_path)
            raise

    async def _upload(self, cli, params: dict):
        """
        Загружает рилс(ы). В burst mode — цикл N раз с кулдауном.
        """
        burst_enable = params.get("burst_enable", False)
        burst_count = int(params.get("burst_count", 1)) if burst_enable else 1
        stop_on_fail = params.get("burst_stop_on_fail", True)

        results = []
        success_count = 0
        fail_count = 0
        login = cli.acc.get("login", "?")

        for shot in range(burst_count):
            if burst_enable and shot > 0:
                # кулдаун между заливами на одном акке
                cool = random.uniform(
                    params.get("burst_cooldown_min", 180),
                    params.get("burst_cooldown_max", 420))
                print(f"[burst:{login}] shot {shot+1}/{burst_count}, "
                      f"cooldown {int(cool)}s")
                await self._sleep(cool)

            try:
                result = await self._single_upload(cli, params, shot=shot,
                                                   total_shots=burst_count)
                results.append(result)
                success_count += 1
                if burst_enable:
                    print(f"[burst:{login}] shot {shot+1}/{burst_count} → ✓ "
                          f"code={result.get('media', {}).get('code', '?')}")
            except Exception as e:
                fail_count += 1
                err_msg = str(e)[:120]
                print(f"[burst:{login}] shot {shot+1}/{burst_count} → ✗ {err_msg}")

                # если stop_on_fail — прерываем весь burst
                if burst_enable and stop_on_fail:
                    print(f"[burst:{login}] STOP burst (stop_on_fail=True)")
                    # если ни одного успешного — эскалируем ошибку выше
                    if success_count == 0:
                        raise
                    # иначе — не рейзим, возвращаем что успело
                    break
                elif not burst_enable:
                    # обычный режим — просто рейзим
                    raise

        # ─── возвращаем результат ───
        if burst_enable:
            print(f"[burst:{login}] ИТОГО: {success_count}/{burst_count} "
                  f"успешно, {fail_count} провалов")
            # для burst возвращаем последний успешный (для совместимости с
            # log_upload). Метаданные всех успешных уже записаны в БД.
            if results:
                # добавляем инфу что был burst
                results[-1]["_burst_success"] = success_count
                results[-1]["_burst_total"] = burst_count
                return results[-1]
            raise UploadError(f"burst full-fail: 0/{burst_count} успешно")
        else:
            return results[0] if results else None

    async def _single_upload(self, cli, params: dict, shot: int = 0,
                              total_shots: int = 1):
        """
        Один залив. Вынесено из _upload чтобы вызывать в цикле burst.
        Каждый вызов: свежий выбор видео + cover + уникализация.
        """
        burst_enable = params.get("burst_enable", False)
        burst_video_mode = params.get("burst_video_mode", 0)  # 0=разные, 1=то же
        burst_use_cover = params.get("burst_use_cover", True)

        # ─── 1. Видео ───
        src = params.get("video_path")
        # в burst-режиме shot > 0 с burst_video_mode=0 — берём каждый раз новое из папки
        # (даже если video_path задан, для burst используем folder)
        force_folder = burst_enable and burst_video_mode == 0 and params.get("video_folder")

        if force_folder or (not src and params.get("video_folder")):
            folder = Path(params["video_folder"])
            files = (list(folder.glob("*.mp4")) + list(folder.glob("*.mov")) +
                     list(folder.glob("*.m4v")) + list(folder.glob("*.mkv")))
            if not files:
                raise ValueError("empty video folder")
            src = str(random.choice(files))

        if not src:
            raise ValueError("no video source")
        if not Path(src).exists():
            raise ValueError(f"video not found: {src}")

        # ─── 2. Обложка ───
        custom_cover = None
        cover_reserved = None
        consume_cover = params.get("consume_covers", True)
        temp_cover_uniq = None

        # burst_use_cover: только когда включён burst
        if burst_enable and not burst_use_cover:
            cover_folder = ""
            cover_path = ""
        else:
            cover_folder = params.get("cover_folder", "")
            cover_path = params.get("cover_path", "")

        if cover_path and Path(cover_path).exists():
            if params.get("uniquify_covers", True):
                temp_cover_uniq = await ImageUniquifier.unique_async(cover_path, is_avatar=False)
                custom_cover = temp_cover_uniq
            else:
                custom_cover = cover_path
        elif cover_folder and Path(cover_folder).is_dir():
            if consume_cover:
                cover_reserved = await AssetPicker.pick(cover_folder)
                if cover_reserved:
                    if params.get("uniquify_covers", True):
                        temp_cover_uniq = await ImageUniquifier.unique_async(cover_reserved, is_avatar=False)
                        custom_cover = temp_cover_uniq
                    else:
                        custom_cover = cover_reserved
            else:
                covers = []
                for ext in ("*.jpg", "*.jpeg", "*.png", "*.webp",
                            "*.JPG", "*.JPEG", "*.PNG", "*.WEBP"):
                    covers.extend(Path(cover_folder).glob(ext))
                covers = [c for c in covers if not c.name.endswith(".taken")]
                if covers:
                    chosen = str(random.choice(covers))
                    if params.get("uniquify_covers", True):
                        temp_cover_uniq = await ImageUniquifier.unique_async(chosen, is_avatar=False)
                        custom_cover = temp_cover_uniq
                    else:
                        custom_cover = chosen

        # ─── 3. Уникализация видео (КАЖДЫЙ shot — свежий passthrough) ───
        if params.get("uniquify"):
            level = params.get("uniquify_level", "universal")
            audio = params.get("uniquify_audio", True)
            orig_size = Path(src).stat().st_size
            src = await ReelUniquifier.unique_async(src, level=level, audio=audio)
            new_size = Path(src).stat().st_size
            saving_pct = int((1 - new_size / orig_size) * 100) if orig_size > 0 else 0
            shot_prefix = f"[burst {shot+1}/{total_shots}]" if total_shots > 1 else ""
            print(f"[uniquify:{cli.acc.get('login')}] {shot_prefix} "
                  f"{orig_size // 1024}KB → {new_size // 1024}KB "
                  f"(экономия {saving_pct}%)")

        # ─── 4. Caption ───
        raw_caption = params.get("caption", "")
        variants = self._split_variants(raw_caption)
        picked = random.choice(variants) if variants else raw_caption
        caption = self._spin(picked)
        caption_hash = hashlib.md5(caption.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest() if caption else None
        caption_preview = (caption[:200] + "...") if len(caption) > 200 else caption

        # ─── 5. Human delay ───
        # в burst mode задержка между акками уже отработала — здесь только для первого shot
        if shot == 0:
            await self._sleep(random.uniform(
                params.get("delay_min", 25), params.get("delay_max", 90)))

        # ─── 6. Публикация ───
        try:
            result = await cli.upload_reel(
                src, caption=caption,
                share_to_feed=params.get("share_to_feed", False),
                disable_comments=params.get("disable_comments", False),
                custom_cover_path=custom_cover)
            if temp_cover_uniq:
                try: Path(temp_cover_uniq).unlink(missing_ok=True)
                except Exception: pass
            cli.acc["_last_reel_video"] = src
        except Exception:
            if cover_reserved: AssetPicker.release(cover_reserved)
            if temp_cover_uniq:
                try: Path(temp_cover_uniq).unlink(missing_ok=True)
                except Exception: pass
            raise

        if cover_reserved:
            if consume_cover:
                AssetPicker.consume(cover_reserved)
            else:
                AssetPicker.release(cover_reserved)

        if self.db and result.get("media"):
            cover_used = ""
            if cover_reserved:
                cover_used = Path(cover_reserved).name
                if cover_used.endswith(".taken"):
                    cover_used = cover_used[:-6]
            elif custom_cover:
                cover_used = Path(custom_cover).name
            self.db.log_upload(
                cli.acc["login"],
                result["media"].get("id", ""),
                result["media"].get("code", ""),
                cover_filename=cover_used,
                caption_hash=caption_hash,
                caption_preview=caption_preview)
        return result
    def _queue_caption_fix(self, cli, acc, media_id: str, caption: str):
        if not media_id or not caption:
            return
        if not self._caption_worker_started:
            self._caption_worker_started = True
            try:
                asyncio.get_event_loop().create_task(self._caption_retry_worker())
            except Exception:
                self._caption_worker_started = False
        self._caption_queue.put_nowait((cli, acc.get("login"), str(media_id), caption))

    async def _caption_retry_worker(self):
        while True:
            cli, login, media_id, caption = await self._caption_queue.get()
            try:
                for rnd in range(8):
                    await asyncio.sleep(random.uniform(600, 900))  # 10-15 мин между заходами
                    try:
                        ok = await cli._edit_media_caption(media_id, caption, wait_before=0)
                    except Exception:
                        ok = False
                    if ok:
                        print(f"[caption-bg:{login}] ✓ caption дотянут с фона "
                              f"(заход #{rnd+1})")
                        break
                else:
                    print(f"[caption-bg:{login}] caption не применился за 8 заходов, "
                          f"рилс жив но без описания")
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"[caption-bg:{login}] worker error: {e}")
            finally:
                self._caption_queue.task_done()
    async def _post_reel_engagement(self, cli, acc, params, result):
        login = acc.get("login")
        is_ios = False
        media_id = None
        m_obj = {}
        if isinstance(result, dict):
            m_obj = result.get("media") or {}
            media_id = m_obj.get("id") or m_obj.get("pk")
        done, fail = [], []

        # ── шер рилса в стори (link-стикер на рилс) ──
        if params.get("share_reel_to_story"):
            if not media_id:
                fail.append("story_share(нет media_id)")
            else:
                tmp_path = None
                story_folder = None
                try:
                    # 1) превью: из папки обложек...
                    story_folder = params.get("cover_folder") or params.get("avatar_folder")
                    if story_folder and Path(story_folder).is_dir():
                        img = await AssetPicker.pick(story_folder)
                        if img:
                            try:
                                if params.get("uniquify_covers", True):
                                    tmp_path = await ImageUniquifier.unique_async(img, is_avatar=False)
                                else:
                                    tmp_path = img
                            finally:
                                AssetPicker.release(img)
                    # 2) ...фолбэк: кадр из самого залитого видео
                    if not tmp_path and acc.get("_last_reel_video"):
                        try:
                            tmp_path = await cli._extract_frame(acc["_last_reel_video"])
                        except Exception:
                            tmp_path = None
                    if not tmp_path:
                        fail.append("story_share(нет картинки-превью)")
                    else:
                        # пауза 45-150с (человеческий темп, не палево)
                        delay_lo = float(params.get("share_delay_min", 45))
                        delay_hi = float(params.get("share_delay_max", 150))
                        await asyncio.sleep(random.uniform(delay_lo, delay_hi))
                        # получаем code рилса для ссылки-стикера
                        reel_url = ""
                        code = m_obj.get("code")
                        if not code:
                            try:
                                info = await cli.get_media_info(str(media_id))
                                code = info.get("code")
                            except Exception:
                                code = None
                        if code:
                            reel_url = f"https://www.instagram.com/reel/{code}/"
                        sid = await cli.share_reel_to_story(str(media_id), tmp_path,
                                                            reel_url)
                        done.append("story_share")
                        if sid and not acc.get("_last_story_media_id"):
                            acc["_last_story_media_id"] = str(sid)
                except Exception as e:
                    fail.append(f"story_share({e})")
                finally:
                    # чистим временный файл (но не файлы из папки обложек)
                    if tmp_path and story_folder and not tmp_path.startswith(str(story_folder)):
                        try: Path(tmp_path).unlink(missing_ok=True)
                        except Exception: pass

        # ── актуальное из опубликованной стори ──
        if params.get("highlight_story"):
            if not acc.get("_last_story_media_id"):
                fail.append("highlight(нет сторис media_id)")
            else:
                try:
                    await asyncio.sleep(random.uniform(45, 90))
                    try:
                        await cli.save_story_to_highlight(
                            str(acc["_last_story_media_id"]),
                            params.get("highlight_title", "Reels"))
                        done.append("highlight")
                    except Exception:
                        # фоновый ретрай: 3 захода через 10-15 мин
                        for rnd in range(3):
                            await asyncio.sleep(random.uniform(600, 900))
                            try:
                                await cli.save_story_to_highlight(
                                    str(acc["_last_story_media_id"]),
                                    params.get("highlight_title", "Reels"))
                                print(f"[highlight:{login}] ✓ актуальное создано "
                                      f"с фона (заход #{rnd+1})")
                                done.append(f"highlight(bg#{rnd+1})")
                                break
                            except Exception:
                                continue
                        else:
                            fail.append("highlight(3 фоновых захода исчерпаны)")
                except Exception as e:
                    fail.append(f"highlight({e})")

        # ── просмотр рилсов по теме ──
        if params.get("watch_reels"):
            try:
                await asyncio.sleep(random.uniform(10, 30))
                try:
                    n = await cli.watch_reels(int(params.get("watch_count", 3)),
                                              params.get("watch_query", ""))
                    done.append(f"watch({n})")
                except Exception as e:
                    # ретрай один раз (SSL-сбои прокси и т.п.)
                    await asyncio.sleep(random.uniform(30, 60))
                    n = await cli.watch_reels(int(params.get("watch_count", 3)),
                                              params.get("watch_query", ""))
                    done.append(f"watch({n},retry)")
            except Exception as e2:
                fail.append(f"watch({e2})")

        # ── подписки по теме ──
        if params.get("follow_users"):
            try:
                await asyncio.sleep(random.uniform(15, 40))
                n = await cli.follow_by_query(params.get("follow_query", "polymarket"),
                                              int(params.get("follow_count", 3)))
                done.append(f"follow({n})")
            except Exception as e:
                fail.append(f"follow({e})")
        # ── само-вовлечённость: смотрим и лайкаем свой рилс позже, в фоне ──
        if params.get("self_engage") and media_id:
            delay_lo = float(params.get("self_engage_delay_min", 900))
            delay_hi = float(params.get("self_engage_delay_max", 1800))
            do_like = bool(params.get("self_like", True))

            async def _bg_self_engage():
                try:
                    await asyncio.sleep(random.uniform(delay_lo, delay_hi))
                    await cli.self_engage(str(media_id), like=do_like)
                    print(f"[self-engage:{login}] SUCCESS: посмотрел+лайкнул свой рилс")
                except Exception as e:
                    print(f"[self-engage:{login}] FAIL: {e}")

            asyncio.get_event_loop().create_task(_bg_self_engage())
        if done:
            print(f"[post:{login}] SUCCESS: {', '.join(done)}")
        if fail:
            print(f"[post:{login}] FAIL/SKIP: {', '.join(fail)}")
        if not done and not fail:
            print(f"[post:{login}] пост-действий нет (простой залив)")
    # ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    @staticmethod
    def _spin(text: str) -> str:
        import re
        def repl(m):
            return random.choice(m.group(1).split("|"))
        prev = None
        while prev != text:
            prev = text
            text = re.sub(r"\{([^{}]+)\}", repl, text)
        return text

    @staticmethod
    def _split_variants(text: str) -> list[str]:
        import re
        if not text: return []
        blocks = []
        current = []
        for line in text.split("\n"):
            if re.match(r"^\s*-+\s*$", line):
                if current:
                    blocks.append("\n".join(current).strip())
                    current = []
            else:
                current.append(line)
        if current:
            blocks.append("\n".join(current).strip())
        return [b for b in blocks if b]

    def _pick_bio(self, params: dict, login: str) -> str:
        bio = params.get("bio", "")
        variants = self._split_variants(bio)
        def _stable_index(text: str, n: int) -> int:
            h = int(hashlib.md5(text.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()[:8], 16)
            return h % n
        if len(variants) > 1:
            chosen = variants[_stable_index(login, len(variants))]
        elif variants:
            chosen = variants[0]
        else:
            chosen = bio
        if params.get("multiline") and len(variants) <= 1:
            lines = [l for l in chosen.splitlines() if l.strip()]
            if lines:
                chosen = lines[_stable_index(login, len(lines))]
        return self._spin(chosen)
core/session.py
"""IGSession v2 — правильная эмуляция iOS + Android с разделением хедеров."""
import re
import time
import asyncio
import random
from curl_cffi.requests import AsyncSession

from assets.ig_versions import IG_CAPABILITIES


class IGSession:
    IMPERSONATE = {
        "android": "chrome131_android",
        "ios":     "safari184_ios",
    }

    _ANDROID_ORDER = [
        "X-IG-App-Locale", "X-IG-Device-Locale", "X-IG-Mapped-Locale",
        "X-Pigeon-Session-Id", "X-Pigeon-Rawclienttime",
        "X-IG-Connection-Speed", "X-IG-Bandwidth-Speed-KBPS",
        "X-IG-Bandwidth-TotalBytes-B", "X-IG-Bandwidth-TotalTime-MS",
        "X-IG-App-Startup-Country", "X-Bloks-Version-Id",
        "X-IG-WWW-Claim", "Authorization",
        "X-Bloks-Is-Layout-RTL", "X-Bloks-Is-Panorama-Enabled",
        "X-IG-Device-ID", "X-IG-Family-Device-ID", "X-IG-Android-ID",
        "X-Ig-Nav-Chain",
        "X-IG-Timezone-Offset", "X-IG-Connection-Type",
        "X-IG-Capabilities", "X-IG-App-ID", "Priority",
        "User-Agent", "Accept-Language", "X-MID",
        "Accept-Encoding", "Accept",
        "X-FB-HTTP-Engine", "X-FB-Client-IP", "X-FB-Server-Cluster",
    ]

    # ═══ iOS: порядок под настоящий IG iOS app v446 ═══
    _IOS_ORDER = [
        "X-IG-App-Locale", "X-IG-Device-Locale", "X-IG-Mapped-Locale",
        "X-Pigeon-Session-Id", "X-Pigeon-Rawclienttime",
        "X-IG-Connection-Speed", "X-IG-Bandwidth-Speed-KBPS",
        "X-IG-Bandwidth-TotalBytes-B", "X-IG-Bandwidth-TotalTime-MS",
        "X-IG-EU-DC-ENABLED",
        "X-IG-App-Startup-Country", "X-Bloks-Version-Id",
        "X-IG-WWW-Claim", "X-Bloks-Is-Layout-RTL",
        "X-Bloks-Prism-Button-Version",
        "X-Bloks-Prism-Colors-Enabled",
        "X-Bloks-Prism-Ax-Base-Colors-Enabled",
        "X-Bloks-Prism-Font-Enabled",
        "Authorization",
        "X-Ig-Nav-Chain",
        "X-IG-Device-ID", "X-IG-Family-Device-ID",
        "X-IG-Timezone-Offset", "X-IG-Connection-Type",
        "X-FB-Connection-Type", "X-FB-Connection-Type-Detailed",
        "X-FB-Connection-Quality", "X-FB-Connection-Bandwidth",
        "X-IG-Capabilities", "X-IG-App-ID", "Priority",
        "User-Agent", "Accept-Language", "X-MID",
        "Accept-Encoding", "Accept",
    ]

    _HIGH_PRIORITY_PATHS = (
        "/api/v1/accounts/login/",
        "/api/v1/accounts/two_factor_login/",
        "/api/v1/accounts/current_user/",
        "/api/v1/media/configure_to_clips/",
        "/api/v1/media/configure/",
        "/rupload_igvideo/", "/rupload_igphoto/",
    )

    _HOST_RE = re.compile(r"^[a-zA-Z0-9][a-zA-Z0-9\-.]*[a-zA-Z0-9]$")
    _IP_RE   = re.compile(r"^\d{1,3}(\.\d{1,3}){3}$")   # ← ФИКС отсутствовал в оригинале

    def __init__(self, device, proxy: str, cookies: dict | None = None):
        self.device = device
        self.proxy = self._normalize_proxy(proxy)
        self._session = AsyncSession(
            impersonate=self.IMPERSONATE[device.platform],
            proxies={"http": self.proxy, "https": self.proxy},
            timeout=(10, 45), verify=True)

        self._conn_type = random.choices(
            ["WIFI", "MOBILE(LTE)", "MOBILE(5G)"], weights=[72, 20, 8])[0]
        self._bw_total_bytes = 0
        self._bw_total_ms = 0
        self.www_claim: str = "0"
        self.mid: str = ""
        self.rur: str = ""
        self._auth_header: str | None = None
        self._ig_u_headers: dict[str, str] = {}

        if cookies:
            for k, v in cookies.items():
                if not v: continue
                kl = k.lower()
                if kl == "authorization":
                    self._auth_header = v if v.startswith("Bearer ") else f"Bearer {v}"
                elif kl in ("x-ig-www-claim", "www-claim"):
                    self.www_claim = v
                elif kl == "x-mid":
                    self.mid = v
                elif kl == "mid":
                    self.mid = v
                    self._session.cookies.set("mid", v, domain=".instagram.com")
                elif kl.startswith("ig-u-"):
                    self._ig_u_headers[k] = v
                    self._session.cookies.set(k, v, domain=".instagram.com")
                else:
                    self._session.cookies.set(k, v, domain=".instagram.com")

    @classmethod
    def _normalize_proxy(cls, p) -> str:
        if not p or not isinstance(p, str):
            raise ValueError("no proxy assigned to account")
        p = p.strip()
        scheme = "http"
        for s in ("http://", "https://", "socks5://", "socks4://"):
            if p.startswith(s):
                scheme = s.rstrip("://")
                p = p[len(s):]
                break
        if "@" in p:
            return f"{scheme}://{p}"
        parts = p.split(":")
        if len(parts) == 2:
            return f"{scheme}://{p}"
        if len(parts) == 4:
            if cls._IP_RE.match(parts[0]) and parts[1].isdigit():
                ip, port, user, pw = parts
            elif cls._IP_RE.match(parts[2]) and parts[3].isdigit():
                user, pw, ip, port = parts
            else:
                raise ValueError(f"Cannot parse proxy: {p}")
            return f"{scheme}://{user}:{pw}@{ip}:{port}"
        if len(parts) == 3:
            if cls._IP_RE.match(parts[0]) and parts[1].isdigit():
                return f"{scheme}://{parts[2]}@{parts[0]}:{parts[1]}"
        raise ValueError(f"Cannot parse proxy: {p}")

    def _is_high_priority(self, url: str) -> bool:
        return any(p in url for p in self._HIGH_PRIORITY_PATHS)

    def _build_headers(self, url: str, extra: dict | None = None) -> dict:
        d = self.device
        conn_type = self._conn_type

        raw = {
            "X-IG-App-Locale":               d.locale,
            "X-IG-Device-Locale":            d.locale,
            "X-IG-Mapped-Locale":            d.locale,
            "X-Pigeon-Session-Id":           d.pigeon_session_id,
            "X-Pigeon-Rawclienttime":        f"{time.time():.3f}",
            "X-IG-Connection-Speed":         f"{random.randint(800, 6500)}kbps",
            "X-IG-Bandwidth-Speed-KBPS":     f"{random.uniform(1500, 4500):.3f}",
            "X-IG-Bandwidth-TotalBytes-B":   str(self._bw_total_bytes),
            "X-IG-Bandwidth-TotalTime-MS":   str(self._bw_total_ms),
            "X-IG-App-Startup-Country":      d.country,
            "X-Bloks-Version-Id":            d.bloks_version,
            "X-IG-WWW-Claim":                self.www_claim,
            "X-Bloks-Is-Layout-RTL":         "true" if d.language in ("ar", "he", "fa", "ur") else "false",
            "X-IG-Device-ID":                d.device_id,
            "X-IG-Family-Device-ID":         d.family_device_id,
            "X-IG-Timezone-Offset":          str(d.timezone_offset),
            "X-IG-Connection-Type":          conn_type,
            "X-IG-Capabilities":             IG_CAPABILITIES,
            "X-IG-App-ID":                   d.ig_app_id,
            "Priority":                      "u=1, i" if self._is_high_priority(url) else "u=3",
            "User-Agent":                    d.user_agent(),
            "Accept-Language":               f"{d.locale.replace('_','-')}, {d.language};q=0.9",
            "Accept":                        "*/*",
        }

        # ═══ ПЛАТФОРМО-СПЕЦИФИКА ═══
        if d.platform == "android":
            raw["Accept-Encoding"]       = "gzip, deflate"
            raw["X-IG-Android-ID"]       = f"android-{d.android_id}"
            raw["X-FB-HTTP-Engine"]      = "Liger"
            raw["X-FB-Client-IP"]        = "True"
            raw["X-FB-Server-Cluster"]   = "True"
            raw["X-Bloks-Is-Panorama-Enabled"] = "true"
        else:
            # ═══ iOS ═══
            raw["Accept-Encoding"] = "gzip, deflate, br"
            # X-IG-EU-DC-ENABLED — присутствует в EU, "true" если гео EU
            raw["X-IG-EU-DC-ENABLED"] = "true" if d.country in (
                "DE","FR","ES","IT","NL","BE","PL","PT","SE","NO","DK","FI",
                "IE","AT","GR","CZ","HU","RO","BG","LT","LV","EE","SK","SI",
                "HR","LU","MT","CY","GB") else "false"
            # Prism бэйдж — включён во всех современных IG iOS
            raw["X-Bloks-Prism-Button-Version"]           = "INDIGO_PRIMARY_BORDERED"
            raw["X-Bloks-Prism-Colors-Enabled"]           = "true"
            raw["X-Bloks-Prism-Ax-Base-Colors-Enabled"]   = "false"
            raw["X-Bloks-Prism-Font-Enabled"]             = "true"
            # FB Connection quality — реальный iOS шлёт эти 4 всегда
            raw["X-FB-Connection-Type"]          = "wifi"
            raw["X-FB-Connection-Type-Detailed"] = "wifi"
            raw["X-FB-Connection-Quality"]       = random.choice(
                ["EXCELLENT", "GOOD", "MODERATE"])
            raw["X-FB-Connection-Bandwidth"]     = str(random.randint(1500000, 8000000))

        # Nav chain — только для upload path и current_user
        if any(x in url for x in ("/configure_to_clips/", "/rupload_igvideo",
                                    "/rupload_igphoto")):
            nav_time = time.time()
            raw["X-Ig-Nav-Chain"] = (
                f"MainFeedFragment:feed_timeline:1:cold_start:{nav_time:.3f}::,"
                f"IgCameraFragment:ig_camera:2:button:{nav_time+random.randint(30,120):.3f}::,"
                f"ClipsShareSheetFragment:clips_share_sheet:3:button:{nav_time+random.randint(60,180):.3f}::"
            )

        if self.mid:
            raw["X-MID"] = self.mid

        if self._auth_header:
            raw["Authorization"] = self._auth_header

        for hk, hv in self._ig_u_headers.items():
            raw[hk] = hv

        for c in self._session.cookies.jar:
            if c.name == "csrftoken" and c.value:
                raw["X-CSRFToken"] = c.value
                break

        if extra:
            raw.update(extra)

        order = self._ANDROID_ORDER if d.platform == "android" else self._IOS_ORDER
        ordered = {}
        for k in order:
            if k in raw:
                ordered[k] = raw.pop(k)
        ordered.update(raw)
        return ordered

    def _absorb_response(self, r):
        set_claim = r.headers.get("X-IG-Set-WWW-Claim") or r.headers.get("x-ig-set-www-claim")
        if set_claim: self.www_claim = set_claim
        set_mid = r.headers.get("ig-set-x-mid") or r.headers.get("X-IG-Set-Mid")
        if set_mid: self.mid = set_mid
        set_auth = r.headers.get("ig-set-authorization")
        if set_auth:
            self._auth_header = set_auth if set_auth.startswith("Bearer ") else f"Bearer {set_auth}"
        try:
            for c in self._session.cookies.jar:
                if c.name == "mid" and c.value: self.mid = c.value
                elif c.name == "rur" and c.value: self.rur = c.value
        except Exception:
            pass

    async def request(self, method, url, **kw):
        import time as _t
        extra = kw.pop("headers", {}) or {}
        headers = self._build_headers(url, extra)

        TRANSIENT_MARKERS = (
            "curl: (7)", "curl: (28)", "curl: (35)",
            "curl: (55)", "curl: (56)",
            "Connection reset", "closed abruptly",
        )

        last_exc = None
        for attempt in range(3):
            _start = _t.monotonic()
            try:
                r = await self._session.request(method, url, headers=headers, **kw)
            except Exception as e:
                emsg = str(e)
                if attempt < 2 and any(m in emsg for m in TRANSIENT_MARKERS):
                    await asyncio.sleep(
                        random.uniform(1.0, 2.0) if attempt == 0
                        else random.uniform(3.0, 5.0))
                    last_exc = e
                    continue
                raise
            elapsed_ms = int((_t.monotonic() - _start) * 1000)
            try:
                content_len = int(r.headers.get("content-length", 0)) or len(r.content)
            except Exception:
                content_len = 0
            self._bw_total_bytes += content_len
            self._bw_total_ms += elapsed_ms
            self._absorb_response(r)
            return r

        if last_exc:
            raise last_exc

    async def close(self):
        await self._session.close()
core/uniquifier.py
"""
Уникализатор с семафором на ffmpeg (защита от перегрузки CPU при 70+ потоках).
Универсальный пресет = быстрый + реально ломает pHash + чистит метаданные.
"""
import subprocess
import random
import tempfile
import os
import asyncio
from pathlib import Path


# Семафор для параллельных ffmpeg — независим от network threads
# 6 одновременных ffmpeg = ~90% загрузка 6-8 ядерного CPU
_FFMPEG_SEM = asyncio.Semaphore(12)


class ReelUniquifier:
    """
    Уровни:
        fast       — только remux + metadata strip (~0.5 сек, слабое изменение pHash)
        universal  — trim + micro-crop + audio pitch + metadata strip (~2-4 сек, СИЛЬНОЕ изменение pHash)
        light      — как universal но нежнее (~3-5 сек)
        medium     — universal + eq + rotate (~5-8 сек)
        heavy      — full processing, лучше не юзать на 70 потоков (~10-15 сек)
    """

    @staticmethod
    async def unique_async(src: str, dst: str = None, level: str = "universal",
                           audio: bool = True) -> str:
        """Async обёртка с семафором. Используй эту в orchestrator."""
        async with _FFMPEG_SEM:
            loop = asyncio.get_event_loop()
            return await loop.run_in_executor(
                None, ReelUniquifier.unique, src, dst, level, audio
            )

    @staticmethod
    def unique(src: str, dst: str = None, level: str = "universal",
               audio: bool = True) -> str:
        """
        Основной диспетчер уровней уникализации.
        Если доступен NVENC (NVIDIA GPU) — universal автоматически идёт через GPU.
        """
        dst = dst or tempfile.mktemp(suffix=".mp4")

        # ── авто GPU для universal, если есть NVENC (без параметра use_gpu) ──
        if level == "universal" and ReelUniquifier._has_nvenc():
            return ReelUniquifier._universal_nvenc(src, dst, audio)

        if level == "fast":
            return ReelUniquifier._fast(src, dst)
        if level == "compact":
            return ReelUniquifier._compact(src, dst, audio)
        if level == "universal":
            return ReelUniquifier._universal(src, dst, audio)
        if level == "light":
            return ReelUniquifier._light(src, dst, audio)
        if level == "medium":
            return ReelUniquifier._medium(src, dst, audio)
        if level == "heavy":
            return ReelUniquifier._heavy(src, dst, audio)

        return ReelUniquifier._universal(src, dst, audio)

    # ─────────────────────────────────────
    @staticmethod
    def _random_encoder_signature():
        """Случайные метаданные — маскируем что это ffmpeg."""
        return {
            "encoder": random.choice([
                "Lavc60.31.102 libx264",       # ffmpeg
                "Apple H.264 Encoder",         # iPhone
                "Android MediaCodec",          # Android
                "InstagramAndroid 449.0.0",
                "InstagramiOS 449.0.0",
            ]),
            "comment": os.urandom(8).hex(),
            "title": f"video_{random.randint(100000, 999999)}",
            "software": random.choice([
                "Instagram", "com.instagram.android", "iOS Photos",
            ]),
        }
    @staticmethod
    def _has_nvenc() -> bool:
        """Проверяет доступность NVENC (NVIDIA GPU encoder)."""
        if hasattr(ReelUniquifier, "_nvenc_cache"):
            return ReelUniquifier._nvenc_cache
        try:
            out = subprocess.check_output(
                ["ffmpeg", "-hide_banner", "-encoders"],
                stderr=subprocess.STDOUT, timeout=5
            ).decode(errors="ignore")
            has = "h264_nvenc" in out
            ReelUniquifier._nvenc_cache = has
            return has
        except Exception:
            ReelUniquifier._nvenc_cache = False
            return False
    @staticmethod
    def _run_ffmpeg(cmd: list, description: str = "ffmpeg") -> None:
        """
        Запускает ffmpeg и при ошибке выводит РЕАЛЬНЫЙ stderr,
        а не проглатывает его.
        """
        try:
            subprocess.run(cmd, check=True, capture_output=True, timeout=180)
        except subprocess.CalledProcessError as e:
            stderr = ""
            if e.stderr:
                stderr = e.stderr.decode(errors='ignore').strip()
            # берём последние 400 символов — там обычно причина
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"[{description}] ffmpeg failed: {stderr[-400:]}"
            ) from e
        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
            raise RuntimeError(f"[{description}] ffmpeg timeout > 180s")

    @staticmethod
    def _universal_nvenc(src, dst, audio):
        """
        NVENC-ускоренная версия. При ошибке fallback на CPU-версию.
        """
        speed = random.uniform(0.99, 1.01)
        trim_start = random.uniform(0.10, 0.25)
        crop = random.randint(2, 6)
        audio_semitones = random.uniform(-0.15, 0.15)

        vf = (
            f"trim=start={trim_start},setpts=PTS-STARTPTS,"
            f"crop=iw-{crop*2}:ih-{crop*2},"
            f"scale=720:1280:force_original_aspect_ratio=decrease,"
            f"pad=720:1280:(ow-iw)/2:(oh-ih)/2:color=black,"
            f"setpts={1/speed}*PTS"
        )

        cmd = ["ffmpeg", "-y", "-hide_banner", "-loglevel", "error",
               "-i", src, "-vf", vf]

        if audio:
            audio_speed = speed * (2 ** (audio_semitones / 12))
            af = (
                f"atrim=start={trim_start},asetpts=PTS-STARTPTS,"
                f"asetrate=44100*{2**(audio_semitones/12)},aresample=44100,"
                f"atempo={audio_speed / (2**(audio_semitones/12))}"
            )
            cmd += ["-af", af, "-c:a", "aac", "-b:a", "96k"]
        else:
            cmd += ["-an"]

        meta = ReelUniquifier._random_encoder_signature()
        cmd += [
            "-c:v", "h264_nvenc",
            "-preset", "p4",
            "-rc", "vbr",
            "-cq", "26",
            "-b:v", "0",
            "-profile:v", "high",
            "-pix_fmt", "yuv420p",
            "-g", str(random.choice([48, 60, 72])),
            "-t", "60",
            "-map_metadata", "-1",
            "-metadata", f"encoder={meta['encoder']}",
            "-metadata", f"comment={meta['comment']}",
            "-movflags", "+faststart",
            dst,
        ]

        try:
            ReelUniquifier._run_ffmpeg(cmd, "universal-nvenc")
            return dst
        except RuntimeError as e:
            # NVENC-ошибка → отключаем на будущее + fallback на CPU
            print(f"[uniquify] NVENC failed, fallback to CPU: {str(e)[:200]}")
            ReelUniquifier._nvenc_cache = False   # больше не пытаемся GPU
            return ReelUniquifier._universal(src, dst, audio)
    # ─────────────────────────────────────
    @staticmethod
    def _fast(src, dst):
        """Только remux + metadata strip. Быстро но pHash слабо меняется."""
        meta = ReelUniquifier._random_encoder_signature()
        cmd = [
            "ffmpeg", "-y", "-hide_banner", "-loglevel", "error",
            "-i", src,
            "-c", "copy",
            "-map_metadata", "-1",           # убираем ВСЕ метаданные из инпута
            "-movflags", "+faststart",
            "-metadata", f"encoder={meta['encoder']}",
            "-metadata", f"comment={meta['comment']}",
            "-metadata", f"title={meta['title']}",
            dst,
        ]
        ReelUniquifier._run_ffmpeg(cmd, "fast")   # или "compact"/"light"/"medium"/"heavy"
        return dst

    # ─────────────────────────────────────
    @staticmethod
    def _universal(src, dst, audio):
        """
        Universal с обработкой ошибок и fallback:
        - Если аудио-фильтр падает (нет аудио дорожки) — retry без -af
        - Ошибки ffmpeg показываются с реальным stderr
        """
        speed = random.uniform(0.99, 1.01)
        trim_start = random.uniform(0.10, 0.25)
        crop = random.randint(2, 6)
        audio_semitones = random.uniform(-0.15, 0.15)

        vf = (
            f"trim=start={trim_start},setpts=PTS-STARTPTS,"
            f"crop=iw-{crop*2}:ih-{crop*2},"
            f"scale=720:1280:force_original_aspect_ratio=decrease,"
            f"pad=720:1280:(ow-iw)/2:(oh-ih)/2:color=black,"
            f"setpts={1/speed}*PTS"
        )

        def _build_cmd(with_audio: bool):
            cmd = ["ffmpeg", "-y", "-hide_banner", "-loglevel", "error",
                   "-i", src, "-vf", vf]

            if with_audio and audio:
                audio_speed = speed * (2 ** (audio_semitones / 12))
                af = (
                    f"atrim=start={trim_start},asetpts=PTS-STARTPTS,"
                    f"asetrate=44100*{2**(audio_semitones/12)},aresample=44100,"
                    f"atempo={audio_speed / (2**(audio_semitones/12))}"
                )
                cmd += ["-af", af, "-c:a", "aac", "-b:a", "96k"]
            else:
                cmd += ["-an"]

            meta = ReelUniquifier._random_encoder_signature()
            cmd += [
                "-c:v", "libx264",
                "-preset", "veryfast",
                "-crf", "26",
                "-profile:v", "high",
                "-pix_fmt", "yuv420p",
                "-g", str(random.choice([48, 60, 72])),
                "-t", "60",
                "-map_metadata", "-1",
                "-metadata", f"encoder={meta['encoder']}",
                "-metadata", f"comment={meta['comment']}",
                "-metadata", f"title={meta['title']}",
                "-metadata", f"software={meta['software']}",
                "-movflags", "+faststart",
                dst,
            ]
            return cmd

        # первая попытка с аудио
        try:
            ReelUniquifier._run_ffmpeg(_build_cmd(with_audio=True), "universal")
            return dst
        except RuntimeError as e:
            msg = str(e).lower()
            # fallback: если проблема в аудио — пробуем без него
            if "stream" in msg or "audio" in msg or "atrim" in msg or "atempo" in msg:
                print(f"[uniquify] retry без аудио (source без audio track?)")
                try:
                    ReelUniquifier._run_ffmpeg(_build_cmd(with_audio=False), "universal-noaudio")
                    return dst
                except RuntimeError:
                    raise
            raise
    @staticmethod
    def _compact(src, dst, audio):
        """
        МАКСИМАЛЬНАЯ экономия трафика.
        - 720x1280
        - CRF 28 (заметно ниже качество на big screens, но идеально для mobile IG)
        - superfast preset
        - Audio 64k mono (говорильные видео)
        - Итог: файл ~1.5-2.5 MB на 15-сек рилс (~85% экономия)
        
        Использовать если: очень дорогие прокси, короткие рилсы (5-15 сек),
        контент с речью (без сложной музыки).
        """
        speed = random.uniform(0.99, 1.01)
        trim_start = random.uniform(0.10, 0.20)
        crop = random.randint(2, 5)

        vf = (
            f"trim=start={trim_start},setpts=PTS-STARTPTS,"
            f"crop=iw-{crop*2}:ih-{crop*2},"
            f"scale=720:1280:force_original_aspect_ratio=decrease,"
            f"pad=720:1280:(ow-iw)/2:(oh-ih)/2:color=black,"
            f"setpts={1/speed}*PTS"
        )

        cmd = ["ffmpeg", "-y", "-hide_banner", "-loglevel", "error",
               "-i", src, "-vf", vf]

        if audio:
            # моно + низкий bitrate — максимум экономии
            cmd += [
                "-af", f"atrim=start={trim_start},asetpts=PTS-STARTPTS,atempo={speed},aresample=44100",
                "-c:a", "aac",
                "-b:a", "64k",
                "-ac", "1",   # моно
            ]
        else:
            cmd += ["-an"]

        meta = ReelUniquifier._random_encoder_signature()
        cmd += [
            "-c:v", "libx264",
            "-preset", "superfast",
            "-crf", "28",
            "-profile:v", "main",
            "-pix_fmt", "yuv420p",
            "-g", "60",
            "-t", "60",
            "-map_metadata", "-1",
            "-metadata", f"encoder={meta['encoder']}",
            "-movflags", "+faststart",
            dst,
        ]
        subprocess.run(cmd, check=True, capture_output=True)
        return dst
    # ─────────────────────────────────────
    @staticmethod
    def _light(src, dst, audio):
        """Universal - минус trim (мягче для короткого контента)."""
        speed = random.uniform(0.995, 1.005)
        crop = random.randint(1, 3)
        brtn = random.uniform(-0.01, 0.01)
        satu = random.uniform(0.99, 1.01)

        vf = (
            f"crop=iw-{crop*2}:ih-{crop*2},"
            f"eq=brightness={brtn}:saturation={satu},"
            f"setpts={1/speed}*PTS"
        )
        cmd = ["ffmpeg", "-y", "-hide_banner", "-loglevel", "error",
               "-i", src, "-vf", vf]

        if audio:
            cmd += ["-af", f"atempo={speed}", "-c:a", "aac", "-b:a", "128k"]
        else:
            cmd += ["-an"]

        meta = ReelUniquifier._random_encoder_signature()
        cmd += [
            "-c:v", "libx264", "-preset", "ultrafast", "-crf", "23",
            "-g", "60", "-map_metadata", "-1",
            "-metadata", f"encoder={meta['encoder']}",
            "-metadata", f"comment={meta['comment']}",
            "-movflags", "+faststart",
            dst,
        ]
        subprocess.run(cmd, check=True, capture_output=True)
        return dst

    # ─────────────────────────────────────
    @staticmethod
    def _medium(src, dst, audio):
        """Universal + eq + rotate. Заметно медленнее."""
        speed = random.uniform(0.98, 1.02)
        trim_start = random.uniform(0.15, 0.30)
        crop = random.randint(3, 8)
        rot = random.uniform(-0.4, 0.4)
        brtn = random.uniform(-0.03, 0.03)
        contr = random.uniform(0.97, 1.03)
        satu = random.uniform(0.95, 1.05)
        audio_semitones = random.uniform(-0.2, 0.2)

        vf = (
            f"trim=start={trim_start},setpts=PTS-STARTPTS,"
            f"crop=iw-{crop*2}:ih-{crop*2},"
            f"eq=brightness={brtn}:contrast={contr}:saturation={satu},"
            f"rotate={rot}*PI/180:fillcolor=black@0,"
            f"scale=1080:1920:force_original_aspect_ratio=decrease,"
            f"pad=1080:1920:(ow-iw)/2:(oh-ih)/2:color=black,"
            f"setpts={1/speed}*PTS"
        )
        cmd = ["ffmpeg", "-y", "-hide_banner", "-loglevel", "error",
               "-i", src, "-vf", vf]

        if audio:
            audio_speed = speed * (2 ** (audio_semitones / 12))
            af = (
                f"atrim=start={trim_start},asetpts=PTS-STARTPTS,"
                f"asetrate=44100*{2**(audio_semitones/12)},aresample=44100,"
                f"atempo={audio_speed / (2**(audio_semitones/12))}"
            )
            cmd += ["-af", af, "-c:a", "aac", "-b:a", "128k"]
        else:
            cmd += ["-an"]

        meta = ReelUniquifier._random_encoder_signature()
        cmd += [
            "-c:v", "libx264", "-preset", "ultrafast", "-crf", "22",
            "-g", "60", "-t", "60", "-map_metadata", "-1",
            "-metadata", f"encoder={meta['encoder']}",
            "-metadata", f"comment={meta['comment']}",
            "-movflags", "+faststart",
            dst,
        ]
        subprocess.run(cmd, check=True, capture_output=True)
        return dst

    # ─────────────────────────────────────
    @staticmethod
    def _heavy(src, dst, audio):
        """Как medium но с более агрессивными изменениями. Не для 70 потоков."""
        speed = random.uniform(0.97, 1.03)
        trim_start = random.uniform(0.20, 0.40)
        crop = random.randint(5, 12)
        rot = random.uniform(-0.7, 0.7)
        brtn = random.uniform(-0.05, 0.05)
        contr = random.uniform(0.93, 1.07)
        satu = random.uniform(0.92, 1.08)
        audio_semitones = random.uniform(-0.3, 0.3)

        vf = (
            f"trim=start={trim_start},setpts=PTS-STARTPTS,"
            f"crop=iw-{crop*2}:ih-{crop*2},"
            f"eq=brightness={brtn}:contrast={contr}:saturation={satu},"
            f"rotate={rot}*PI/180:fillcolor=black@0,"
            f"scale=1080:1920:force_original_aspect_ratio=decrease,"
            f"pad=1080:1920:(ow-iw)/2:(oh-ih)/2:color=black,"
            f"setpts={1/speed}*PTS"
        )
        cmd = ["ffmpeg", "-y", "-hide_banner", "-loglevel", "error",
               "-i", src, "-vf", vf]

        if audio:
            audio_speed = speed * (2 ** (audio_semitones / 12))
            af = (
                f"atrim=start={trim_start},asetpts=PTS-STARTPTS,"
                f"asetrate=44100*{2**(audio_semitones/12)},aresample=44100,"
                f"atempo={audio_speed / (2**(audio_semitones/12))}"
            )
            cmd += ["-af", af, "-c:a", "aac", "-b:a", "128k"]
        else:
            cmd += ["-an"]

        meta = ReelUniquifier._random_encoder_signature()
        cmd += [
            "-c:v", "libx264", "-preset", "veryfast", "-crf", "20",
            "-g", "60", "-t", "60", "-map_metadata", "-1",
            "-metadata", f"encoder={meta['encoder']}",
            "-metadata", f"comment={meta['comment']}",
            "-movflags", "+faststart",
            dst,
        ]
        subprocess.run(cmd, check=True, capture_output=True)
        return dst
gui/__init__.py

gui/accounts_table.py
from PyQt6.QtWidgets import (QTableWidget, QTableWidgetItem, QAbstractItemView,
                             QMenu, QApplication, QInputDialog, QFileDialog, QMessageBox)
from PyQt6.QtGui import QKeySequence, QColor, QShortcut, QAction
from PyQt6.QtCore import Qt, QItemSelection, QItemSelectionModel
import json

class AccountsTable(QTableWidget):
    STATUS_COLORS = {
        "idle":    QColor(38, 40, 50),
        "running": QColor(46, 92, 55),
        "success": QColor(35, 100, 45),
        "error":   QColor(140, 45, 45),
        "dead":      QColor(60, 20, 20),   # ← ДОБАВИТЬ (тёмный красный)
        "banned":  QColor(90, 25, 25),
        "suspended": QColor(60, 20, 20),
                # ── новые ──
        "session_expired": QColor(120, 90, 30),   # оранжевый
        "checkpoint":      QColor(120, 90, 30),   # оранжевый
        "rate_limited":    QColor(90, 90, 30),    # жёлто-серый

    }
    COLUMNS = ["№", "Аккаунт", "Пароль", "Email", "Прокси", "Устройство",
               "Платформа", "Пачка", "Статус", "Последняя ошибка",
               "Просмотры", "Лайки", "Подписчики"]

    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__(0, len(self.COLUMNS))
        self.setHorizontalHeaderLabels(self.COLUMNS)
        self.setSelectionBehavior(QAbstractItemView.SelectionBehavior.SelectRows)
        self.setSelectionMode(QAbstractItemView.SelectionMode.ExtendedSelection)
        self.setContextMenuPolicy(Qt.ContextMenuPolicy.CustomContextMenu)
        self.customContextMenuRequested.connect(self._menu)
        self.setSortingEnabled(True)
        self.setAlternatingRowColors(True)
        self.verticalHeader().setVisible(False)
        
        self.setColumnHidden(2, True)  # пароль скрыт по умолчанию

        # data storage: row → account dict
        self._accounts: dict[str, dict] = {}

        
        QShortcut(QKeySequence("Delete"), self, self._delete_selected)
        QShortcut(QKeySequence("Ctrl+C"), self, self._copy_selected)
        QShortcut(QKeySequence("Ctrl+V"), self, self._paste_accounts)
        QShortcut(QKeySequence("Ctrl+F"), self, self._focus_search)
        QShortcut(QKeySequence("Ctrl+H"), self,
                  lambda: self.setColumnHidden(2, not self.isColumnHidden(2)))

    # ---------- data API ----------
    def add_account(self, acc: dict):
        was = self.isSortingEnabled()
        self.setSortingEnabled(False)
        r = self.rowCount()
        self.insertRow(r)
        login = acc.get("login", f"__unknown_{r}")
        self._accounts[login] = acc
        self._render_row(r, acc)
        self.setSortingEnabled(was)

    def get_account(self, row: int) -> dict:
        """Читает login из первой колонки (устойчиво к сортировке)."""
        it = self.item(row, 1)   # колонка "Аккаунт"
        if not it:
            return {}
        login = it.text()
        return self._accounts.get(login, {})

    def _render_row(self, r, acc):
        num_item = QTableWidgetItem()
        num_item.setData(Qt.ItemDataRole.DisplayRole, r + 1)
        self.setItem(r, 0, num_item)
        self.setItem(r, 1, QTableWidgetItem(acc.get("login", "")))
        self.setItem(r, 2, QTableWidgetItem(acc.get("password", "")))
        self.setItem(r, 3, QTableWidgetItem(acc.get("email", "")))
        proxy_val = acc.get("proxy", "") or ""
        proxy_item = QTableWidgetItem(proxy_val)
        proxy_item.setToolTip(proxy_val)   # полный прокси при наведении
        self.setItem(r, 4, proxy_item)
        self.setItem(r, 5, QTableWidgetItem(acc.get("device_summary", "auto")))
        self.setItem(r, 6, QTableWidgetItem(acc.get("platform", "auto")))
        self.setItem(r, 7, QTableWidgetItem(acc.get("import_batch") or ""))   # ← новая
        status = (acc.get("status") or "idle").lower()
        self.set_row_status(r, status, acc.get("last_error") or "")
        self.setItem(r, 10, QTableWidgetItem(str(acc.get("total_views") or "")))
        self.setItem(r, 11, QTableWidgetItem(str(acc.get("total_likes") or "")))
        self.setItem(r, 12, QTableWidgetItem(str(acc.get("followers") or "")))
        search_str = " ".join(str(v) for v in [
            acc.get("login", ""),
            acc.get("proxy", "") or "",
            acc.get("status", "") or "",
            acc.get("import_batch") or "",
            acc.get("email", "") or "",
        ]).lower()
        self._accounts.setdefault(acc.get("login", ""), acc)["_search_cache"] = search_str
    # ---------- menu ----------
    def _menu(self, pos):
        m = QMenu(self)
        m.addAction("Запустить только выделенные\tF5", self._run_selected_signal)
        m.addAction("Копировать (login:pass:proxy)\tCtrl+C", self._copy_selected)
        m.addAction("Показать/скрыть пароли\tCtrl+H",
                    lambda: self.setColumnHidden(2, not self.isColumnHidden(2)))
        m.addSeparator()
        m.addAction("Назначить пачку выделенным…", self._assign_batch)
        m.addAction("Привязать прокси выделенным (только без прокси)", self._bind_selected)
        m.addAction("ПЕРЕ-привязать прокси выделенным (замена)", self._rebind_selected)
        m.addSeparator()
        m.addAction("Привязать ВЫДЕЛЕННЫЕ прокси (из панели «Прокси»)", self._bind_selected_proxies_from_panel)
        m.addAction("Открыть в браузере (профиль)", self._open_in_browser)
        m.addAction("Сбросить статус", self._reset_status)
        m.addAction("Сбросить прокси у выделенных", self._clear_proxy_selected)
        m.addAction("Пересгенерировать устройство", self._regen_device)
        m.addSeparator()
        m.addAction("Разрешить повторную установку био (сбросить bio_hash)",
                    self._reset_bio_hash)
        m.addAction("Сбросить cooldown этой задачи", self._reset_cooldown_for_action)
        m.addAction("Сбросить cooldown всех задач",  self._reset_cooldown_all)
        m.addAction("Экспорт выделенных (IAM txt)", self._export_iam)
        m.addAction("Экспорт с cookies (JSON)", self._export_json)
        m.addSeparator()
        m.addAction("Удалить\tDel", self._delete_selected)
        m.exec(self.viewport().mapToGlobal(pos))

    # ---------- операции ----------
    def _selected_rows(self):
        return sorted({i.row() for i in self.selectedIndexes()})

    def _delete_selected(self):
        rows = self._selected_rows()
        if not rows:
            return
        db = None
        p = self.parent()
        while p is not None:
            if hasattr(p, "db"):
                db = p.db
                break
            p = p.parent()

        # собираем логины ПЕРЕД удалением строк (индексы сдвинутся)
        logins_to_delete = []
        for r in rows:
            it = self.item(r, 1)
            if it:
                logins_to_delete.append(it.text())

        for r in reversed(rows):
            self.removeRow(r)
        for login in logins_to_delete:
            self._accounts.pop(login, None)
            if db:
                db.delete_account(login)

        # обновим нумерацию № (для видимого порядка)
        for r in range(self.rowCount()):
            it = self.item(r, 0)
            if it:
                from PyQt6.QtCore import Qt
                it.setData(Qt.ItemDataRole.DisplayRole, r + 1)
    def _select_all_visible(self):
        """Ctrl+A — выделяет ТОЛЬКО видимые строки (не скрытые фильтром)."""
        sm = self.selectionModel()
        sm.clearSelection()

        selection = QItemSelection()
        for r in range(self.rowCount()):
            if not self.isRowHidden(r):
                top_left = self.model().index(r, 0)
                bottom_right = self.model().index(r, self.columnCount() - 1)
                selection.select(top_left, bottom_right)

        sm.select(
            selection,
            QItemSelectionModel.SelectionFlag.Select |
            QItemSelectionModel.SelectionFlag.Rows
        )
    def _copy_selected(self):
        lines = []
        for r in self._selected_rows():
            a = self.get_account(r)          # ← вместо self._accounts[r]
            if not a.get("login"):
                continue
            line = f"{a['login']}:{a.get('password','')}"
            if a.get("proxy"):
                line += f":{a['proxy']}"
            lines.append(line)
        QApplication.clipboard().setText("\n".join(lines))
    def _assign_batch(self):
        from PyQt6.QtWidgets import QInputDialog, QTableWidgetItem
        rows = self._selected_rows()
        if not rows:
            return
        name, ok = QInputDialog.getText(self, "Пачка", "Название пачки:")
        if not ok or not name.strip():
            return
        name = name.strip()

        db = None
        p = self.parent()
        while p is not None:
            if hasattr(p, "db"):
                db = p.db
                break
            p = p.parent()
        if not db:
            return

        for r in rows:
            acc = self.get_account(r)
            login = acc.get("login")
            if not login:
                continue
            db.conn.execute(
                "UPDATE accounts SET import_batch=? WHERE login=?",
                (name, login)
            )
            self._accounts[login]["import_batch"] = name
            self.setItem(r, 7, QTableWidgetItem(name))
        db.conn.commit()
    def _paste_accounts(self):
        # прокидывается в MainWindow — там доступ к БД
        # тут просто эмулируем
        from storage.importer import AccountImporter
        txt = QApplication.clipboard().text()
        for line in txt.splitlines():
            acc = AccountImporter.parse_line(line)
            if acc and acc.get("login"):
                self.add_account(acc)
    def _bind_selected_proxies_from_panel(self):
        mw = self._get_main_window()
        if not mw:
            return
        rows = self._selected_rows()
        accs = [self.get_account(r) for r in rows]
        accs = [a for a in accs if a.get("login")]
        if not accs:
            QMessageBox.information(self, "!", "Нет выделенных аккаунтов")
            return
        mw.bind_selected_proxies_to_accounts(accs)
        # рефреш видимых прокси в колонке
        for r in rows:
            acc = self.get_account(r)
            proxy_val = acc.get("proxy", "") or ""
            it = QTableWidgetItem(proxy_val)
            it.setToolTip(proxy_val)
            self.setItem(r, 4, it)
    def _focus_search(self):
        # обрабатывается MainWindow — эмитим сигнал через parent
        pass

    def _run_selected_signal(self):
        # MainWindow слушает
        pass

    def _reset_status(self):
        for r in self._selected_rows():
            self.set_row_status(r, "idle", "")
    def _reset_bio_hash(self):
        mw = self._get_main_window()
        if not mw or not mw.db:
            return
        rows = self._selected_rows()
        if not rows:
            return
        reply = QMessageBox.question(
            self, "?",
            f"Сбросить bio_hash у {len(rows)} акков?\n"
            f"После этого при следующей задаче 'Био' на них снова будет ставиться."
        )
        if reply != QMessageBox.StandardButton.Yes:
            return
        for r in rows:
            acc = self.get_account(r)
            login = acc.get("login")
            if login:
                mw.db.conn.execute(
                    "UPDATE accounts SET bio_hash=NULL, bio_updated_at=NULL WHERE login=?",
                    (login,)
                )
                self._accounts[login]["bio_hash"] = None
        mw.db.conn.commit()
        QMessageBox.information(self, "OK", f"Сброшено у {len(rows)} акков")
    def _regen_device(self):
        from PyQt6.QtWidgets import QTableWidgetItem
        for r in self._selected_rows():
            acc = self.get_account(r)   # ← теперь через метод
            acc.pop("device_id", None)
            acc.pop("android_id", None)
            acc.pop("user_agent", None)
            self.setItem(r, 5, QTableWidgetItem("auto (regen)"))

    def _open_in_browser(self):
        import webbrowser
        for r in self._selected_rows():
            acc = self.get_account(r)
            if acc.get("login"):
                webbrowser.open(f"https://instagram.com/{acc['login']}")

    def _export_iam(self):
        p, _ = QFileDialog.getSaveFileName(self, "Экспорт", "accounts.txt", "*.txt")
        if not p:
            return
        rows = self._selected_rows() or list(range(self.rowCount()))
        lines = []
        for r in rows:
            a = self.get_account(r)          # ← фикс
            if not a.get("login"):
                continue
            line = f"{a['login']}:{a.get('password','')}"
            if a.get("email"):
                line += f":{a['email']}:{a.get('email_password','')}"
            if a.get("proxy"):
                line += f":{a['proxy']}"
            lines.append(line)
        from pathlib import Path
        Path(p).write_text("\n".join(lines), "utf-8")

    def _export_json(self):
        p, _ = QFileDialog.getSaveFileName(self, "Экспорт JSON", "accounts.json", "*.json")
        if not p:
            return
        rows = self._selected_rows() or list(range(self.rowCount()))
        accs = []
        for r in rows:
            a = self.get_account(r)          # ← фикс
            if a.get("login"):
                accs.append(a)
        from pathlib import Path
        Path(p).write_text(
            json.dumps(accs, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2, default=str),
            "utf-8"
        )
    def _reset_cooldown_for_action(self):
        """Сбрасывает историю только текущей задачи (из currentTab)."""
        from PyQt6.QtWidgets import QInputDialog
        mw = self._get_main_window()
        if not mw:
            return

        # определяем action по текущей вкладке
        tab_widget = mw.tabs.currentWidget()
        from PyQt6.QtWidgets import QScrollArea
        if isinstance(tab_widget, QScrollArea):
            tab_widget = tab_widget.widget()

        action_map = {
            id(mw.reels_panel):  "upload_reel",
            id(mw.bio_panel):    "set_bio",
            id(mw.avatar_panel): "set_avatar",
            id(mw.stats_panel):  "collect_stats",
        }
        action = action_map.get(id(tab_widget))
        if not action:
            QMessageBox.information(self, "!",
                "Переключись на вкладку задачи (Reels/Био/Аватар/Стата) "
                "и повтори действие.")
            return

        rows = self._selected_rows()
        if not rows:
            return
        for r in rows:
            login = self.get_account(r).get("login")
            if login:
                mw.db.clear_task_history(login=login, action=action)
        QMessageBox.information(self, "OK",
            f"Cooldown '{action}' сброшен для {len(rows)} акков")

    def _reset_cooldown_all(self):
        mw = self._get_main_window()
        if not mw:
            return
        rows = self._selected_rows()
        if not rows:
            return
        for r in rows:
            login = self.get_account(r).get("login")
            if login:
                mw.db.clear_task_history(login=login)
        QMessageBox.information(self, "OK",
            f"Cooldown всех задач сброшен для {len(rows)} акков")
    # ---------- статус ----------
    def set_row_status(self, row: int, status: str, error: str = ""):
        color = self.STATUS_COLORS.get(status, QColor(38, 40, 50))

        # обновляем цвет только видимых колонок
        for col in range(self.columnCount()):
            if self.isColumnHidden(col):
                continue
            it = self.item(row, col)
            if it:
                it.setBackground(color)

        # ставим новый item в колонке "Статус" (8) с точкой-индикатором
        dot_color = {
            "running": "#7bd88f", "success": "#7bd88f",
            "error":   "#ff6b6b", "banned":  "#ff4a4a",
            "dead":    "#ff4a4a", "suspended": "#ff4a4a",
            "idle":    "#7a7d88",
            "session_expired": "#ffb84d",
            "checkpoint":      "#ffb84d",
            "rate_limited":    "#ffd54d",
        }.get(status, "#c0c0c0")

        status_item = QTableWidgetItem(f"● {status}")
        status_item.setForeground(QColor(dot_color))
        status_item.setBackground(color)
        self.setItem(row, 8, status_item)

        # ставим item в колонке "Последняя ошибка" (9)
        if error:
            e = QTableWidgetItem(error[:200])
            e.setBackground(color)
            e.setToolTip(error)
            self.setItem(row, 9, e)

    def bulk_load_accounts(self, accounts: list[dict]):
        """
        Быстрая массовая загрузка без render per-row.
        В 20-50 раз быстрее чем add_account в цикле.
        """
        was_sorted = self.isSortingEnabled()
        self.setSortingEnabled(False)   # ← критично: сортировка в O(n²)
        self.setUpdatesEnabled(False)   # ← блокируем перерисовку

        try:
            # выставляем сразу нужное количество строк — SQLite быстрее чем insertRow x N
            self.setRowCount(len(accounts))

            for r, acc in enumerate(accounts):
                login = acc.get("login", f"__unknown_{r}")
                self._accounts[login] = acc
                self._render_row_fast(r, acc)
        finally:
            self.setUpdatesEnabled(True)
            self.setSortingEnabled(was_sorted)


    def _render_row_fast(self, r, acc):
        """Оптимизированная версия _render_row без лишних QColor вызовов."""
        from PyQt6.QtCore import Qt
        from PyQt6.QtWidgets import QTableWidgetItem

        # получаем цвет статуса один раз
        status = (acc.get("status") or "idle").lower()
        color = self.STATUS_COLORS.get(status, self.STATUS_COLORS["idle"])

        # создаём items и сразу красим — без прохода вторым циклом
        items = [
            None,   # 0: номер, сделаем ниже через setData
            QTableWidgetItem(acc.get("login", "")),
            QTableWidgetItem(acc.get("password", "")),
            QTableWidgetItem(acc.get("email", "")),
            QTableWidgetItem(acc.get("proxy", "") or ""),
            QTableWidgetItem(acc.get("device_summary", "auto")),
            QTableWidgetItem(acc.get("platform", "auto")),
            QTableWidgetItem(acc.get("import_batch") or ""),
            QTableWidgetItem(f"● {status}"),
            QTableWidgetItem((acc.get("last_error") or "")[:200]),
            QTableWidgetItem(str(acc.get("total_views") or "")),
            QTableWidgetItem(str(acc.get("total_likes") or "")),
            QTableWidgetItem(str(acc.get("followers") or "")),
        ]

        # номер строки
        num_item = QTableWidgetItem()
        num_item.setData(Qt.ItemDataRole.DisplayRole, r + 1)
        items[0] = num_item

        # прокси tooltip
        if items[4]:
            items[4].setToolTip(acc.get("proxy", "") or "")

        # красим все ячейки одним циклом
        for col, it in enumerate(items):
            if it:
                it.setBackground(color)
                self.setItem(r, col, it)

        # status колонка — доп. цветной foreground для точки
        dot_colors = {"running": "#7bd88f", "success": "#7bd88f",
                      "error": "#ff6b6b", "banned": "#ff4a4a",
                      "dead": "#ff4a4a", "idle": "#7a7d88"}
        from PyQt6.QtGui import QColor
        items[8].setForeground(QColor(dot_colors.get(status, "#c0c0c0")))

        # tooltip для ошибки
        if acc.get("last_error"):
            items[9].setToolTip(acc.get("last_error"))
    def _get_main_window(self):
        p = self.parent()
        while p is not None:
            if hasattr(p, "proxy_panel"):
                return p
            p = p.parent()
        return None

    def _bind_selected(self):
        mw = self._get_main_window()
        if not mw:
            return
        rows = self._selected_rows()
        accs = [self.get_account(r) for r in rows]
        accs = [a for a in accs if a.get("login")]
        mw.proxy_panel.bind_free_to(accs)
        # обновим отображение
        for r in rows:
            acc = self.get_account(r)
            proxy_val = acc.get("proxy", "") or ""
            it = QTableWidgetItem(proxy_val)
            it.setToolTip(proxy_val)
            self.setItem(r, 4, it)

    def _rebind_selected(self):
        from PyQt6.QtWidgets import QMessageBox
        mw = self._get_main_window()
        if not mw:
            return
        rows = self._selected_rows()
        if not rows:
            return
        reply = QMessageBox.question(
            self, "Пере-привязка",
            f"ЗАМЕНИТЬ прокси у {len(rows)} акков на новые из пула?\n"
            f"Старые прокси станут свободными."
        )
        if reply != QMessageBox.StandardButton.Yes:
            return
        accs = [self.get_account(r) for r in rows]
        accs = [a for a in accs if a.get("login")]
        mw.proxy_panel.rebind_to(accs, force=True)
        for r in rows:
            acc = self.get_account(r)
            proxy_val = acc.get("proxy", "") or ""
            it = QTableWidgetItem(proxy_val)
            it.setToolTip(proxy_val)
            self.setItem(r, 4, it)

    def _clear_proxy_selected(self):
        mw = self._get_main_window()
        db = mw.db if mw else None
        for r in self._selected_rows():
            acc = self.get_account(r)
            login = acc.get("login")
            old = acc.get("proxy")
            if not login:
                continue
            if db:
                db.conn.execute("UPDATE accounts SET proxy=NULL WHERE login=?", (login,))
                if old:
                    db.conn.execute("UPDATE proxies SET bound_to=NULL WHERE proxy=?", (old,))
            acc.pop("proxy", None)
            self.setItem(r, 4, QTableWidgetItem(""))
        if db:
            db.conn.commit()
gui/devices_panel.py
from PyQt6.QtWidgets import (QWidget, QVBoxLayout, QHBoxLayout, QGroupBox,
                             QSpinBox, QComboBox, QPushButton, QLabel, QFormLayout,
                             QTableWidget, QTableWidgetItem, QAbstractItemView,
                             QFileDialog, QMessageBox, QCheckBox)
from PyQt6.QtCore import Qt
import json
from pathlib import Path
from core.device import DeviceProfile

class DevicesPanel(QWidget):
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__()
        v = QVBoxLayout(self)

        info = QLabel("Генератор реальных device-профилей (Samsung / Xiaomi / Google / OnePlus / iPhone). "
                      "До 10 000 за раз. Каждый профиль — актуальная модель, DPI, разрешение, "
                      "CPU, версия IG APK/iOS.")
        info.setWordWrap(True)
        v.addWidget(info)

        gb = QGroupBox("Параметры генерации")
        fl = QFormLayout(gb)

        self.count = QSpinBox(); self.count.setRange(1, 100_000); self.count.setValue(1000)
        fl.addRow("Количество:", self.count)

        self.platform = QComboBox()
        self.platform.addItems(["Микс android/iOS 50/50", "Android 70% / iOS 30%",
                               "Только Android", "Только iOS"])
        fl.addRow("Платформа:", self.platform)

        self.locale = QComboBox()
        self.locale.addItems(["en_US", "ru_RU", "ja_JP", "ko_KR", "zh_CN", "es_ES", "pt_BR", "de_DE"])
        fl.addRow("Локаль:", self.locale)

        self.only_latest = QCheckBox("Только последние 3 версии IG APK (рекомендуется)")
        self.only_latest.setChecked(True)
        fl.addRow(self.only_latest)

        self.stable_seed = QCheckBox("Привязать к будущим аккаунтам (seed=login) — рекомендуется")
        self.stable_seed.setChecked(True)
        self.stable_seed.setToolTip("Если галка стоит — устройство ГЕНЕРИРУЕТСЯ ЗАНОВО на лету от логина. "
                                    "Тогда сохранённый пул нужен только чтобы полюбоваться. "
                                    "Обычно ты именно этого и хочешь.")
        fl.addRow(self.stable_seed)

        v.addWidget(gb)

        h = QHBoxLayout()
        self.btn_gen = QPushButton("Сгенерировать")
        self.btn_export = QPushButton("Экспорт JSON")
        self.btn_clear = QPushButton("Очистить")
        for b in (self.btn_gen, self.btn_export, self.btn_clear): h.addWidget(b)
        h.addStretch()
        v.addLayout(h)

        self.table = QTableWidget(0, 8)
        self.table.setHorizontalHeaderLabels(
            ["#", "Платформа", "Модель", "OS", "IG версия", "DPI", "Разрешение", "UA (preview)"])
        self.table.setSelectionBehavior(QAbstractItemView.SelectionBehavior.SelectRows)
        v.addWidget(self.table, 1)

        self.btn_gen.clicked.connect(self._generate)
        self.btn_export.clicked.connect(self._export)
        self.btn_clear.clicked.connect(lambda: self.table.setRowCount(0))

    def _generate(self):
        import random
        n = self.count.value()
        p_idx = self.platform.currentIndex()
        locale = self.locale.currentText()

        def pick_platform():
            if p_idx == 0: return random.choice(["android", "ios"])
            if p_idx == 1: return random.choices(["android", "ios"], weights=[70, 30])[0]
            if p_idx == 2: return "android"
            return "ios"

        self.table.setRowCount(0)
        self.table.setUpdatesEnabled(False)
        for i in range(n):
            dev = DeviceProfile.generate(platform=pick_platform(), locale=locale,
                                          seed=f"preview_{i}")
            r = self.table.rowCount(); self.table.insertRow(r)
            self.table.setItem(r, 0, QTableWidgetItem(str(i + 1)))
            self.table.setItem(r, 1, QTableWidgetItem(dev.platform))
            self.table.setItem(r, 2, QTableWidgetItem(dev.model))
            self.table.setItem(r, 3, QTableWidgetItem(dev.os_release))
            self.table.setItem(r, 4, QTableWidgetItem(dev.ig_version))
            self.table.setItem(r, 5, QTableWidgetItem(dev.dpi))
            self.table.setItem(r, 6, QTableWidgetItem(dev.resolution))
            self.table.setItem(r, 7, QTableWidgetItem(dev.user_agent()[:120] + "…"))
        self.table.setUpdatesEnabled(True)

    def _export(self):
        p, _ = QFileDialog.getSaveFileName(self, "Экспорт устройств", "devices_pool.json", "*.json")
        if not p: return
        rows = []
        for r in range(self.table.rowCount()):
            rows.append({
                "platform": self.table.item(r, 1).text(),
                "model":    self.table.item(r, 2).text(),
                "os":       self.table.item(r, 3).text(),
                "ig":       self.table.item(r, 4).text(),
                "dpi":      self.table.item(r, 5).text(),
                "resolution": self.table.item(r, 6).text(),
            })
        Path(p).write_text(json.dumps(rows, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), "utf-8")
        QMessageBox.information(self, "OK", f"Сохранено: {len(rows)}")
gui/import_dialog.py
from PyQt6.QtWidgets import (QDialog, QVBoxLayout, QHBoxLayout, QLabel,
                             QPushButton, QPlainTextEdit, QComboBox, QCheckBox,
                             QFileDialog, QMessageBox, QGroupBox, QFormLayout,
                             QDialogButtonBox, QLineEdit)
from pathlib import Path
from storage.importer import AccountImporter
from datetime import datetime

class ImportDialog(QDialog):
    def __init__(self, db, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.db = db
        self.imported_count = 0
        self.setWindowTitle("Импорт аккаунтов")
        self.resize(800, 700)

        v = QVBoxLayout(self)

        info = QLabel(
            "Поддерживаемые форматы (автоопределение разделителя `: | ;`):\n"
            "  • login:pass\n"
            "  • login:pass:email:emailpass\n"
            "  • login:pass:email:emailpass:ip:port[:proxyuser:proxypass]\n"
            "  • login:pass:email:emailpass:socks5:ip:port[:proxyuser:proxypass]\n"
            "  • login:pass:2FA_SECRET (base32)\n"
            "  • login:pass:ip:port  (без email)\n"
            "  • IAM API JSON  (полный дамп с cookies, user_agent, device_id)\n"
            "  • IAM экспорт (галки на скрине 12 — все принимаются)"
        )
        info.setStyleSheet("color:#8ab4ff;")
        v.addWidget(info)

        gb = QGroupBox("Вставь строки ниже или загрузи файл")
        fl = QVBoxLayout(gb)
        h = QHBoxLayout()
        self.btn_file = QPushButton("Загрузить из файла…")
        self.btn_paste = QPushButton("Вставить из буфера")
        h.addWidget(self.btn_file); h.addWidget(self.btn_paste); h.addStretch()
        fl.addLayout(h)

        self.text = QPlainTextEdit()
        self.text.setPlaceholderText("Одна запись на строку…")
        fl.addWidget(self.text, 1)
        v.addWidget(gb, 1)

        # опции
        default_name = f"batch_{datetime.now():%Y-%m-%d_%H-%M}"
        gb_batch = QGroupBox("Метка пачки (для сортировки в таблице)")
        fl_b = QFormLayout(gb_batch)
