        self.batch_name = QLineEdit(default_name)
        self.batch_name.setPlaceholderText("название пачки — увидишь в колонке 'Пачка'")
        fl_b.addRow("Имя пачки:", self.batch_name)
        v.addWidget(gb_batch)
        gb2 = QGroupBox("Опции")
        fl2 = QFormLayout(gb2)
        self.dedupe = QCheckBox("Пропускать дубликаты (по логину)"); self.dedupe.setChecked(True)
        fl2.addRow(self.dedupe)
        self.auto_proxy = QCheckBox("Привязать свободные прокси из пула к акккаунтам без прокси")
        self.auto_proxy.setChecked(True)
        fl2.addRow(self.auto_proxy)
        self.trust_device = QCheckBox(
            "Использовать device_id/user_agent из строки (если есть) — не генерировать заново")
        self.trust_device.setChecked(True)
        fl2.addRow(self.trust_device)
        v.addWidget(gb2)

        # buttons
        bb = QDialogButtonBox(QDialogButtonBox.StandardButton.Ok | QDialogButtonBox.StandardButton.Cancel)
        bb.button(QDialogButtonBox.StandardButton.Ok).setText("Импортировать")
        bb.accepted.connect(self._do_import)
        bb.rejected.connect(self.reject)
        v.addWidget(bb)

        self.btn_file.clicked.connect(self._load_file)
        self.btn_paste.clicked.connect(self._paste)

    def _load_file(self):
        p, _ = QFileDialog.getOpenFileName(self, "Файл аккаунтов",
                                            filter="Text/JSON (*.txt *.json *.csv);;All (*)")
        if p:
            self.text.setPlainText(Path(p).read_text("utf-8"))

    def _paste(self):
        from PyQt6.QtWidgets import QApplication
        self.text.setPlainText(QApplication.clipboard().text())

    def _do_import(self):
            from datetime import datetime as _dt
            from PyQt6.QtWidgets import QProgressDialog, QApplication
            from PyQt6.QtCore import Qt

            lines = self.text.toPlainText().splitlines()
            if not lines:
                return

            batch_name = self.batch_name.text().strip() or f"batch_{_dt.now():%Y-%m-%d_%H-%M}"
            existing = set(self.db.list_logins()) if self.dedupe.isChecked() else set()

            # ── 1. Парсим ВСЕ строки в память (быстро) ──
            progress = QProgressDialog("Парсинг строк...", "Отмена", 0, len(lines), self)
            progress.setWindowModality(Qt.WindowModality.WindowModal)
            progress.setMinimumDuration(500)

            parsed = []
            skipped = 0
            errors = 0
            error_details = []

            for i, line in enumerate(lines):
                if i % 100 == 0:
                    progress.setValue(i)
                    QApplication.processEvents()
                    if progress.wasCanceled():
                        return

                try:
                    acc = AccountImporter.parse_line(line)
                    if not acc or not acc.get("login") or not acc.get("password"):
                        continue
                    if acc["login"] in existing:
                        skipped += 1
                        continue
                    if not self.trust_device.isChecked():
                        acc.pop("user_agent", None)
                        acc.pop("device_id", None)
                        acc.pop("android_id", None)
                    acc["import_batch"] = batch_name
                    parsed.append(acc)
                    existing.add(acc["login"])
                except Exception as e:
                    errors += 1
                    error_details.append(
                        f"{type(e).__name__}: {str(e)[:100]} | line: {line[:80]}"
                    )

            progress.setValue(len(lines))

            # ── 2. Bulk-запись одной транзакцией (в 50-100 раз быстрее) ──
            if parsed:
                progress2 = QProgressDialog(f"Запись {len(parsed)} акков в БД...", None, 0, 0, self)
                progress2.setWindowModality(Qt.WindowModality.WindowModal)
                progress2.setMinimumDuration(300)
                QApplication.processEvents()

                try:
                    inserted, updated = self.db.bulk_upsert_accounts(parsed)
                except AttributeError:
                    # fallback если bulk метод ещё не добавлен
                    inserted = 0
                    updated = 0
                    for acc in parsed:
                        try:
                            self.db.upsert_account(acc)
                            inserted += 1
                        except Exception:
                            errors += 1
                except Exception as e:
                    QMessageBox.critical(self, "Ошибка", f"Bulk insert failed: {e}")
                    progress2.close()
                    return

                progress2.close()
                self.imported_count = inserted
            else:
                self.imported_count = 0

            if error_details:
                print("\n=== Import errors (first 5) ===")
                for err in error_details[:5]:
                    print(f"  {err}")

            QMessageBox.information(
                self, "Импорт завершён",
                f"Добавлено новых: {self.imported_count}\n"
                f"Пропущено (дубли): {skipped}\n"
                f"Ошибок парсинга: {errors}"
            )
            self.accept()
gui/ios_accounts_panel.py
"""
iOS-панель: отдельный интерфейс для iOS IAM аккаунтов.
Фичи:
  1. Просмотр только iOS акков (is_ios_iam=1 OR platform='ios')
  2. Импорт iOS IAM base64 формата
  3. Health check — проверить живость без залива
  4. A/B Analytics — какие настройки лучше сохраняют жизнь акка
  5. Быстрый запуск iOS-специфичной задачи с оптимальными параметрами
"""
from PyQt6.QtWidgets import (
    QWidget, QVBoxLayout, QHBoxLayout, QLabel, QTabWidget, QPushButton,
    QTableWidget, QTableWidgetItem, QHeaderView, QAbstractItemView,
    QLineEdit, QPlainTextEdit, QComboBox, QSpinBox, QFileDialog,
    QMessageBox, QGroupBox, QFormLayout, QCheckBox, QSplitter,
    QDialog, QDialogButtonBox, QProgressBar, QApplication
)
from PyQt6.QtCore import Qt, QTimer, pyqtSignal
from PyQt6.QtGui import QColor, QFont
from core.device import DeviceProfile, device_for

import asyncio
import json
import time
import warnings
from datetime import datetime
from pathlib import Path


# ═══════════════ helpers ═══════════════
def _get_loop() -> asyncio.AbstractEventLoop:
    """
    Возвращает qasync event loop.
    - Если мы внутри async — get_running_loop() (правильный способ)
    - Если снаружи (sync callback от кнопки) — fallback на get_event_loop(),
      подавляя DeprecationWarning в Python 3.12+ (в qasync-приложении loop
      уже установлен через asyncio.set_event_loop в main.py).
    """
    try:
        return asyncio.get_running_loop()
    except RuntimeError:
        with warnings.catch_warnings():
            warnings.simplefilter("ignore", DeprecationWarning)
            return asyncio.get_event_loop()


class _NumItem(QTableWidgetItem):
    def __init__(self, value):
        super().__init__()
        try:
            self.setData(Qt.ItemDataRole.DisplayRole, int(value))
        except Exception:
            self.setText(str(value))
        self.setTextAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignRight | Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignVCenter)
        self.setFlags(self.flags() ^ Qt.ItemFlag.ItemIsEditable)


class IOSAccountsPanel(QWidget):
    """
    Главный виджет — четыре подвкладки внутри.
    Требует MainWindow для запуска задач (доступ через self._get_mw()).
    """
    STATUS_COLORS = {
        "idle":            QColor(38, 40, 50),
        "running":         QColor(46, 92, 55),
        "success":         QColor(35, 100, 45),
        "error":           QColor(140, 45, 45),
        "session_expired": QColor(120, 90, 30),
        "checkpoint":      QColor(120, 90, 30),
        "rate_limited":    QColor(90, 90, 30),
        "dead":            QColor(60, 20, 20),
        "banned":          QColor(90, 25, 25),
        "suspended":       QColor(60, 20, 20),
    }

    def __init__(self, db):
        super().__init__()
        self.db = db
        v = QVBoxLayout(self)
        v.setContentsMargins(4, 4, 4, 4)

        # заголовок
        header = QLabel(
            "<b>iOS IAM Accounts</b> — отдельный пайплайн для акков с готовой "
            "session (Bearer + device fingerprint из IAM-дампа). "
            "НЕ перелогинивается при старте — использует существующий токен. "
            "Максимально похоже на настоящий iPhone на сетевом уровне."
        )
        header.setWordWrap(True)
        header.setStyleSheet("color:#8ab4ff; padding:6px; "
                             "background:#262832; border-radius:4px;")
        v.addWidget(header)

        self.tabs = QTabWidget()
        self.tabs.addTab(self._build_accounts_tab(),  "📱 Аккаунты")
        self.tabs.addTab(self._build_health_tab(),    "🩺 Health Check")
        self.tabs.addTab(self._build_analytics_tab(), "📊 A/B Analytics")
        self.tabs.addTab(self._build_quick_run_tab(), "🚀 Быстрый залив")
        v.addWidget(self.tabs)

        # первый rebuild
        QTimer.singleShot(200, self._reload_accounts)

    # ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    #                     Вкладка 1: Аккаунты
    # ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    def _build_accounts_tab(self) -> QWidget:

        self.lbl_count.setStyleSheet("font-weight:600;")
        h.addWidget(self.lbl_count)

        hf = QHBoxLayout()
        hf.addWidget(QLabel("🔍"))
        self.filter_text = QLineEdit()
        self.filter_text.setPlaceholderText("логин / прокси / статус…")

        hf.addWidget(QLabel("Статус:"))

        self.filter_status.addItems([
            "Все", "idle", "running", "success",

        self.tbl_accounts.setHorizontalHeaderLabels([
            "№", "Логин", "Модель iPhone", "iOS", "IG ver",
            "Локаль", "Прокси", "Статус", "Последняя ошибка", "Обновлено"
        ])
        h_hdr = self.tbl_accounts.horizontalHeader()
        h_hdr.setSectionResizeMode(QHeaderView.ResizeMode.Interactive)
        h_hdr.setStretchLastSection(True)
        self.tbl_accounts.setColumnWidth(0, 50)
        self.tbl_accounts.setColumnWidth(1, 200)
        self.tbl_accounts.setColumnWidth(2, 130)
        self.tbl_accounts.setColumnWidth(3, 70)
        self.tbl_accounts.setColumnWidth(4, 80)
        self.tbl_accounts.setColumnWidth(5, 70)
        self.tbl_accounts.setColumnWidth(6, 220)
        self.tbl_accounts.setColumnWidth(7, 120)
        self.tbl_accounts.setColumnWidth(8, 200)
        self.tbl_accounts.setAlternatingRowColors(True)
        self.tbl_accounts.setSelectionBehavior(
            QAbstractItemView.SelectionBehavior.SelectRows)
        self.tbl_accounts.setSelectionMode(
            QAbstractItemView.SelectionMode.ExtendedSelection)
        self.tbl_accounts.setSortingEnabled(True)
        v.addWidget(self.tbl_accounts, 1)

        # сигналы
        self.btn_import_ios.clicked.connect(self._open_import_ios)
        self.btn_reload.clicked.connect(self._reload_accounts)
        self.btn_export.clicked.connect(self._export_ios)
        self.btn_delete.clicked.connect(self._delete_selected)

        # дебаунс фильтра
        self._filter_timer = QTimer(self)
        self._filter_timer.setSingleShot(True)
        self._filter_timer.setInterval(250)
        self._filter_timer.timeout.connect(self._apply_filter)
        self.filter_text.textChanged.connect(lambda: self._filter_timer.start())
        self.filter_status.currentTextChanged.connect(self._apply_filter)

    def _reload_accounts(self):
        try:
            accounts = self.db.get_ios_accounts()
        except AttributeError:
            all_accs = self.db.list_accounts()
            accounts = [a for a in all_accs
                        if a.get("is_ios_iam") or a.get("platform") == "ios"]

        self.tbl_accounts.setUpdatesEnabled(False)
        was_sorted = self.tbl_accounts.isSortingEnabled()
        self.tbl_accounts.setSortingEnabled(False)
        try:
            self.tbl_accounts.setRowCount(0)
            for r, acc in enumerate(accounts):
                self.tbl_accounts.insertRow(r)
                status = (acc.get("status") or "idle").lower()
                color = self.STATUS_COLORS.get(status, self.STATUS_COLORS["idle"])

                cells = [
                    (0, _NumItem(r + 1)),
                    (1, QTableWidgetItem(acc.get("login", ""))),
                    (2, QTableWidgetItem(acc.get("model", ""))),
                    (3, QTableWidgetItem(acc.get("os_release", ""))),
                    (4, QTableWidgetItem(acc.get("ig_version", ""))),
                    (5, QTableWidgetItem(acc.get("locale", ""))),
                    (6, QTableWidgetItem(acc.get("proxy", "") or "")),
                    (7, QTableWidgetItem(f"● {status}")),
                    (8, QTableWidgetItem((acc.get("last_error") or "")[:200])),
                    (9, QTableWidgetItem(str(acc.get("updated_at") or "")[:19])),
                ]
                for col, it in cells:
                    if it:
                        it.setBackground(color)
                        self.tbl_accounts.setItem(r, col, it)
                if self.tbl_accounts.item(r, 6):
                    self.tbl_accounts.item(r, 6).setToolTip(acc.get("proxy") or "")
                if self.tbl_accounts.item(r, 8) and acc.get("last_error"):
                    self.tbl_accounts.item(r, 8).setToolTip(acc.get("last_error"))
        finally:
            self.tbl_accounts.setSortingEnabled(was_sorted)
            self.tbl_accounts.setUpdatesEnabled(True)

        self.lbl_count.setText(f"iOS акков: {len(accounts)}")
        self._apply_filter()

    def _apply_filter(self):
        text = self.filter_text.text().lower().strip()
        status_f = self.filter_status.currentText()

        if status_f.startswith("──"):
            self.filter_status.setCurrentIndex(0)
            return

        allowed = None
        if status_f == "живые (idle+success+running+session_expired)":
            allowed = {"idle", "success", "running", "session_expired"}
        elif status_f == "мёртвые (dead+banned+suspended)":
            allowed = {"dead", "banned", "suspended"}
        elif status_f != "Все":
            allowed = {status_f.lower()}

        for r in range(self.tbl_accounts.rowCount()):
            visible = True
            if text:
                haystack = "|".join(
                    (self.tbl_accounts.item(r, c).text().lower()
                     if self.tbl_accounts.item(r, c) else "")
                    for c in (1, 6, 7)
                )
                if text not in haystack:
                    visible = False
            if visible and allowed is not None:
                status_cell = self.tbl_accounts.item(r, 7)
                s = (status_cell.text() if status_cell else "").replace("●", "").strip().lower()
                if s not in allowed:
                    visible = False
            self.tbl_accounts.setRowHidden(r, not visible)

    def _delete_selected(self):
        rows = sorted({i.row() for i in self.tbl_accounts.selectedIndexes()},
                      reverse=True)
        if not rows:
            return
        reply = QMessageBox.question(
            self, "Удаление",
            f"Удалить {len(rows)} iOS акков навсегда?")
        if reply != QMessageBox.StandardButton.Yes:
            return
        logins = []
        for r in rows:
            it = self.tbl_accounts.item(r, 1)
            if it: logins.append(it.text())
        try:
            self.db.delete_accounts_bulk(logins)
        except AttributeError:
            for l in logins:
                self.db.delete_account(l)
        self._reload_accounts()
        self._reload_from_mainwindow()

    def _export_ios(self):
        rows = self.tbl_accounts.selectionModel().selectedRows()
        if not rows:
            rows = [self.tbl_accounts.model().index(r, 0)
                    for r in range(self.tbl_accounts.rowCount())
                    if not self.tbl_accounts.isRowHidden(r)]
        if not rows:
            QMessageBox.information(self, "Пусто", "Нет данных для экспорта")
            return
        path, _ = QFileDialog.getSaveFileName(
            self, "Экспорт iOS акков",
            f"ios_export_{datetime.now():%Y%m%d_%H%M}.json",
            "JSON (*.json)")
        if not path:
            return

        by_login = {a["login"]: a for a in self.db.get_ios_accounts()}
        exp = []
        for idx in rows:
            it = self.tbl_accounts.item(idx.row(), 1)
            if it and it.text() in by_login:
                exp.append(by_login[it.text()])
        Path(path).write_text(
            json.dumps(exp, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2, default=str), "utf-8")
        QMessageBox.information(self, "OK", f"Сохранено: {len(exp)} акков")

    # ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    #              Вкладка 2: Health Check
    # ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    def _build_health_tab(self) -> QWidget:
        w = QWidget()
        v = QVBoxLayout(w)

        info = QLabel(
            "<b>Health Check</b> — безопасная проверка живости iOS акков.<br>"
            "Дёргает ТОЛЬКО <code>/accounts/current_user/</code> — один запрос "
            "на акк, без warmup, без feed, без stats. Отделяет:<br>"
            "&nbsp;&nbsp;• <b>alive</b> — акк живой, session валидна<br>"
            "&nbsp;&nbsp;• <b>session_expired</b> — Bearer протух (нужен ре-импорт)<br>"
            "&nbsp;&nbsp;• <b>rate_limited</b> — 429, живой но занят<br>"
            "&nbsp;&nbsp;• <b>checkpoint</b> — challenge_required<br>"
            "&nbsp;&nbsp;• <b>dead</b> — реально suspended/disabled<br><br>"
            "Используй чтобы пересобрать статистику своих 18к после массовых "
            "ложных банов — 30-50% из dead могут оказаться живыми."
        )
        info.setWordWrap(True)
        info.setStyleSheet("color:#8ab4ff; background:#262832; "
                          "padding:8px; border-radius:4px;")
        v.addWidget(info)

        gb = QGroupBox("Параметры проверки")

            "Все iOS акки",

        self.hc_reset_on_alive = QCheckBox(
            "🔓 Если акк живой — сбросить статус в 'idle' (снять bogus dead)")
        self.hc_reset_on_alive.setChecked(True)
        fl.addRow(self.hc_reset_on_alive)

        v.addWidget(gb)

        h = QHBoxLayout()
        self.btn_hc_run = QPushButton("🩺 Запустить Health Check")
        self.btn_hc_run.setStyleSheet(
            "background:#2d5cff; padding:10px; font-weight:bold;")
        self.btn_hc_stop = QPushButton("⏹ Стоп")
        self.btn_hc_stop.setEnabled(False)
        h.addWidget(self.btn_hc_run)
        h.addWidget(self.btn_hc_stop)
        h.addStretch()
        v.addLayout(h)

        self.hc_progress = QProgressBar()
        v.addWidget(self.hc_progress)

        self.hc_stats_label = QLabel("")
        self.hc_stats_label.setStyleSheet(
            "background:#1e1f26; padding:10px; border-radius:4px; font-family:Consolas;")
        v.addWidget(self.hc_stats_label)

        self.hc_result_table = QTableWidget(0, 5)
        self.hc_result_table.setHorizontalHeaderLabels(
            ["Логин", "Было", "Стало", "Latency (мс)", "Детали"])
        self.hc_result_table.horizontalHeader().setStretchLastSection(True)
        self.hc_result_table.setColumnWidth(0, 200)
        self.hc_result_table.setColumnWidth(1, 120)
        self.hc_result_table.setColumnWidth(2, 120)
        self.hc_result_table.setColumnWidth(3, 100)
        self.hc_result_table.setAlternatingRowColors(True)
        v.addWidget(self.hc_result_table, 1)

        self._hc_task = None
        self.btn_hc_run.clicked.connect(self._run_health_check)
        self.btn_hc_stop.clicked.connect(self._stop_health_check)

        return w

    def _run_health_check(self):
        scope_idx = self.hc_scope.currentIndex()
        all_ios = self.db.get_ios_accounts()

        if scope_idx == 0:
            accs = all_ios
        elif scope_idx == 1:
            accs = [a for a in all_ios
                    if (a.get("status") or "").lower() in
                    ("dead", "banned", "suspended")]
        elif scope_idx == 2:
            accs = [a for a in all_ios
                    if (a.get("status") or "").lower() == "session_expired"]
        else:
            selected_logins = set()
            for i in self.tbl_accounts.selectionModel().selectedRows():
                it = self.tbl_accounts.item(i.row(), 1)
                if it: selected_logins.add(it.text())
            accs = [a for a in all_ios if a["login"] in selected_logins]

        if not accs:
            QMessageBox.information(self, "Пусто", "Нет акков под критерии")
            return

        without_proxy = [a for a in accs if not a.get("proxy")]
        if without_proxy:
            reply = QMessageBox.warning(
                self, "Без прокси",
                f"{len(without_proxy)} акков без прокси — health check без прокси "
                f"крайне не рекомендуется (свой IP спалит акки).\n\n"
                f"Yes — пропустить их, гонять только {len(accs) - len(without_proxy)}\n"
                f"No — отменить и назначить прокси в основной вкладке")
            if reply != QMessageBox.StandardButton.Yes:
                return
            skipped = [a["login"] for a in without_proxy]
            accs = [a for a in accs if a.get("proxy")]
            mw = self._get_mw()
            if mw:
                sample = ", ".join(skipped[:10])
                more = f" + ещё {len(skipped) - 10}" if len(skipped) > 10 else ""
                mw._log("warn",
                    f"[iOS HC] пропущено без прокси: {len(skipped)} — {sample}{more}")

        if not accs:
            return

        self.hc_result_table.setRowCount(0)
        self.hc_progress.setMaximum(len(accs))
        self.hc_progress.setValue(0)
        self.btn_hc_run.setEnabled(False)
        self.btn_hc_stop.setEnabled(True)

        loop = _get_loop()
        self._hc_task = loop.create_task(self._async_health_check(accs))

    def _stop_health_check(self):
        if self._hc_task and not self._hc_task.done():
            self._hc_task.cancel()

    async def _async_health_check(self, accounts):
        from core.session import IGSession
        from core.device import DeviceProfile
        from core.ig_client import (IGClient, SuspendedError, CheckpointError,
                                    SessionExpiredError, RateLimitError,
                                    TransientError, LoginError, TwoFactorRequired)

        sem = asyncio.Semaphore(self.hc_threads.value())
        counts = {"alive": 0, "session_expired": 0, "checkpoint": 0,
                  "rate_limited": 0, "dead": 0, "error": 0}
        revived = 0   # dead → alive

        async def check_one(acc):
            nonlocal revived
            async with sem:
                login = acc["login"]
                old_status = (acc.get("status") or "").lower()
                t0 = time.monotonic()
                new_status = "error"
                detail = ""

                try:
                    device = device_for(acc)
                    cookies = acc.get("cookies")
                    if not cookies and acc.get("cookies_json"):
                        try:
                            cookies = json.loads(acc["cookies_json"])
                        except Exception:
                            pass
                    sess = IGSession(device, acc["proxy"], cookies)
                    cli = IGClient(sess, acc)
                    try:
                        uid = await cli.login()
                        new_status = "alive"
                        detail = f"uid={uid}"
                        if old_status in ("dead", "banned", "suspended"):
                            revived += 1
                        if self.hc_reset_on_alive.isChecked():
                            self.db.mark_alive(login)
                    finally:
                        try: await sess.close()
                        except Exception: pass

                except SuspendedError as e:
                    new_status = "dead"
                    detail = str(e)[:100]
                    self.db.set_status(login, "dead", f"health_check: {detail}")
                except SessionExpiredError as e:
                    new_status = "session_expired"
                    detail = str(e)[:100]
                    self.db.set_status(login, "session_expired", f"health_check: {detail}")
                except CheckpointError as e:
                    new_status = "checkpoint"
                    detail = str(e.args[0] if e.args else e)[:100]
                    self.db.set_status(login, "checkpoint", f"health_check: {detail}")
                except RateLimitError as e:
                    new_status = "rate_limited"
                    detail = str(e)[:100]
                except (TransientError, LoginError, TwoFactorRequired) as e:
                    new_status = "error"
                    detail = f"{type(e).__name__}: {str(e)[:80]}"
                except Exception as e:
                    new_status = "error"
                    detail = f"{type(e).__name__}: {str(e)[:80]}"

                counts[new_status] = counts.get(new_status, 0) + 1
                latency_ms = int((time.monotonic() - t0) * 1000)
                self._hc_add_result_row(login, old_status, new_status, latency_ms, detail)

        try:
            await asyncio.gather(*[check_one(a) for a in accounts],
                                 return_exceptions=True)
        except asyncio.CancelledError:
            pass
        finally:
            self.btn_hc_run.setEnabled(True)
            self.btn_hc_stop.setEnabled(False)

            summary = " | ".join(f"{k}: {v}" for k, v in counts.items() if v > 0)
            self.hc_stats_label.setText(
                f"<b>Готово:</b> {sum(counts.values())} акков проверено<br>"
                f"<b>Результаты:</b> {summary}<br>"
                f"<b>Оживлено (bogus dead → alive):</b> <span style='color:#7bd88f;'>"
                f"{revived}</span>"
            )
            mw = self._get_mw()
            if mw:
                mw._log("info",
                    f"[iOS HC] завершено: {sum(counts.values())} акков, "
                    f"оживлено {revived}, {summary}")
            self._reload_accounts()
            self._reload_from_mainwindow()

    def _hc_add_result_row(self, login, old, new, latency_ms, detail):
        r = self.hc_result_table.rowCount()
        self.hc_result_table.insertRow(r)
        color_map = {
            "alive":            QColor(35, 100, 45),
            "dead":             QColor(140, 45, 45),
            "session_expired":  QColor(120, 90, 30),
            "checkpoint":       QColor(120, 90, 30),
            "rate_limited":     QColor(90, 90, 30),
            "error":            QColor(80, 50, 50),
        }
        new_color = color_map.get(new, QColor(38, 40, 50))

        items = [
            QTableWidgetItem(login),
            QTableWidgetItem(old or "—"),
            QTableWidgetItem(new),
            _NumItem(latency_ms),
            QTableWidgetItem(detail),
        ]
        for c, it in enumerate(items):
            if c == 2:
                it.setBackground(new_color)
            self.hc_result_table.setItem(r, c, it)
        self.hc_progress.setValue(self.hc_progress.value() + 1)

    # ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    #              Вкладка 3: A/B Analytics
    # ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    def _build_analytics_tab(self) -> QWidget:
        w = QWidget()
        v = QVBoxLayout(w)

        info = QLabel(
            "<b>A/B Analytics</b> — какие настройки продлевают жизнь акков.<br>"
            "Данные копятся автоматически при каждом запуске задачи. "
            "Задавай <b>variant</b> при запуске из главного окна (Ctrl+Shift+E) "
            "чтобы разделять эксперименты."
        )
        info.setWordWrap(True)
        info.setStyleSheet("color:#8ab4ff; background:#262832; padding:8px; "
                          "border-radius:4px;")
        v.addWidget(info)

        h = QHBoxLayout()
        h.addWidget(QLabel("Период:"))
        self.an_period = QComboBox()
        self.an_period.addItems([
            "Все время", "24 часа", "7 дней", "30 дней"])
        h.addWidget(self.an_period)
        h.addWidget(QLabel("Минимум образцов:"))
        self.an_min_samples = QSpinBox()
        self.an_min_samples.setRange(1, 10000); self.an_min_samples.setValue(10)
        h.addWidget(self.an_min_samples)
        self.btn_refresh_analytics = QPushButton("🔄 Обновить")
        h.addWidget(self.btn_refresh_analytics)
        h.addStretch()
        v.addLayout(h)

        splitter = QSplitter(Qt.Orientation.Vertical)

        gb_var = QGroupBox("Варианты (сортировано по survival rate ↓)")
        vv = QVBoxLayout(gb_var)
        self.tbl_variants = QTableWidget(0, 9)
        self.tbl_variants.setHorizontalHeaderLabels([
            "Variant", "Всего", "Success", "Dead", "Rate-limited",
            "Session exp.", "Survival %", "Success %", "Params (JSON)"
        ])
        self.tbl_variants.horizontalHeader().setStretchLastSection(True)
        for c, w_ in enumerate([120, 80, 80, 80, 100, 100, 100, 100]):
            self.tbl_variants.setColumnWidth(c, w_)
        self.tbl_variants.setAlternatingRowColors(True)
        self.tbl_variants.setSortingEnabled(True)
        vv.addWidget(self.tbl_variants)
        splitter.addWidget(gb_var)

        gb_meta = QGroupBox("Разрезы: iOS vs Android · Fresh vs Warm")
        vm = QVBoxLayout(gb_meta)
        self.tbl_platform = QTableWidget(0, 8)
        self.tbl_platform.setHorizontalHeaderLabels([
            "Разрез", "Всего", "Success", "Dead", "Error",
            "Rate-limited", "Survival %", "Success %"
        ])
        self.tbl_platform.horizontalHeader().setStretchLastSection(True)
        for c, w_ in enumerate([200, 80, 80, 80, 80, 100, 100]):
            self.tbl_platform.setColumnWidth(c, w_)
        self.tbl_platform.setAlternatingRowColors(True)
        vm.addWidget(self.tbl_platform)
        splitter.addWidget(gb_meta)

        splitter.setSizes([400, 200])
        v.addWidget(splitter, 1)

        self.an_recommendations = QPlainTextEdit()
        self.an_recommendations.setReadOnly(True)
        self.an_recommendations.setMaximumHeight(140)
        self.an_recommendations.setStyleSheet(
            "background:#1e1f26; font-family:Consolas; color:#7bd88f;")
        v.addWidget(self.an_recommendations)

        self.btn_refresh_analytics.clicked.connect(self._refresh_analytics)
        return w

    def _refresh_analytics(self):
        try:
            from core.experiment_tracker import ExperimentAnalyzer
            analyzer = ExperimentAnalyzer(self.db)
        except Exception as e:
            QMessageBox.warning(self, "!",
                f"experiment_tracker не подключён: {e}\n\n"
                f"Убедись что файл core/experiment_tracker.py существует.")
            return

        period_map = {0: None, 1: 24, 2: 168, 3: 720}
        hours = period_map[self.an_period.currentIndex()]

        variants = analyzer.variant_summary(since_hours=hours)
        variants = [v for v in variants if v["total"] >= self.an_min_samples.value()]

        was_sorted = self.tbl_variants.isSortingEnabled()
        self.tbl_variants.setSortingEnabled(False)
        self.tbl_variants.setRowCount(0)
        for v_row in variants:
            r = self.tbl_variants.rowCount()
            self.tbl_variants.insertRow(r)
            self.tbl_variants.setItem(r, 0, QTableWidgetItem(
                f"{v_row['variant']} / {v_row['params_hash'][:8]}"))
            self.tbl_variants.setItem(r, 1, _NumItem(v_row["total"]))
            self.tbl_variants.setItem(r, 2, _NumItem(v_row["success"]))
            self.tbl_variants.setItem(r, 3, _NumItem(v_row["dead"]))
            self.tbl_variants.setItem(r, 4, _NumItem(v_row["rate_limited"]))
            self.tbl_variants.setItem(r, 5, _NumItem(v_row["session_expired"]))

            sr_item = QTableWidgetItem(f"{v_row['survival_rate']*100:.1f}")
            sr_val = v_row['survival_rate']
            sr_item.setBackground(QColor(
                int(255 * (1 - sr_val)), int(255 * sr_val), 60))
            sr_item.setForeground(QColor("black" if sr_val > 0.5 else "white"))
            self.tbl_variants.setItem(r, 6, sr_item)

            self.tbl_variants.setItem(r, 7,
                QTableWidgetItem(f"{v_row['success_rate']*100:.1f}"))
            params_short = (v_row["params_json"] or "")[:150]
            pj = QTableWidgetItem(params_short)
            pj.setToolTip(v_row["params_json"] or "")
            self.tbl_variants.setItem(r, 8, pj)
        self.tbl_variants.setSortingEnabled(was_sorted)

        platform_data = analyzer.platform_split(since_hours=hours or 168)
        fresh_warm = analyzer.fresh_vs_warm(since_hours=hours or 168)

        self.tbl_platform.setRowCount(0)
        rows_data = []
        for plat, d in platform_data.items():
            rows_data.append((f"Platform: {plat}", d))
        for label, d in fresh_warm.items():
            rows_data.append((f"Fresh state: {label}", d))

        for label, d in rows_data:
            r = self.tbl_platform.rowCount()
            self.tbl_platform.insertRow(r)
            self.tbl_platform.setItem(r, 0, QTableWidgetItem(label))
            self.tbl_platform.setItem(r, 1, _NumItem(d.get("total", 0)))
            self.tbl_platform.setItem(r, 2, _NumItem(d.get("success", 0)))
            self.tbl_platform.setItem(r, 3, _NumItem(d.get("dead", 0)))
            self.tbl_platform.setItem(r, 4, _NumItem(d.get("error", 0)))
            self.tbl_platform.setItem(r, 5, _NumItem(d.get("rate_limited", 0)))
            self.tbl_platform.setItem(r, 6,
                QTableWidgetItem(f"{d.get('survival_rate', 0)*100:.1f}"))
            self.tbl_platform.setItem(r, 7,
                QTableWidgetItem(f"{d.get('success_rate', 0)*100:.1f}"))

        recs = []
        if variants:
            top = variants[0]
            bottom = variants[-1]
            if top["survival_rate"] - bottom["survival_rate"] > 0.15:
                recs.append(
                    f"✓ ТОП variant: {top['variant']} ({top['params_hash'][:8]}) — "
                    f"survival {top['survival_rate']*100:.1f}%, "
                    f"выборка {top['total']}")
                recs.append(
                    f"✗ ХУДШИЙ variant: {bottom['variant']} — "
                    f"survival {bottom['survival_rate']*100:.1f}%")
                recs.append(f"Разница: {(top['survival_rate']-bottom['survival_rate'])*100:.1f}%")
                recs.append("")
                recs.append(f"Топ params_json:\n  {top['params_json'][:400]}")

        if "ios" in platform_data and "android" in platform_data:
            ios_sr = platform_data["ios"]["survival_rate"]
            and_sr = platform_data["android"]["survival_rate"]
            if abs(ios_sr - and_sr) > 0.1:
                winner = "iOS" if ios_sr > and_sr else "Android"
                recs.append("")
                recs.append(f"✓ {winner} держится лучше: "
                           f"iOS {ios_sr*100:.1f}% vs Android {and_sr*100:.1f}%")

        if fresh_warm["fresh"]["total"] > 0 and fresh_warm["warm"]["total"] > 0:
            if fresh_warm["fresh"]["survival_rate"] < fresh_warm["warm"]["survival_rate"] - 0.15:
                recs.append("")
                recs.append(
                    f"⚠ Fresh акки мрут быстрее: "
                    f"{fresh_warm['fresh']['survival_rate']*100:.1f}% vs warm "
                    f"{fresh_warm['warm']['survival_rate']*100:.1f}% — "
                    f"увеличь fresh delay в orchestrator._adjust_params_for_fresh")

        if not recs:
            recs.append("Мало данных или разница между вариантами меньше значимой.")
            recs.append("Запусти несколько задач с разными variant в Ctrl+Shift+E.")

        self.an_recommendations.setPlainText("\n".join(recs))

    # ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    #              Вкладка 4: Быстрый залив (iOS-optimized)

    def _build_quick_run_tab(self) -> QWidget:
        w = QWidget()

            "<b>iOS Quick Upload</b> — предустановленные оптимальные параметры "

        self.qr_delay_min.setRange(0, 3600); self.qr_delay_min.setValue(60)

        hd = QHBoxLayout()
        hd.addWidget(self.qr_delay_min); hd.addWidget(QLabel("—"))

    def _pick_folder(self, line_edit):
        p = QFileDialog.getExistingDirectory(self, "Папка")
        if p:
            line_edit.setText(p)
            self._save_qr_settings()

    def _run_quick(self, only_selected: bool):
        mw = self._get_mw()
        if not mw:
            QMessageBox.warning(self, "!", "MainWindow не найден")
            return
        video_folder = self.qr_video_folder.text().strip()
        if not video_folder or not Path(video_folder).is_dir():
            QMessageBox.warning(self, "!", "Укажи существующую папку с видео")
            return

        all_ios = self.db.get_ios_accounts()

        if only_selected:
            sel_logins = set()
            for i in self.tbl_accounts.selectionModel().selectedRows():
                it = self.tbl_accounts.item(i.row(), 1)
                if it: sel_logins.add(it.text())
            accs = [a for a in all_ios if a["login"] in sel_logins]
        else:
            accs = [a for a in all_ios if (a.get("status") or "idle").lower()
                    in ("idle", "success")]

        if not accs:
            QMessageBox.information(self, "Пусто", "Нет подходящих iOS акков")
            return

        # ── прокси check ──
        without_proxy = [a for a in accs if not a.get("proxy")]
        if without_proxy:
            reply = QMessageBox.question(
                self, "Без прокси",
                f"{len(without_proxy)} акков без прокси. Привязать из пула?")
            if reply == QMessageBox.StandardButton.Yes:
                mw.proxy_panel.bind_free_to(accs)
                # обновляем прокси в acc-объектах из свежей БД
                fresh_by_login = {a["login"]: a for a in self.db.list_accounts()}
                for a in accs:
                    fresh = fresh_by_login.get(a["login"])
                    if fresh and fresh.get("proxy"):
                        a["proxy"] = fresh["proxy"]

            # ЯВНО логируем что отбрасываем без прокси
            still_no_proxy = [a for a in accs if not a.get("proxy")]
            if still_no_proxy:
                skipped_logins = [a["login"] for a in still_no_proxy]
                sample = ", ".join(skipped_logins[:10])
                more = f" + ещё {len(skipped_logins) - 10}" if len(skipped_logins) > 10 else ""
                mw._log("warn",
                    f"[iOS quick] пропущено {len(still_no_proxy)} акков без прокси: "
                    f"{sample}{more}")
                accs = [a for a in accs if a.get("proxy")]

        if not accs:
            QMessageBox.information(self, "Пусто",
                "После фильтрации прокси не осталось акков.")
            return

        # ── variant с точностью до минут ──
        variant = self.qr_variant.text().strip() or \
                  f"ios_quick_{datetime.now():%Y%m%d_%H%M}"

        params = {
            "video_folder":        video_folder,
            "cover_folder":        self.qr_cover_folder.text().strip(),
            "caption":             self.qr_caption.toPlainText(),
            "uniquify":            True,
            "uniquify_level":      "universal",
            "uniquify_audio":      True,
            "uniquify_covers":     self.qr_uniquify_covers.isChecked(),
            "uniquify_avatars":    True,
            "consume_covers":      self.qr_consume_covers.isChecked(),
            "share_to_feed":       False,
            "disable_comments":    False,
            "delay_min":           self.qr_delay_min.value(),
            "delay_max":           self.qr_delay_max.value(),
            "warmup":              "min",
            "retry":               1,
            "rotate_proxy_on_traffic": True,
            "rotate_proxy_on_ban": False,
            "ensure_public":       True,
            "first_setup_enable":  False,
            "first_setup_avatar":  False,
            "platform":            "ios",
            "skip_if_ever_set_bio": True,
        }

        # ── сброс собственного счётчика ──
        self._qr_done = 0
        self._qr_total = len(accs)
        self._qr_success = 0
        self._qr_error = 0
        self._qr_dead = 0
        self.qr_progress.setMaximum(self._qr_total)
        self.qr_progress.setValue(0)

        from core.orchestrator import Orchestrator
        idx_to_login = {i: a["login"] for i, a in enumerate(accs)}
        def _status_with_login(idx, status, err):
            login = idx_to_login.get(idx, f"?_{idx}")
            self._on_status_from_orch_with_login(login, status, err)

        orch = Orchestrator(
            threads=self.qr_threads.value(),
            on_status=_status_with_login,
            db=mw.db,
            settings=mw._collect_settings() if hasattr(mw, "_collect_settings") else {},
            stats_sink=None,
            geo=mw.geo if hasattr(mw, "geo") else None,
            experiment_variant=variant,
        )

        loop = _get_loop()
        task = loop.create_task(orch.run_batch(accs, "upload_reel", params))

        # регистрируем в mw чтобы Stop работал, но НЕ трогаем mw.progress
        info = {"task": task, "orch": orch, "action": "upload_reel_ios",
                "count": len(accs), "done": 0, "ios_panel": True}
        mw._running_tasks.append(info)
        task.add_done_callback(lambda t: self._on_qr_task_finished(mw, info, t))
        mw.act_stop.setEnabled(True)

        self.qr_status.setText(
            f"🚀 Запущено {len(accs)} iOS акков, variant='{variant}'. "
            f"Прогресс ниже. Stop работает через тулбар главного окна.")
        mw._log("info", f"[iOS quick] variant={variant}, {len(accs)} акков, "
                       f"threads={self.qr_threads.value()}")

    def _on_qr_task_finished(self, mw, info, task):
        """Колбэк завершения quick-задачи. Обновляет UI, снимает из mw._running_tasks."""
        try:
            task.result()
        except asyncio.CancelledError:
            mw._log("warn", "[iOS quick] задача остановлена")
        except Exception as e:
            mw._log("error", f"[iOS quick] задача упала: {e}")

        if info in mw._running_tasks:
            mw._running_tasks.remove(info)
        if not mw._running_tasks:
            mw.act_stop.setEnabled(False)

        # финальный отчёт
        self.qr_status.setText(
            f"✅ Готово. Всего: {self._qr_total}, "
            f"success: {self._qr_success}, error: {self._qr_error}, "
            f"dead: {self._qr_dead}. Обновляю таблицу iOS акков…"
        )
        self._reload_accounts()

    def _on_status_from_orch(self, idx, status, err):
        """
        Обработчик статусов от orchestrator. Обновляет собственный прогресс/счётчики
        и прокидывает в главный лог с корректным уровнем.
        Уровни: success | error | warn | banned | info
        """
        # финальные статусы бампают счётчик
        if status in ("success", "error", "dead", "banned", "rate_limited",
                      "checkpoint", "session_expired"):
            self._qr_done += 1
            if status == "success":
                self._qr_success += 1
            elif status in ("dead", "banned", "suspended"):
                self._qr_dead += 1
            elif status == "error":
                self._qr_error += 1
            self.qr_progress.setValue(self._qr_done)

        mw = self._get_mw()
        if not mw:
            return
        try:
            # маппинг: dead — визуально красный, но по логическому уровню это error
            # (у LogViewer есть уровень 'banned' — резервируем ТОЛЬКО для реальных банов
            # чтобы не смешивать с dead из health_check)
            level_map = {
                "success":         "success",
                "error":           "error",
                "banned":          "banned",
                "dead":            "error",     # с префиксом [DEAD] в тексте
                "suspended":       "error",
                "session_expired": "warn",
                "checkpoint":      "warn",
                "rate_limited":    "warn",
                "running":         "info",
            }
            level = level_map.get(status, "info")
            prefix = ""
            if status == "dead":
                prefix = "[DEAD] "
            elif status == "suspended":
                prefix = "[SUSPENDED] "
            elif status == "session_expired":
                prefix = "[SESSION_EXPIRED] "

            msg = f"[iOS] #{idx} → {prefix}{status}"
            if err:
                msg += f" | {err[:150]}"
            mw.log_signal.emit(level, msg)
        except Exception:
            pass

    # ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    #                    UTILS
    # ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    def _open_import_ios(self):
        dlg = _IOSImportDialog(self.db, self)
        if dlg.exec():
            self._reload_accounts()
            self._reload_from_mainwindow()
    def _on_status_from_orch_with_login(self, login: str, status: str, err: str):
        # обновляем счётчики как раньше
        if status in ("success", "error", "dead", "banned", "rate_limited",
                      "checkpoint", "session_expired"):
            self._qr_done += 1
            if status == "success": self._qr_success += 1
            elif status in ("dead", "banned", "suspended"): self._qr_dead += 1
            elif status == "error": self._qr_error += 1
            self.qr_progress.setValue(self._qr_done)

        mw = self._get_mw()
        if not mw: return
        level_map = {"success": "success", "error": "error",
                     "banned": "banned", "dead": "error",
                     "suspended": "error", "session_expired": "warn",
                     "checkpoint": "warn", "rate_limited": "warn",
                     "running": "info"}
        level = level_map.get(status, "info")
        prefix = ""
        if status in ("dead", "suspended", "banned"):
            prefix = f"[{status.upper()}] "
        elif status == "session_expired":
            prefix = "[SESSION_EXPIRED] "
        msg = f"[iOS] {login:30s} → {prefix}{status}"
        if err: msg += f" | {err[:150]}"
        try: mw.log_signal.emit(level, msg)
        except Exception: pass
    def _get_mw(self):
        p = self.parent()
        while p is not None:
            if hasattr(p, "proxy_panel") and hasattr(p, "db") and hasattr(p, "table"):
                return p
            p = p.parent()
        return None

    def _reload_from_mainwindow(self):
        mw = self._get_mw()
        if mw and hasattr(mw, "_load_accounts_from_db"):
            mw._load_accounts_from_db()

    def _cfg_path(self):
        p = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent / "data" / "ios_quick_settings.json"
        p.parent.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
        return p

    def _save_qr_settings(self):
        try:
            self._cfg_path().write_text(json.dumps({
                "video_folder":    self.qr_video_folder.text(),
                "cover_folder":    self.qr_cover_folder.text(),
                "caption":         self.qr_caption.toPlainText(),
                "variant":         self.qr_variant.text(),
                "threads":         self.qr_threads.value(),
                "delay_min":       self.qr_delay_min.value(),
                "delay_max":       self.qr_delay_max.value(),
                "uniquify_covers": self.qr_uniquify_covers.isChecked(),
                "consume_covers":  self.qr_consume_covers.isChecked(),
            }, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), "utf-8")
        except Exception:
            pass

    def _load_qr_settings(self):
        p = self._cfg_path()
        if not p.exists(): return
        try:
            cfg = json.loads(p.read_text("utf-8"))
        except Exception:
            return
        self.qr_video_folder.setText(cfg.get("video_folder", ""))
        self.qr_cover_folder.setText(cfg.get("cover_folder", ""))
        self.qr_caption.setPlainText(cfg.get("caption", ""))
        self.qr_variant.setText(cfg.get("variant", ""))
        self.qr_threads.setValue(cfg.get("threads", 20))
        self.qr_delay_min.setValue(cfg.get("delay_min", 60))
        self.qr_delay_max.setValue(cfg.get("delay_max", 180))
        self.qr_uniquify_covers.setChecked(cfg.get("uniquify_covers", True))
        self.qr_consume_covers.setChecked(cfg.get("consume_covers", True))


# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

class _IOSImportDialog(QDialog):
    def __init__(self, db, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.db = db
        self.imported_count = 0
        self.setWindowTitle("Импорт iOS IAM аккаунтов")
        self.resize(750, 600)
        v = QVBoxLayout(self)

        info = QLabel(
            "<b>Формат iOS IAM:</b><br>"
            "<code>login:password:TOTP_BASE32:base64_url_safe_JSON</code><br><br>"
            "JSON внутри должен содержать: username, password, session (authorization, "
            "ds_user_id, mid), device (guid, phone_id, ...), app (app_version), "
            "ua_profile (device_model, os_version, locale, tz_name, resolution).<br><br>"
            "Пример начала строки:<br>"
            "<code>milo.jones.164636:3NGXy13q7g-nL:HVVSYBEP5RDKSWXBN7GCNMLTT7YUKZOQ:eyJzYXZlZF9hdCI6...</code>"
        )
        info.setWordWrap(True)
        info.setStyleSheet("color:#8ab4ff; padding:6px;")
        v.addWidget(info)

        h = QHBoxLayout()
        self.btn_file = QPushButton("Загрузить из файла…")
        self.btn_paste = QPushButton("Вставить из буфера")
        h.addWidget(self.btn_file); h.addWidget(self.btn_paste); h.addStretch()
        v.addLayout(h)

        self.text = QPlainTextEdit()
        self.text.setPlaceholderText("Одна iOS IAM запись на строку…")
        self.text.setFont(QFont("Consolas", 9))
        v.addWidget(self.text, 1)

        gb = QGroupBox("Метка пачки")
        fl = QFormLayout(gb)
        self.batch_name = QLineEdit(f"ios_iam_{datetime.now():%Y-%m-%d_%H-%M}")
        fl.addRow("Имя пачки:", self.batch_name)
        v.addWidget(gb)

        self.dedupe = QCheckBox("Пропускать дубликаты (по логину)")
        self.dedupe.setChecked(True)
        v.addWidget(self.dedupe)

        self.overwrite_session = QCheckBox(
            "Перезаписать session у существующих (если уже был в базе — обновить Bearer/cookies)")
        self.overwrite_session.setChecked(True)
        v.addWidget(self.overwrite_session)

        bb = QDialogButtonBox(QDialogButtonBox.StandardButton.Ok |
                              QDialogButtonBox.StandardButton.Cancel)
        bb.button(QDialogButtonBox.StandardButton.Ok).setText("Импортировать")
        bb.accepted.connect(self._do_import)
        bb.rejected.connect(self.reject)
        v.addWidget(bb)

        self.btn_file.clicked.connect(self._load_file)
        self.btn_paste.clicked.connect(self._paste)

    def _load_file(self):
        p, _ = QFileDialog.getOpenFileName(self, "iOS IAM файл",
                                            filter="Text (*.txt);;All (*)")
        if p:
            self.text.setPlainText(Path(p).read_text("utf-8"))

    def _paste(self):
        self.text.setPlainText(QApplication.clipboard().text())

    def _do_import(self):
        from storage.ios_importer import IOSAccountImporter

        lines = self.text.toPlainText().splitlines()
        if not lines:
            return

        batch = self.batch_name.text().strip() or f"ios_iam_{datetime.now():%Y-%m-%d_%H-%M}"
        existing = set(self.db.list_logins())

        parsed, dup, errors = [], 0, 0
        for line in lines:
            line = line.strip()
            if not line or line.startswith("#"): continue
            if not IOSAccountImporter.detect(line):
                errors += 1
                continue
            acc = IOSAccountImporter.parse_line(line)
            if not acc or not acc.get("login") or not acc.get("password"):
                errors += 1
                continue
            if acc["login"] in existing and self.dedupe.isChecked():
                if not self.overwrite_session.isChecked():
                    dup += 1
                    continue
                # даже с dedupe — обновляем session/cookies
            acc["import_batch"] = batch
            parsed.append(acc)
            existing.add(acc["login"])

        if parsed:
            try:
                ins, upd = self.db.bulk_upsert_accounts(parsed)
                self.imported_count = ins
            except AttributeError:
                for a in parsed:
                    try:
                        self.db.upsert_account(a)
                    except Exception:
                        errors += 1
                self.imported_count = len(parsed) - errors

        QMessageBox.information(
            self, "Импорт iOS IAM",
            f"Добавлено: {self.imported_count}\n"
            f"Пропущено дубликатов: {dup}\n"
            f"Ошибок парсинга: {errors}\n\n"
            f"Все акки помечены is_ios_iam=1 — будут использовать существующую "
            f"session и НЕ будут перелогиниваться при старте.")
        self.accept()
gui/log_viewer.py
from PyQt6.QtWidgets import (QWidget, QVBoxLayout, QHBoxLayout, QPlainTextEdit,
                             QPushButton, QComboBox, QLabel, QCheckBox, QLineEdit)
from PyQt6.QtGui import QColor, QTextCharFormat, QFont, QTextCursor
from PyQt6.QtCore import Qt, QTimer
from datetime import datetime

LEVEL_COLORS = {
    "info":    QColor("#a0c0ff"),
    "warn":    QColor("#ffb84d"),
    "error":   QColor("#ff6b6b"),
    "success": QColor("#7bd88f"),
    "banned":  QColor("#ff4a4a"),
    "debug":   QColor("#7a7d88"),
}


class LogViewer(QWidget):
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__()
        v = QVBoxLayout(self)
        v.setContentsMargins(0, 4, 0, 0)

        # хранилище всех строк для фильтрации
        self._all_lines: list[tuple[str, str, str]] = []  # (ts, level, msg)
        self._max_lines = 50_000

        bar = QHBoxLayout()
        bar.addWidget(QLabel("Лог:"))
        self.filter = QComboBox()
        self.filter.addItems(["Всё", "info", "success", "warn", "error", "banned", "debug"])
        bar.addWidget(self.filter)

        self.search = QLineEdit()
        self.search.setPlaceholderText("Поиск в логе (текст или regex)…")
        bar.addWidget(self.search, 1)

        self.autoscroll = QCheckBox("Автоскролл")
        self.autoscroll.setChecked(True)
        bar.addWidget(self.autoscroll)

        self.btn_clear = QPushButton("Очистить")
        bar.addWidget(self.btn_clear)

        self.btn_save = QPushButton("Сохранить в файл")
        bar.addWidget(self.btn_save)
        v.addLayout(bar)

        self.view = QPlainTextEdit()
        self.view.setReadOnly(True)
        self.view.setMaximumBlockCount(self._max_lines)
        f = QFont("Consolas", 9)
        self.view.setFont(f)
        v.addWidget(self.view)

        # ── debounce для поиска ──
        self._search_timer = QTimer(self)
        self._search_timer.setSingleShot(True)
        self._search_timer.setInterval(300)
        self._search_timer.timeout.connect(self._apply_filter)

        # сигналы
        self.btn_clear.clicked.connect(self._clear_all)
        self.btn_save.clicked.connect(self._save)
        self.filter.currentTextChanged.connect(self._apply_filter)
        self.search.textChanged.connect(lambda: self._search_timer.start())

    def append_line(self, level: str, msg: str):
        """Добавляет строку в лог, применяет фильтры."""
        ts = datetime.now().strftime("%H:%M:%S.%f")[:-3]
        self._all_lines.append((ts, level, msg))

        # ограничиваем размер хранилища
        if len(self._all_lines) > self._max_lines:
            self._all_lines = self._all_lines[-self._max_lines:]

        # проверяем проходит ли новая строка текущий фильтр
        if not self._line_matches(level, msg):
            return

        self._write_to_view(ts, level, msg)

    def _line_matches(self, level: str, msg: str) -> bool:
        """Проверяет проходит ли строка текущие фильтры."""
        # фильтр по уровню
        level_f = self.filter.currentText()
        if level_f != "Всё" and level != level_f:
            return False

        # фильтр по тексту
        search_txt = self.search.text().strip().lower()
        if search_txt and search_txt not in msg.lower() and search_txt not in level.lower():
            return False

        return True

    def _write_to_view(self, ts: str, level: str, msg: str):
        """Пишет одну строку в view (без изменения хранилища)."""
        color = LEVEL_COLORS.get(level, QColor("#e5e5e5"))
        sb = self.view.verticalScrollBar()
        scroll_pos = sb.value()

        cur = QTextCursor(self.view.document())
        cur.movePosition(QTextCursor.MoveOperation.End)
        fmt = QTextCharFormat()
        fmt.setForeground(color)
        cur.setCharFormat(fmt)
        cur.insertText(f"[{ts}] {level.upper():7} {msg}\n")

        if self.autoscroll.isChecked():
            sb.setValue(sb.maximum())
        else:
            sb.setValue(scroll_pos)

    def _apply_filter(self):
        """Перерисовывает view применяя фильтры к хранилищу."""
        self.view.setUpdatesEnabled(False)
        try:
            self.view.clear()
            for ts, level, msg in self._all_lines:
                if self._line_matches(level, msg):
                    self._write_to_view(ts, level, msg)
        finally:
            self.view.setUpdatesEnabled(True)

    def _clear_all(self):
        """Очищает view И хранилище."""
        self._all_lines.clear()
        self.view.clear()

    def append_status(self, row: int, login: str, status: str, err: str = ""):
        level = {"running": "info", "success": "success",
                 "error": "error", "banned": "banned"}.get(status, "info")
        msg = f"#{row+1:04d}  {login:30s}  → {status}"
        if err:
            msg += f"   [{err}]"
        self.append_line(level, msg)

    def _save(self):
        from PyQt6.QtWidgets import QFileDialog
        from pathlib import Path
        p, _ = QFileDialog.getSaveFileName(
            self, "Лог",
            f"log_{datetime.now():%Y%m%d_%H%M}.txt", "*.txt"
        )
        if p:
            # сохраняем ВСЁ хранилище, не отфильтрованное
            with open(p, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                for ts, level, msg in self._all_lines:
                    f.write(f"[{ts}] {level.upper():7} {msg}\n")

    def _wrap_scroll(self, widget) -> QScrollArea:
        """Оборачивает панель в QScrollArea с вертикальным скроллом."""
        sa = QScrollArea()
        sa.setWidget(widget)
        sa.setWidgetResizable(True)
        sa.setHorizontalScrollBarPolicy(Qt.ScrollBarPolicy.ScrollBarAlwaysOff)
        sa.setVerticalScrollBarPolicy(Qt.ScrollBarPolicy.ScrollBarAsNeeded)
        sa.setFrameShape(QScrollArea.Shape.NoFrame)
        return sa
    def _apply_smart_geometry(self):
        """Адаптивный размер под доступную область экрана с учётом DPI."""
        from PyQt6.QtGui import QGuiApplication
        screen = QGuiApplication.primaryScreen()
        avail = screen.availableGeometry()          # без панели задач
        # берём 90% доступной области, но не больше 1600x950
        w = min(1600, int(avail.width() * 0.90))
        h = min(950, int(avail.height() * 0.90))
        # минимальный — с запасом чтобы не вываливалось
        min_w = min(1100, avail.width() - 40)
        min_h = min(700, avail.height() - 80)
        self.setMinimumSize(min_w, min_h)
        self.resize(w, h)
        # центрируем
        self.move(
            avail.x() + (avail.width() - w) // 2,
            avail.y() + (avail.height() - h) // 2,
        )
    def _build_toolbar(self):
        tb = QToolBar()
        tb.setMovable(False)
        tb.setIconSize(tb.iconSize() * 1)
        self.act_start = QAction("▶  Старт", self); self.act_start.setShortcut("F5")
        self.act_stop = QAction("■  Стоп", self);  self.act_stop.setShortcut("F6")

        tb.addWidget(QLabel(" Потоков: "))
        self.threads_spin = QSpinBox()
        self.threads_spin.setRange(1, 500)
        self.threads_spin.setValue(70)
        self.threads_spin.setMinimumWidth(80)
        tb.addWidget(self.threads_spin)

        tb.addWidget(QLabel("  Платформа: "))
        self.platform_combo = QComboBox()
        self.platform_combo.addItems(["mix (рандом)", "только Android", "только iOS"])
        self.platform_combo.setMinimumWidth(150)
        tb.addWidget(self.platform_combo)

        self.cooldown_enable = QCheckBox("пропускать если задача была")

            "Работает per-action: 'upload_reel' и 'set_bio' считаются "
            "независимо."
        )

        tb.addSeparator()

        self.btn_del_bad = QPushButton("🗑 Удалить banned+dead+suspended")
        self.btn_del_bad.setStyleSheet("background:#8c2d2d; color:white;")

        self.btn_del_err = QPushButton("🗑 Удалить error")
        self.btn_del_err.setStyleSheet("background:#8c6b2d; color:white;")
        self.btn_del_err.clicked.connect(self._delete_errors)
        tb.addWidget(self.btn_del_err)

        self.addToolBar(tb)

    def _build_statusbar(self):
        sb = QStatusBar()
        self.setStatusBar(sb)
