            self.tbl_views_details.setItem(r, 6, QTableWidgetItem(res.get("cover_filename", "") or ""))
            code = res.get("code", "")
            url = f"https://instagram.com/reel/{code}" if code else ""
            link_it = QTableWidgetItem(url)
            link_it.setForeground(QColor("#8ab4ff"))
            link_it.setToolTip("Правой кнопкой → Copy link")
            self.tbl_views_details.setItem(r, 7, link_it)

        self.tbl_views_details.setSortingEnabled(was_sorted)
        self.tbl_views_details.sortItems(3, Qt.SortOrder.DescendingOrder)

        # ─── сводка по группам ───
        from collections import defaultdict
        import statistics

        by_group = defaultdict(lambda: {
            "preset": "?", "views": [], "likes": [], "comments": [],
            "alive": 0, "total_uploads": 0, "dead_uploads": 0,
        })

        for res in results:
            g = res.get("group_letter", "?")
            d = by_group[g]
            d["preset"] = res.get("preset", "?")
            d["total_uploads"] += 1
            if not res.get("fetch_ok"):
                d["dead_uploads"] += 1
                continue
            d["alive"] += 1
            d["views"].append(int(res.get("play_count", 0)))
            d["likes"].append(int(res.get("like_count", 0)))
            d["comments"].append(int(res.get("comment_count", 0)))

        was_sorted = self.tbl_views_summary.isSortingEnabled()
        self.tbl_views_summary.setSortingEnabled(False)
        self.tbl_views_summary.setRowCount(0)

        for letter in sorted(by_group.keys()):
            d = by_group[letter]
            r = self.tbl_views_summary.rowCount()
            self.tbl_views_summary.insertRow(r)

            self.tbl_views_summary.setItem(r, 0, QTableWidgetItem(letter))
            self.tbl_views_summary.setItem(r, 1, QTableWidgetItem(d["preset"]))
            self.tbl_views_summary.setItem(r, 2, _NumItem(d["total_uploads"]))

            alive_pct = d["alive"] / max(d["total_uploads"], 1) * 100
            alive_it = QTableWidgetItem(f"{alive_pct:.0f}")
            sr = alive_pct / 100.0
            alive_it.setBackground(QColor(int(255*(1-sr)), int(255*sr), 60))
            alive_it.setForeground(QColor("black" if sr > 0.5 else "white"))
            self.tbl_views_summary.setItem(r, 3, alive_it)

            if d["views"]:
                total_v = sum(d["views"])
                median_v = int(statistics.median(d["views"]))
                avg_v = int(statistics.mean(d["views"]))
                max_v = max(d["views"])
                total_l = sum(d["likes"])
                total_c = sum(d["comments"])
            else:
                total_v = median_v = avg_v = max_v = total_l = total_c = 0

            self.tbl_views_summary.setItem(r, 4, _NumItem(total_v))
            self.tbl_views_summary.setItem(r, 5, _NumItem(median_v))
            self.tbl_views_summary.setItem(r, 6, _NumItem(avg_v))
            self.tbl_views_summary.setItem(r, 7, _NumItem(max_v))
            self.tbl_views_summary.setItem(r, 8, _NumItem(total_l))
            self.tbl_views_summary.setItem(r, 9, _NumItem(total_c))

        self.tbl_views_summary.setSortingEnabled(was_sorted)
        self.tbl_views_summary.sortItems(5, Qt.SortOrder.DescendingOrder)

        # ─── ВЕРДИКТ ───
        verdict = []
        verdict.append(f"═══ VIEWS REPORT: {self.vr_test_id.currentText()} ═══")
        verdict.append("")

        if not by_group:
            verdict.append("Нет данных для анализа.")
            self.vr_verdict.setPlainText("\n".join(verdict))
            return

        # ранжируем по median views (главная метрика)
        ranked = sorted(by_group.items(),
                       key=lambda x: -(statistics.median(x[1]["views"]) if x[1]["views"] else 0))

        for i, (letter, d) in enumerate(ranked):
            median_v = int(statistics.median(d["views"])) if d["views"] else 0
            avg_v = int(statistics.mean(d["views"])) if d["views"] else 0
            total_v = sum(d["views"])
            alive_pct = d["alive"] / max(d["total_uploads"], 1) * 100

            rank_icon = "🥇" if i == 0 else "🥈" if i == 1 else "🥉" if i == 2 else "  "
            verdict.append(
                f"{rank_icon} Группа {letter} ({d['preset']}): "
                f"median={median_v:,} avg={avg_v:,} total={total_v:,} "
                f"| alive: {d['alive']}/{d['total_uploads']} ({alive_pct:.0f}%)"
            )

        verdict.append("")

        # Комбинированный score = median_views * alive_pct
        # Чтобы группа с 10 просмотрами и 100% survival не выигрывала
        # у группы с 1000 просмотрами и 50% survival.
        scored = []
        for letter, d in by_group.items():
            median_v = statistics.median(d["views"]) if d["views"] else 0
            alive_pct = d["alive"] / max(d["total_uploads"], 1)
            score = median_v * alive_pct
            scored.append((letter, d["preset"], score, median_v, alive_pct))
        scored.sort(key=lambda x: -x[2])

        if len(scored) >= 2:
            best_l, best_p, best_s, best_m, best_a = scored[0]
            worst_l, worst_p, worst_s, worst_m, worst_a = scored[-1]

            verdict.append(f"🏆 ЛУЧШИЙ combined score: группа {best_l} ({best_p})")
            verdict.append(f"   score = median × survival = {int(best_m):,} × {best_a*100:.0f}% = {int(best_s):,}")
            verdict.append("")
            if best_s > worst_s * 2:
                verdict.append(f"✓ Разница ×{best_s / max(worst_s, 1):.1f} — используй '{best_p}' как основной пресет.")
            else:
                verdict.append(f"~ Разница небольшая ({(best_s / max(worst_s, 1)):.1f}x) — повтори тест на большей выборке.")

            # особые случаи
            total_median = sum(statistics.median(d["views"]) if d["views"] else 0
                              for d in by_group.values()) / len(by_group)
            if total_median < 100:
                verdict.append("")
                verdict.append("⚠ Median по всем группам < 100 просмотров — "
                              "акки в shadow ban на distribution. Причины:")
                verdict.append("  • Свежие акки без прогрева (первые 3-7 дней рилсы душат)")
                verdict.append("  • Прокси спалилась (IG понижает reach из этой подсети)")
                verdict.append("  • Однотипный контент (много одинаковых видео за короткое время)")

        self.vr_verdict.setPlainText("\n".join(verdict))
storage/__init__.py

storage/db.py
"""SQLite слой с полной схемой + миграция + универсальный upsert."""
import sqlite3
import json
import threading
from pathlib import Path
from contextlib import contextmanager


SCHEMA = """
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS accounts (
    login TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    sort_order INTEGER,
    import_batch TEXT,
    password TEXT NOT NULL,
    email TEXT, email_password TEXT,
    proxy TEXT, proxy_type TEXT,
    totp_secret TEXT,
    cookies_json TEXT,

    ig_version TEXT, ig_version_code TEXT,
    bloks_version TEXT, ig_app_id TEXT,

    platform TEXT,
    os_release TEXT, os_api TEXT,

    manufacturer TEXT, brand TEXT,
    model TEXT, device_name TEXT,
    board TEXT, hardware TEXT, cpu TEXT, gpu TEXT,
    chip_commercial TEXT,
    supported_abis TEXT,
    radio_version TEXT,

    resolution TEXT, dpi TEXT, scale TEXT,

    build_id TEXT, build_incremental TEXT,
    build_fingerprint TEXT, security_patch TEXT,

    locale TEXT, language TEXT, country TEXT,
    timezone_offset INTEGER,
    carrier_name TEXT, carrier_mcc TEXT, carrier_mnc TEXT,

    android_id TEXT, device_id TEXT,
    family_device_id TEXT, phone_id TEXT, advertising_id TEXT,
    bio_hash TEXT,
    bio_updated_at TIMESTAMP,

    user_agent TEXT,

    status TEXT DEFAULT 'idle',
    last_error TEXT,
    total_views INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    total_likes INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    followers INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    following INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    updated_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_status ON accounts(status);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_batch ON accounts(import_batch);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS experiments (
    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    login TEXT NOT NULL,
    action TEXT NOT NULL,
    outcome TEXT NOT NULL,          -- success | dead | error | rate_limited | checkpoint | session_expired
    err_type TEXT,
    variant TEXT DEFAULT 'default',
    platform TEXT,
    is_fresh INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    params_hash TEXT,
    params_json TEXT,
    logged_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_exp_variant ON experiments(variant, outcome);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_exp_login   ON experiments(login, logged_at DESC);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_exp_time    ON experiments(logged_at DESC);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_exp_hash    ON experiments(params_hash, outcome);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS proxies (
    proxy TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    type TEXT, geo TEXT,
    status TEXT DEFAULT 'unknown',
    latency INTEGER DEFAULT -1,
    bound_to TEXT,
    checked_at TIMESTAMP
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS reels_uploads (
    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    login TEXT, media_id TEXT, code TEXT,
    cover_filename TEXT,
    caption_hash TEXT,
    caption_preview TEXT,
    uploaded_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    views INTEGER DEFAULT 0, likes INTEGER DEFAULT 0
);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_uploads_cover ON reels_uploads(cover_filename);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_uploads_login ON reels_uploads(login);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS task_history (
    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    login  TEXT    NOT NULL,
    action TEXT    NOT NULL,
    status TEXT,
    ran_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_task_history_lookup
    ON task_history(login, action, ran_at DESC);
"""


class DB:
    def __init__(self, path: str):
        Path(path).parent.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
        self._lock = threading.RLock()
        self.conn = sqlite3.connect(path, check_same_thread=False, isolation_level=None)
        self.conn.execute("PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL")
        self.conn.execute("PRAGMA synchronous=NORMAL")
        self.conn.execute("PRAGMA cache_size=-64000")
        self.conn.execute("PRAGMA temp_store=MEMORY")
        self.conn.execute("PRAGMA mmap_size=268435456")
        self.conn.execute("PRAGMA busy_timeout=30000")
        self.conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
        with self._lock:
            self.conn.executescript(SCHEMA)
        self._migrate()

    def _migrate(self):
        """Догоняет колонки если БД старая."""
        # ── accounts ──
        with self._cursor() as c:
            cols = {r[1] for r in c.execute("PRAGMA table_info(accounts)")}
        new_cols = {
            "ig_version": "TEXT", "ig_version_code": "TEXT",
            "bloks_version": "TEXT", "ig_app_id": "TEXT",
            "os_release": "TEXT", "os_api": "TEXT",
            "manufacturer": "TEXT", "brand": "TEXT",
            "model": "TEXT", "device_name": "TEXT",
            "board": "TEXT", "hardware": "TEXT",
            "cpu": "TEXT", "gpu": "TEXT",
            "chip_commercial": "TEXT",
            "first_setup_done": "INTEGER DEFAULT 0",
            "is_ios_iam":       "INTEGER DEFAULT 0",
            "import_source":    "TEXT",
            "session_expired_at": "TIMESTAMP",
            "last_alive_check": "TIMESTAMP",
            "avatar_hash": "TEXT",
            "avatar_updated_at": "TIMESTAMP",
            "is_public_verified": "INTEGER DEFAULT 0",
            "reels_count": "INTEGER DEFAULT 0",
            "avg_views_per_reel": "REAL DEFAULT 0",
            "stats_updated_at": "TIMESTAMP",
            "supported_abis": "TEXT", "radio_version": "TEXT",
            "resolution": "TEXT", "dpi": "TEXT", "scale": "TEXT",
            "build_id": "TEXT", "build_incremental": "TEXT",
            "build_fingerprint": "TEXT", "security_patch": "TEXT",
            "language": "TEXT", "country": "TEXT",
            "timezone_offset": "INTEGER",
            "carrier_name": "TEXT", "carrier_mcc": "TEXT", "carrier_mnc": "TEXT",
            "family_device_id": "TEXT", "phone_id": "TEXT",
            "bio_hash": "TEXT",
            "last_error_detail": "TEXT",
            "bio_updated_at": "TIMESTAMP",
            "sort_order": "INTEGER",
            "import_batch": "TEXT",
            "advertising_id": "TEXT",
        }
        with self._cursor() as c:
            for col, typ in new_cols.items():
                if col not in cols:
                    try:
                        c.execute(f"ALTER TABLE accounts ADD COLUMN {col} {typ}")
                    except sqlite3.OperationalError:
                        pass

            # ── индекс только ПОСЛЕ того как колонка точно существует ──
            try:
                c.execute(
                    "CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_avg_views "
                    "ON accounts(avg_views_per_reel)"
                )
            except sqlite3.OperationalError:
                pass

        # ── reels_uploads: догоняем новые колонки ──
        with self._cursor() as c:
            upload_cols = {r[1] for r in c.execute("PRAGMA table_info(reels_uploads)")}
            for col, typ in [
                ("cover_filename", "TEXT"),
                ("caption_hash", "TEXT"),
                ("caption_preview", "TEXT"),
            ]:
                if col not in upload_cols:
                    try:
                        c.execute(f"ALTER TABLE reels_uploads ADD COLUMN {col} {typ}")
                    except sqlite3.OperationalError:
                        pass

            # ── теперь безопасно создать индекс на caption_hash (колонка уже есть) ──
            try:
                c.execute(
                    "CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_uploads_caption "
                    "ON reels_uploads(caption_hash)"
                )
            except sqlite3.OperationalError:
                pass

    @contextmanager
    def _cursor(self):
        with self._lock:
            cur = self.conn.cursor()
            try:
                yield cur
            finally:
                cur.close()

    # ============================================================ маркеры
    def mark_setup_done(self, login: str):
        with self._cursor() as c:
            c.execute(
                "UPDATE accounts SET first_setup_done=1 WHERE login=?",
                (login,)
            )

    def mark_public_verified(self, login: str):
        """Помечает что аккаунт уже проверен как публичный — больше не проверяем."""
        with self._cursor() as c:
            c.execute(
                "UPDATE accounts SET is_public_verified=1 WHERE login=?",
                (login,)
            )

    def set_bio_hash(self, login: str, bio_hash: str):
        with self._cursor() as c:
            c.execute(
                "UPDATE accounts SET bio_hash=?, bio_updated_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP "
                "WHERE login=?", (bio_hash, login)
            )

    # ============================================================ accounts
    def upsert_account(self, acc: dict):
        """Универсальный upsert. sort_order присваивается только при INSERT."""
        import json as _json

        def _safe_json(obj):
            return _json.dumps(obj, default=lambda o:
                               list(o) if isinstance(o, (set, frozenset))
                               else str(o))

        acc = dict(acc)
        if acc.get("cookies") and not acc.get("cookies_json"):
            acc["cookies_json"] = _safe_json(acc.pop("cookies"))
        sa = acc.get("supported_abis")
        if isinstance(sa, (list, set, frozenset)):
            acc["supported_abis"] = _safe_json(sa)

        for k, v in list(acc.items()):
            if isinstance(v, (set, frozenset)):
                acc[k] = list(v)

        with self._cursor() as c:
            known = {r[1] for r in c.execute("PRAGMA table_info(accounts)")}

            exists = c.execute("SELECT 1 FROM accounts WHERE login=?",
                               (acc["login"],)).fetchone()
            if not exists and "sort_order" in known:
                max_order = c.execute("SELECT COALESCE(MAX(sort_order), 0) FROM accounts").fetchone()[0]
                acc["sort_order"] = max_order + 1

        payload = {k: v for k, v in acc.items() if k in known}
        if "login" not in payload or "password" not in payload:
            raise ValueError("login and password required")

        cols = list(payload.keys())
        placeholders = ",".join(f":{k}" for k in cols)
        protected = {"sort_order", "import_batch"}
        updates = ",".join(f"{k}=excluded.{k}" for k in cols
                           if k != "login" and k not in protected)
        sql = (f"INSERT INTO accounts ({','.join(cols)}) VALUES ({placeholders}) "
               f"ON CONFLICT(login) DO UPDATE SET {updates}, "
               f"updated_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP")
        with self._cursor() as c:
            c.execute(sql, payload)

    def bulk_upsert_accounts(self, accounts: list[dict], batch_size: int = 500):
        """Массовый импорт — одна транзакция вместо N запросов."""
        import json as _json

        if not accounts:
            return 0, 0

        def _safe_json(obj):
            return _json.dumps(obj, default=lambda o:
                              list(o) if isinstance(o, (set, frozenset)) else str(o))

        prepared = []
        for acc in accounts:
            acc = dict(acc)
            if acc.get("cookies") and not acc.get("cookies_json"):
                acc["cookies_json"] = _safe_json(acc.pop("cookies"))
            sa = acc.get("supported_abis")
            if isinstance(sa, (list, set, frozenset)):
                acc["supported_abis"] = _safe_json(sa)
            for k, v in list(acc.items()):
                if isinstance(v, (set, frozenset)):
                    acc[k] = list(v)
            if not acc.get("login") or not acc.get("password"):
                continue
            prepared.append(acc)

        inserted = 0
        updated = 0

        with self._cursor() as c:
            known = {r[1] for r in c.execute("PRAGMA table_info(accounts)")}
            max_order = c.execute("SELECT COALESCE(MAX(sort_order), 0) FROM accounts").fetchone()[0]
            existing = {r[0] for r in c.execute("SELECT login FROM accounts")}

            c.execute("BEGIN TRANSACTION")
            try:
                for i, acc in enumerate(prepared):
                    if acc["login"] not in existing:
                        if "sort_order" in known:
                            max_order += 1
                            acc["sort_order"] = max_order
                        inserted += 1
                    else:
                        updated += 1

                    payload = {k: v for k, v in acc.items() if k in known}
                    cols = list(payload.keys())
                    placeholders = ",".join(f":{k}" for k in cols)
                    protected = {"sort_order", "import_batch"}
                    updates_str = ",".join(f"{k}=excluded.{k}" for k in cols
                                           if k != "login" and k not in protected)
                    sql = (f"INSERT INTO accounts ({','.join(cols)}) VALUES ({placeholders}) "
                           f"ON CONFLICT(login) DO UPDATE SET {updates_str}, "
                           f"updated_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP")
                    c.execute(sql, payload)

                c.execute("COMMIT")
            except Exception:
                c.execute("ROLLBACK")
                raise

        return inserted, updated

    def list_accounts(self) -> list[dict]:
        """Быстрый SELECT + ленивая десериализация JSON."""
        with self._cursor() as c:
            rows = c.execute(
                "SELECT * FROM accounts "
                "ORDER BY COALESCE(sort_order, 999999999), login"
            ).fetchall()

        out = []
        for r in rows:
            d = dict(r)
            sa = d.get("supported_abis")
            if sa and isinstance(sa, str) and sa.startswith("["):
                try:
                    d["supported_abis"] = json.loads(sa)
                except Exception:
                    pass
            out.append(d)
        return out

    def list_logins(self) -> list[str]:
        with self._cursor() as c:
            return [r["login"] for r in c.execute("SELECT login FROM accounts")]

    def delete_account(self, login: str):
        with self._cursor() as c:
            c.execute("DELETE FROM accounts WHERE login=?", (login,))

    def delete_accounts_bulk(self, logins: list[str]):
        """Массовое удаление акков одним пачечным запросом."""
        if not logins:
            return 0

        deleted = 0
        with self._cursor() as c:
            for i in range(0, len(logins), 500):
                chunk = logins[i:i + 500]
                placeholders = ",".join("?" for _ in chunk)

                c.execute(
                    f"UPDATE proxies SET bound_to=NULL "
                    f"WHERE bound_to IN ({placeholders})", chunk)
                r = c.execute(
                    f"DELETE FROM accounts WHERE login IN ({placeholders})", chunk)
                deleted += r.rowcount

                try:
                    c.execute(
                        f"DELETE FROM task_history WHERE login IN ({placeholders})",
                        chunk)
                except Exception:
                    pass
                try:
                    c.execute(
                        f"DELETE FROM reels_uploads WHERE login IN ({placeholders})",
                        chunk)
                except Exception:
                    pass
        return deleted

    def clear_accounts(self):
        with self._cursor() as c:
            c.execute("DELETE FROM accounts")

    def set_status(self, login: str, status: str, err: str = ""):
        with self._cursor() as c:
            c.execute(
                "UPDATE accounts SET status=?, last_error=?, updated_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP "
                "WHERE login=?", (status, err, login))

    def update_stats(self, login: str, stats: dict):
        """Универсальное обновление метрик акка."""
        followers   = int(stats.get("followers", 0) or 0)
        following   = int(stats.get("following", 0) or 0)
        reels_count = int(stats.get("reels_count", 0) or 0)
        total_views = int(stats.get("total_views", 0) or 0)
        total_likes = int(stats.get("total_likes", 0) or 0)
        avg = total_views / reels_count if reels_count > 0 else 0.0

        with self._cursor() as c:
            c.execute(
                "UPDATE accounts SET followers=?, following=?, "
                "reels_count=?, total_views=?, total_likes=?, "
                "avg_views_per_reel=?, stats_updated_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP "
                "WHERE login=?",
                (followers, following, reels_count, total_views,
                 total_likes, avg, login)
            )

    def find_trash_accounts(self,
                            min_reels: int = 10,
                            max_avg_views: float = 10.0,
                            max_total_views: int | None = None) -> list[dict]:
        """Мусорные акки: reels_count >= min_reels И avg <= max_avg_views."""
        with self._cursor() as c:
            if max_total_views is not None:
                rows = c.execute("""
                    SELECT login, reels_count, total_views, total_likes,
                           followers, avg_views_per_reel, import_batch,
                           stats_updated_at
                    FROM accounts
                    WHERE stats_updated_at IS NOT NULL
                      AND reels_count >= ?
                      AND (avg_views_per_reel <= ? OR total_views <= ?)
                    ORDER BY avg_views_per_reel ASC
                """, (min_reels, max_avg_views, max_total_views)).fetchall()
            else:
                rows = c.execute("""
                    SELECT login, reels_count, total_views, total_likes,
                           followers, avg_views_per_reel, import_batch,
                           stats_updated_at
                    FROM accounts
                    WHERE stats_updated_at IS NOT NULL
                      AND reels_count >= ?
                      AND avg_views_per_reel <= ?
                    ORDER BY avg_views_per_reel ASC
                """, (min_reels, max_avg_views)).fetchall()
        return [dict(r) for r in rows]

    # ============================================================ ТОП акки + их обложки
    def find_top_accounts(self, min_avg_views: float = 500.0,
                          min_reels: int = 1) -> list[dict]:
        """
        Возвращает акки с высоким средним просмотром — те где 'выстрелили' обложки.
        Отсортировано по avg_views_per_reel DESC.
        Используй для ручного просмотра залётов.
        """
        with self._cursor() as c:
            rows = c.execute("""
                SELECT login, import_batch, reels_count, total_views,
                       total_likes, avg_views_per_reel, followers,
                       stats_updated_at
                FROM accounts
                WHERE stats_updated_at IS NOT NULL
                  AND reels_count >= ?
                  AND avg_views_per_reel >= ?
                ORDER BY avg_views_per_reel DESC
            """, (min_reels, min_avg_views)).fetchall()
        return [dict(r) for r in rows]

    def get_covers_for_logins(self, logins: list[str]) -> list[dict]:
        """
        Возвращает список [{login, cover_filename, media_id, code, uploaded_at}]
        для указанных логинов. Показывает какие обложки использовались.
        """
        if not logins:
            return []
        placeholders = ",".join("?" for _ in logins)
        with self._cursor() as c:
            rows = c.execute(f"""
                SELECT login, cover_filename, media_id, code, uploaded_at
                FROM reels_uploads
                WHERE login IN ({placeholders})
                  AND cover_filename IS NOT NULL
                  AND cover_filename != ''
                ORDER BY uploaded_at DESC
            """, logins).fetchall()
        return [dict(r) for r in rows]

    def get_top_covers(self, min_avg_views: float = 500.0,
                       min_reels: int = 1) -> list[dict]:
        """
        Возвращает [{cover_filename, login, avg_views_per_reel, media_id, code}]
        отсортированные по среднему просмотру — все обложки от 'залетевших' акков.
        Удобно для ручной сортировки.
        """
        with self._cursor() as c:
            rows = c.execute("""
                SELECT ru.cover_filename, ru.login, ru.media_id, ru.code,
                       a.avg_views_per_reel, a.total_views, a.reels_count,
                       a.import_batch, ru.uploaded_at
                FROM reels_uploads ru
                JOIN accounts a ON a.login = ru.login
                WHERE ru.cover_filename IS NOT NULL
                  AND ru.cover_filename != ''
                  AND a.stats_updated_at IS NOT NULL
                  AND a.reels_count >= ?
                  AND a.avg_views_per_reel >= ?
                ORDER BY a.avg_views_per_reel DESC
            """, (min_reels, min_avg_views)).fetchall()
        return [dict(r) for r in rows]

    def save_cookies(self, login: str, cookies: dict):
        safe = {k: (list(v) if isinstance(v, (set, frozenset)) else v)
                for k, v in cookies.items()}
        data = json.dumps(safe, default=lambda o:
                          list(o) if isinstance(o, (set, frozenset)) else str(o))
        with self._cursor() as c:
            c.execute("UPDATE accounts SET cookies_json=? WHERE login=?",
                      (data, login))

    # ============================================================ proxies
    def upsert_proxy(self, p: dict):
        with self._cursor() as c:
            c.execute("""
                INSERT INTO proxies (proxy, type, geo, status, latency)
                VALUES (:proxy, :type, :geo, :status, :latency)
                ON CONFLICT(proxy) DO UPDATE SET
                    type=excluded.type, geo=COALESCE(excluded.geo, geo)
            """, {
                "proxy": p["proxy"], "type": p.get("type", "http"),
                "geo": p.get("geo"), "status": p.get("status", "unknown"),
                "latency": p.get("latency", -1),
            })

    def list_proxies(self) -> list[dict]:
        with self._cursor() as c:
            return [dict(r) for r in c.execute("SELECT * FROM proxies")]

    def delete_proxy(self, proxy: str):
        with self._cursor() as c:
            c.execute("DELETE FROM proxies WHERE proxy=?", (proxy,))

    def clear_proxies(self):
        with self._cursor() as c:
            c.execute("DELETE FROM proxies")

    def update_proxy_status(self, proxy: str, status: str, latency: int):
        with self._cursor() as c:
            c.execute(
                "UPDATE proxies SET status=?, latency=?, checked_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP "
                "WHERE proxy=?", (status, latency, proxy))

    def bind_proxy(self, proxy: str, login: str):
        with self._cursor() as c:
            c.execute("UPDATE proxies SET bound_to=? WHERE proxy=?", (login, proxy))

    def get_free_proxy(self) -> str | None:
        with self._cursor() as c:
            r = c.execute(
                "SELECT proxy FROM proxies WHERE status='ok' "
                "AND (bound_to IS NULL OR bound_to='') "
                "ORDER BY latency ASC LIMIT 1").fetchone()
        return r["proxy"] if r else None

    # ============================================================ task_history
    def log_task_run(self, login: str, action: str, status: str = "success"):
        """Записывает факт выполнения задачи."""
        with self._cursor() as c:
            c.execute(
                "INSERT INTO task_history (login, action, status) VALUES (?, ?, ?)",
                (login, action, status)
            )

    def get_last_runs(self, logins: list[str], action: str) -> dict[str, float]:
        """{login: unix_timestamp_последнего_запуска} для указанных логинов."""
        if not logins:
            return {}
        placeholders = ",".join("?" for _ in logins)
        q = (
            f"SELECT login, MAX(strftime('%s', ran_at)) AS ts "
            f"FROM task_history "
            f"WHERE action=? AND login IN ({placeholders}) "
            f"GROUP BY login"
        )
        with self._cursor() as c:
            rows = c.execute(q, (action, *logins)).fetchall()
        return {r["login"]: float(r["ts"]) for r in rows if r["ts"]}

    def clear_task_history(self, login: str = None, action: str = None):
        """Очистить историю. Без параметров — всё. Иначе по фильтрам."""
        with self._cursor() as c:
            if login and action:
                c.execute("DELETE FROM task_history WHERE login=? AND action=?", (login, action))
            elif login:
                c.execute("DELETE FROM task_history WHERE login=?", (login,))
            elif action:
                c.execute("DELETE FROM task_history WHERE action=?", (action,))
            else:
                c.execute("DELETE FROM task_history")

    # ============================================================ uploads
    def log_upload(self, login: str, media_id: str, code: str,
                   cover_filename: str = None,
                   caption_hash: str = None,
                   caption_preview: str = None):
        """Записывает залив с обложкой и хэшем/превью caption."""
        with self._cursor() as c:
            c.execute(
                "INSERT INTO reels_uploads "
                "(login, media_id, code, cover_filename, caption_hash, caption_preview) "
                "VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?)",
                (login, media_id, code, cover_filename, caption_hash, caption_preview)
            )
    def get_caption_stats(self, min_uploads: int = 5,
                          min_avg_views: float = 0.0) -> list[dict]:
        """
        Аналитика captions: группирует по caption_hash, считает средние метрики
        из stat акков которые использовали этот caption.
        
        min_uploads — не показывать captions использованные < N раз (шум).
        min_avg_views — фильтр по минимальному среднему просмотру.
        """
        with self._cursor() as c:
            rows = c.execute("""
                SELECT 
                    ru.caption_hash,
                    ru.caption_preview,
                    COUNT(DISTINCT ru.id) AS n_uploads,
                    COUNT(DISTINCT ru.login) AS n_accounts,
                    AVG(a.avg_views_per_reel) AS avg_views,
                    AVG(a.total_likes / NULLIF(a.reels_count, 0)) AS avg_likes,
                    SUM(a.total_views) AS total_views_sum,
                    MIN(ru.uploaded_at) AS first_used,
                    MAX(ru.uploaded_at) AS last_used
                FROM reels_uploads ru
                JOIN accounts a ON a.login = ru.login
                WHERE ru.caption_hash IS NOT NULL
                  AND ru.caption_hash != ''
                  AND a.stats_updated_at IS NOT NULL
                  AND a.reels_count > 0
                GROUP BY ru.caption_hash
                HAVING COUNT(DISTINCT ru.id) >= ?
                   AND AVG(a.avg_views_per_reel) >= ?
                ORDER BY avg_views DESC
            """, (min_uploads, min_avg_views)).fetchall()
        return [dict(r) for r in rows]

    def get_uploads_by_caption(self, caption_hash: str) -> list[dict]:
        """Все заливы с указанным caption — для drilldown."""
        with self._cursor() as c:
            rows = c.execute("""
                SELECT ru.login, ru.media_id, ru.code, ru.cover_filename,
                       ru.uploaded_at, a.avg_views_per_reel, a.reels_count,
                       a.import_batch
                FROM reels_uploads ru
                JOIN accounts a ON a.login = ru.login
                WHERE ru.caption_hash = ?
                  AND a.stats_updated_at IS NOT NULL
                ORDER BY a.avg_views_per_reel DESC
            """, (caption_hash,)).fetchall()
        return [dict(r) for r in rows]
    # ═══════════════ EXPERIMENTS ═══════════════
    def log_experiment(self, row: dict):
        with self._cursor() as c:
            c.execute("""
                INSERT INTO experiments
                (login, action, outcome, err_type, variant, platform,
                 is_fresh, params_hash, params_json)
                VALUES (:login,:action,:outcome,:err_type,:variant,:platform,
                        :is_fresh,:params_hash,:params_json)
            """, row)

    def get_experiments_by_login(self, login: str, limit: int = 100):
        with self._cursor() as c:
            rows = c.execute("""
                SELECT * FROM experiments WHERE login=?
                ORDER BY logged_at DESC LIMIT ?
            """, (login, limit)).fetchall()
        return [dict(r) for r in rows]

    def get_variants_summary(self, hours: int = None):
        with self._cursor() as c:
            if hours:
                since = f"datetime('now', '-{hours} hours')"
                rows = c.execute(f"""
                    SELECT variant, params_hash, params_json, outcome, COUNT(*) as n
                    FROM experiments
                    WHERE logged_at > {since}
                    GROUP BY variant, params_hash, outcome
                    ORDER BY variant, params_hash
                """).fetchall()
            else:
                rows = c.execute("""
                    SELECT variant, params_hash, params_json, outcome, COUNT(*) as n
                    FROM experiments
                    GROUP BY variant, params_hash, outcome
                    ORDER BY variant, params_hash
                """).fetchall()
        return [dict(r) for r in rows]

    # ═══════════════ ALIVE CHECK ═══════════════
    def mark_alive(self, login: str):
        with self._cursor() as c:
            c.execute("UPDATE accounts SET status='idle', last_error=NULL, "
                     "last_alive_check=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP WHERE login=?", (login,))

    def get_ios_accounts(self) -> list[dict]:
        """Возвращает только iOS IAM акки."""
        with self._cursor() as c:
            rows = c.execute("""
                SELECT * FROM accounts
                WHERE is_ios_iam = 1 OR platform = 'ios'
                ORDER BY COALESCE(sort_order, 999999999), login
            """).fetchall()
        out = []
        for r in rows:
            d = dict(r)
            import json as _j
            sa = d.get("supported_abis")
            if sa and isinstance(sa, str) and sa.startswith("["):
                try: d["supported_abis"] = _j.loads(sa)
                except Exception: pass
            out.append(d)
        return out
    def get_test_uploads(self, test_id: str, alive_only: bool = True) -> list[dict]:
        """
        Возвращает залитые в тесте рилсы, склеенные с acc и test_lab_runs.
        alive_only=True → только те где acc.status alive/success/idle
                          (мёртвые проверять бесполезно, IG вернёт 404).
        """
        with self._cursor() as c:
            # берём начало теста чтобы фильтровать uploads только за период теста
            row = c.execute(
                "SELECT MIN(started_at) FROM test_lab_runs WHERE test_id=?",
                (test_id,)).fetchone()
            if not row or not row[0]:
                return []
            test_start = row[0]

            alive_filter = ""
            if alive_only:
                alive_filter = " AND a.status IN ('idle','success','running','rate_limited') "

            rows = c.execute(f"""
                SELECT tlr.test_id, tlr.group_letter, tlr.preset,
                       ru.login, ru.media_id, ru.code, ru.cover_filename,
                       ru.caption_preview, ru.uploaded_at,
                       a.proxy, a.cookies_json, a.status,
                       a.platform, a.is_ios_iam
                FROM test_lab_runs tlr
                JOIN reels_uploads ru ON ru.login = tlr.login
                LEFT JOIN accounts a ON a.login = tlr.login
                WHERE tlr.test_id = ?
                  AND ru.uploaded_at >= ?
                  {alive_filter}
                ORDER BY tlr.group_letter, ru.uploaded_at DESC
            """, (test_id, test_start)).fetchall()
        return [dict(r) for r in rows]
storage/importer — копия.py
"""Импорт аккаунтов. IAM формат + смешанные login:pass:totp:...:android-XXX и другие."""
import re
import json


class AccountImporter:
    SEPS = [":", "|", ";", ","]

    UA_ANDROID_RE = re.compile(
        r"Instagram\s+(\S+)\s+Android\s+\("
        r"(\d+)/([\d.]+);\s*(\d+)dpi;\s*(\d+x\d+);\s*"
        r"([^;]+?);\s*([^;]+?);\s*([^;]+?);\s*([^;]+?);\s*"
        r"([\w_-]+);\s*(\d+)\)"
    )
    UA_IOS_RE = re.compile(
        r"Instagram\s+(\S+)\s+\("
        r"(iPhone[\d,]+);\s*iOS\s+([\d_]+);\s*"
        r"([\w_-]+);\s*([\w-]+);\s*scale=([\d.]+);\s*"
        r"(\d+x\d+);\s*(\d+)\)"
    )

    ANDROID_ID_RE = re.compile(r"android-([0-9a-fA-F]{16})")
    UUID_RE = re.compile(r"[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}", re.I)
    TOTP_RE = re.compile(r"^[A-Z2-7]{16,32}$")
    IP_RE = re.compile(r"^\d{1,3}(\.\d{1,3}){3}$")
    COOKIE_KEYS = ("sessionid", "ds_user_id", "csrftoken", "mid", "ig_did",
                   "rur", "Authorization", "authorization")

    @classmethod
    def parse_line(cls, line: str) -> dict | None:
        line = line.strip()

        if not line or line.startswith("#"):
            return None

        # ─── Extended/IAM формат детектится по наличию | и любого из маркеров ───
        is_extended = "|" in line and (
            "|Instagram " in line
            or "android-" in line
            or "sessionid=" in line
            or "Authorization=" in line.replace(" ", "")
            or ";ds_user_id=" in line
        )

        if is_extended:
            return cls._parse_iam(line)

        if line.startswith("{"):
            return cls._parse_json(line)

        return cls._parse_mixed(line)

    @classmethod
    def _parse_iam(cls, line: str) -> dict | None:
        """
        IAM-подобный формат: блоки разделены `|`.
        Каждый блок анализируется на содержимое, не привязываясь к позиции.
        """
        parts = [p.strip() for p in line.split("|")]

        if len(parts) < 2:
            return None

        acc = {"platform": "android"}

        for i, block in enumerate(parts):
            if not block:
                continue

            # 1) login:pass — обычно первый блок
            if i == 0 and ":" in block and "Instagram" not in block:
                cred = block.split(":")
                acc["login"] = cred[0].strip()
                acc["password"] = cred[1].strip()
                # 3-й элемент = TOTP если base32
                if len(cred) >= 3:
                    third = cred[2].strip()
                    if cls.TOTP_RE.match(third):
                        acc["totp_secret"] = third
                        # если ещё есть части — они значимы для чего-то
                        # но пока их игнорируем, редкий случай
                    elif len(cred) > 3:
                        # пароль реально содержит `:` (редко) — склеиваем
                        acc["password"] = ":".join(cred[1:]).strip()
                continue

            # 2) UA
            if block.startswith("Instagram "):
                cls._merge_ua(acc, block)
                continue

            # 3) UUID/android_id блок (содержит android- или UUID, разделён `;`)
            if (
                ("android-" in block or cls.UUID_RE.search(block))
                and ";" in block
                and "=" not in block
            ):
                cls._merge_uuids(acc, block)
                continue

            # 4) cookies/headers блок
            if "=" in block and (
                ";" in block
                or "sessionid" in block.lower()
                or "authorization" in block.lower()
            ):
                cls._merge_cookies_and_headers(acc, block)
                continue

            # 5) email
            if "@" in block and "." in block and " " not in block:
                acc["email"] = block
                continue

            # 6) proxy
            if "://" in block or "@" in block:
                acc["proxy"] = block
                continue

            # ip:port variants
            if (
                block.count(":") in (1, 3)
                and any(c.isdigit() for c in block.split(":")[0])
            ):
                if cls.IP_RE.match(block.split(":")[0]):
                    acc["proxy"] = block
                    continue

            # 7) email password (короткая строка после email)
            if (
                acc.get("email")
                and not acc.get("email_password")
                and len(block) < 40
                and " " not in block
            ):
                acc["email_password"] = block

        return (
            acc
            if acc.get("login") and acc.get("password")
            else None
        )

    @classmethod
    def _merge_ua(cls, acc, ua):
        ua = ua.strip()
        acc["user_agent"] = ua
        m = cls.UA_ANDROID_RE.search(ua)
        if m:
            ig_ver, api, release, dpi, res, manu, dev, model, cpu, locale, code = m.groups()
            manu_part, brand_part = manu.split("/", 1) if "/" in manu else (manu, manu.lower())
            acc.update({
                "platform": "android",
                "ig_version": ig_ver, "ig_version_code": code,
                "os_api": api, "os_release": release,
                "dpi": f"{dpi}dpi", "resolution": res,
                "manufacturer": manu_part.strip(), "brand": brand_part.strip(),
                "device_name": dev.strip(), "model": model.strip(),
                "hardware": cpu.strip(), "cpu": cpu.strip(),
                "locale": locale, "language": locale[:2],
            })
            return
        m = cls.UA_IOS_RE.search(ua)
        if m:
            ig_ver, machine, ios_ver, locale, lang, scale, res, code = m.groups()
            acc.update({
                "platform": "ios",
                "ig_version": ig_ver, "ig_version_code": code,
                "os_release": ios_ver.replace("_", "."),
                "resolution": res, "scale": scale,
                "manufacturer": "Apple", "brand": "Apple",
                "model": machine, "device_name": machine,
                "locale": locale, "language": lang,
            })

    @classmethod
    def _merge_uuids(cls, acc, blob):
        toks = [t.strip() for t in blob.split(";") if t.strip()]
        uuid_slots = ["device_id", "family_device_id", "phone_id", "advertising_id"]
        idx = 0
        for t in toks:
            m = cls.ANDROID_ID_RE.match(t)
            if m:
                acc["android_id"] = m.group(1)
            elif cls.UUID_RE.fullmatch(t):
                if idx < len(uuid_slots):
                    acc[uuid_slots[idx]] = t
                    idx += 1

    @classmethod
    def _merge_cookies_and_headers(cls, acc: dict, blob: str):
        """
        Разбирает блок вида:
          mid=X;ds_user_id=Y;sessionid=Z;Authorization=Bearer IGT:2:...;X-MID=X;X-IG-WWW-Claim=0
        Реальные cookies → в acc['cookies']
        Заголовки (Authorization, X-MID, X-IG-WWW-Claim) → тоже в cookies dict,
        а IGSession уже разделит их сама.
        """
        cookies = acc.get("cookies") or {}

        for pair in blob.split(";"):
            pair = pair.strip()

            if "=" not in pair:
                continue

            k, v = pair.split("=", 1)
            k, v = k.strip(), v.strip()

            if not k or not v:
                continue

            cookies[k] = v

        if cookies:
            acc["cookies"] = cookies

    # ============================================================
    @classmethod
    def _parse_json(cls, line):
        try:
            j = json.loads(line)
        except Exception:
            return None
        return {
            "login":          j.get("username") or j.get("login"),
            "password":       j.get("password", ""),
            "email":          j.get("email"),
            "email_password": j.get("email_password"),
            "totp_secret":    j.get("totp") or j.get("totp_secret"),
            "proxy":          j.get("proxy"),
            "user_agent":     j.get("user_agent") or j.get("userAgent"),
            "device_id":      j.get("device_id"),
            "android_id":     j.get("android_id"),
            "cookies":        {k: v for k, v in j.items() if k in cls.COOKIE_KEYS},
        }

    # ============================================================
    @classmethod
    def _parse_mixed(cls, line):
        """
        Универсальный парсер, ловит:
          login:pass
          login:pass:totp
          login:pass:totp:email:emailpass
          login:pass:totp::android-XXX
          login:pass:email:emailpass:ip:port
          login:pass:email:emailpass:ip:port:user:pass
          login:pass:totp:email:emailpass:sessionid=X;ds_user_id=Y;...
          и любые перестановки — детектит каждый токен эвристически.
        """
        # определяем основной разделитель — обычно `:`, но может быть `|` для extended
        sep = max(cls.SEPS, key=lambda s: line.count(s))
        parts = [p.strip() for p in line.split(sep)]
        if len(parts) < 2:
            return None

        acc = {"login": parts[0], "password": parts[1]}
        rest = parts[2:]

        for t in rest:
            if not t:
                continue
            # cookies-блок целиком (содержит = и ;)
            if "=" in t and (";" in t or "sessionid" in t or "Authorization" in t):
                cls._merge_cookies(acc, t)
                continue
            # email + следующий пасс?
            if "@" in t and "." in t:
                acc["email"] = t
                # ждём чтобы след. токен был паролем; обрабатывается ниже
                continue
            # android-XXX
            m = cls.ANDROID_ID_RE.match(t)
            if m:
                acc["android_id"] = m.group(1)
                continue
            # чистый UUID
            if cls.UUID_RE.fullmatch(t):
                # первый — device_id, второй — family_did, и т.д.
                for slot in ("device_id", "family_device_id", "phone_id", "advertising_id"):
                    if slot not in acc:
                        acc[slot] = t
                        break
                continue
            # TOTP secret (base32, 16-32 симв)
            if cls.TOTP_RE.match(t):
                acc["totp_secret"] = t
                continue
            # прокси (URL или user:pass@ip:port или ip:port)
            if "://" in t or "@" in t:
                acc["proxy"] = t
                continue
            # proxy type
            if t.lower() in ("http", "https", "socks4", "socks5"):
                acc["proxy_type"] = t.lower()
                continue
            # UA (короткая проверка)
            if t.startswith("Instagram "):
                cls._merge_ua(acc, t)
                continue

        # ─── попытка «поженить» email + email_password если email найден ───
        if acc.get("email") and not acc.get("email_password"):
            # ищем в rest токен идущий сразу после email — если он не выглядит как что-то распознанное
            try:
                idx = rest.index(acc["email"])
                if idx + 1 < len(rest):
                    candidate = rest[idx + 1]
                    if candidate and not any(
                        cls.ANDROID_ID_RE.match(candidate),
                        cls.UUID_RE.fullmatch(candidate),
                        cls.TOTP_RE.match(candidate),
                    ):
                        acc["email_password"] = candidate
            except ValueError:
                pass

        return acc
storage/importer.py
"""Импорт аккаунтов. IAM формат + смешанные login:pass:totp:...:android-XXX и другие."""
import re
import json
from storage.ios_importer import IOSAccountImporter   # ← НОВОЕ


class AccountImporter:
    SEPS = [":", "|", ";", ","]

    UA_ANDROID_RE = re.compile(
        r"Instagram\s+(\S+)\s+Android\s+\("
        r"(\d+)/([\d.]+);\s*(\d+)dpi;\s*(\d+x\d+);\s*"
        r"([^;]+?);\s*([^;]+?);\s*([^;]+?);\s*([^;]+?);\s*"
        r"([\w_-]+);\s*(\d+)\)"
    )
    UA_IOS_RE = re.compile(
        r"Instagram\s+(\S+)\s+\("
        r"(iPhone[\d,]+);\s*iOS\s+([\d_]+);\s*"
        r"([\w_-]+);\s*([\w-]+);\s*scale=([\d.]+);\s*"
        r"(\d+x\d+);\s*(\d+)\)"
    )

    ANDROID_ID_RE = re.compile(r"android-([0-9a-fA-F]{16})")
    UUID_RE = re.compile(r"[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}", re.I)
    TOTP_RE = re.compile(r"^[A-Z2-7]{16,32}$")
    IP_RE = re.compile(r"^\d{1,3}(\.\d{1,3}){3}$")
    COOKIE_KEYS = ("sessionid", "ds_user_id", "csrftoken", "mid", "ig_did",
                   "rur", "Authorization", "authorization")

    @classmethod
    def parse_line(cls, line: str) -> dict | None:
        line = line.strip()

        if not line or line.startswith("#"):
            return None

        # ═══ iOS IAM base64 формат — распознаём и уводим в отдельный парсер ═══
        if IOSAccountImporter.detect(line):
            return IOSAccountImporter.parse_line(line)

        # Extended IAM (Android) детектится по наличию | и любого из маркеров
        is_extended = "|" in line and (
            "|Instagram " in line
            or "android-" in line
            or "sessionid=" in line
            or "Authorization=" in line.replace(" ", "")
            or ";ds_user_id=" in line
        )

        if is_extended:
            return cls._parse_iam(line)

        if line.startswith("{"):
            return cls._parse_json(line)

        return cls._parse_mixed(line)

    @classmethod
    def _parse_iam(cls, line: str) -> dict | None:
        """
        IAM-подобный формат: блоки разделены `|`.
        Поддерживает:
          login:pass|UA|uuids|cookies|...
          login|pass|2FA|cookies|email|
          login|pass|2FA|cookies||+phone
        """
        parts = [p.strip() for p in line.split("|")]
        if len(parts) < 2:
            return None

        acc = {"platform": "android"}

        # ─── Частый формат: login|pass|totp|cookies|email[|phone] ───
        # Если первый блок — просто логин (без ':'), разбираем позиционно.
        if (
            parts[0]
            and ":" not in parts[0]
            and not parts[0].startswith("Instagram ")
            and "=" not in parts[0]
        ):
            acc["login"] = parts[0]
            if len(parts) >= 2 and parts[1]:
                # второй блок — пароль (если это не cookies/UA)
                if "=" not in parts[1] and not parts[1].startswith("Instagram "):
                    acc["password"] = parts[1]
            if len(parts) >= 3 and parts[2] and cls.TOTP_RE.match(parts[2]):
                acc["totp_secret"] = parts[2]
            # остальное ниже по общим правилам (начиная с индекса 2/3)

        for i, block in enumerate(parts):
            if not block:
                continue

            # 1) login:pass — классический первый блок
            if i == 0 and ":" in block and "Instagram" not in block and "=" not in block:
                cred = block.split(":")
                acc["login"] = cred[0].strip()
                acc["password"] = cred[1].strip()
                if len(cred) >= 3:
                    third = cred[2].strip()
                    if cls.TOTP_RE.match(third):
                        acc["totp_secret"] = third
                    elif len(cred) > 3:
                        acc["password"] = ":".join(cred[1:]).strip()
                continue

            # уже разобрали login|pass позиционно — не перезаписываем
            if i == 0 and acc.get("login"):
                continue
            if i == 1 and acc.get("password") and block == acc.get("password"):
                continue
            if i == 2 and acc.get("totp_secret") and block == acc.get("totp_secret"):
                continue

            # 2) UA
            if block.startswith("Instagram "):
                cls._merge_ua(acc, block)
                continue

            # 3) UUID / android_id
            if (
                ("android-" in block or cls.UUID_RE.search(block))
                and ";" in block
                and "=" not in block
            ):
                cls._merge_uuids(acc, block)
                continue

            # 4) cookies / headers
            if "=" in block and (
                ";" in block
                or "sessionid" in block.lower()
                or "authorization" in block.lower()
                or "csrftoken" in block.lower()
            ):
                cls._merge_cookies_and_headers(acc, block)
                continue

            # 5) TOTP отдельно (если не поймали позиционно)
            if cls.TOTP_RE.match(block) and not acc.get("totp_secret"):
                acc["totp_secret"] = block
                continue

            # 6) email
            if "@" in block and "." in block and " " not in block and "=" not in block:
                acc["email"] = block
                continue

            # 7) phone (+...)
            if block.startswith("+") and any(c.isdigit() for c in block):
                acc["phone"] = block
                continue

            # 8) proxy
            if "://" in block or ("@" in block and "." in block):
                acc["proxy"] = block
                continue

            if (
                block.count(":") in (1, 3)
                and any(c.isdigit() for c in block.split(":")[0])
            ):
                if cls.IP_RE.match(block.split(":")[0]):
                    acc["proxy"] = block
                    continue

            # 9) email password
            if (
                acc.get("email")
                and not acc.get("email_password")
                and len(block) < 40
                and " " not in block
                and "=" not in block
                and not cls.TOTP_RE.match(block)
            ):
                acc["email_password"] = block

        return acc if acc.get("login") and acc.get("password") else None

    @classmethod
    def _merge_ua(cls, acc, ua):
        ua = ua.strip()
        acc["user_agent"] = ua
        m = cls.UA_ANDROID_RE.search(ua)
        if m:
            ig_ver, api, release, dpi, res, manu, dev, model, cpu, locale, code = m.groups()
            manu_part, brand_part = manu.split("/", 1) if "/" in manu else (manu, manu.lower())
            acc.update({
                "platform": "android",
                "ig_version": ig_ver, "ig_version_code": code,
                "os_api": api, "os_release": release,
                "dpi": f"{dpi}dpi", "resolution": res,
                "manufacturer": manu_part.strip(), "brand": brand_part.strip(),
                "device_name": dev.strip(), "model": model.strip(),
                "hardware": cpu.strip(), "cpu": cpu.strip(),
                "locale": locale, "language": locale[:2],
            })
            return
        m = cls.UA_IOS_RE.search(ua)
        if m:
            ig_ver, machine, ios_ver, locale, lang, scale, res, code = m.groups()
            acc.update({
                "platform": "ios",
                "ig_version": ig_ver, "ig_version_code": code,
                "os_release": ios_ver.replace("_", "."),
                "resolution": res, "scale": scale,
                "manufacturer": "Apple", "brand": "Apple",
                "model": machine, "device_name": machine,
                "locale": locale, "language": lang,
            })

    @classmethod
    def _merge_uuids(cls, acc, blob):
        toks = [t.strip() for t in blob.split(";") if t.strip()]
        uuid_slots = ["device_id", "family_device_id", "phone_id", "advertising_id"]
        idx = 0
        for t in toks:
            m = cls.ANDROID_ID_RE.match(t)
            if m:
                acc["android_id"] = m.group(1)
            elif cls.UUID_RE.fullmatch(t):
                if idx < len(uuid_slots):
                    acc[uuid_slots[idx]] = t
                    idx += 1

    @classmethod
    def _merge_cookies_and_headers(cls, acc: dict, blob: str):
        """
        Разбирает блок вида:
          mid=X;ds_user_id=Y;sessionid=Z;Authorization=Bearer IGT:2:...;X-MID=X;X-IG-WWW-Claim=0
        Реальные cookies → в acc['cookies']
        Заголовки (Authorization, X-MID, X-IG-WWW-Claim) → тоже в cookies dict,
        а IGSession уже разделит их сама.
        """
        cookies = acc.get("cookies") or {}

        for pair in blob.split(";"):
            pair = pair.strip()

            if "=" not in pair:
                continue

            k, v = pair.split("=", 1)
            k, v = k.strip(), v.strip()

            if not k or not v:
                continue

            cookies[k] = v

        if cookies:
            acc["cookies"] = cookies

    # ============================================================
    @classmethod
    def _parse_json(cls, line):
        try:
            j = json.loads(line)
        except Exception:
            return None
        return {
            "login":          j.get("username") or j.get("login"),
            "password":       j.get("password", ""),
            "email":          j.get("email"),
            "email_password": j.get("email_password"),
            "totp_secret":    j.get("totp") or j.get("totp_secret"),
            "proxy":          j.get("proxy"),
            "user_agent":     j.get("user_agent") or j.get("userAgent"),
            "device_id":      j.get("device_id"),
            "android_id":     j.get("android_id"),
            "cookies":        {k: v for k, v in j.items() if k in cls.COOKIE_KEYS},
        }

    # ============================================================
    @classmethod
    def _parse_mixed(cls, line):
        """
        Универсальный парсер, ловит:
          login:pass
          login:pass:totp
          login:pass:totp:email:emailpass
          login:pass:totp::android-XXX
          login:pass:email:emailpass:ip:port
          login:pass:email:emailpass:ip:port:user:pass
          login:pass:totp:email:emailpass:sessionid=X;ds_user_id=Y;...
          и любые перестановки — детектит каждый токен эвристически.
        """
        # определяем основной разделитель — обычно `:`, но может быть `|` для extended
        sep = max(cls.SEPS, key=lambda s: line.count(s))
        parts = [p.strip() for p in line.split(sep)]
        if len(parts) < 2:
            return None

        acc = {"login": parts[0], "password": parts[1]}
        rest = parts[2:]

        for t in rest:
            if not t:
                continue
            # cookies-блок целиком (содержит = и ;)
            if "=" in t and (";" in t or "sessionid" in t or "Authorization" in t):
                cls._merge_cookies(acc, t)
                continue
            # email + следующий пасс?
            if "@" in t and "." in t:
                acc["email"] = t
                # ждём чтобы след. токен был паролем; обрабатывается ниже
                continue
            # android-XXX
            m = cls.ANDROID_ID_RE.match(t)
            if m:
                acc["android_id"] = m.group(1)
                continue
            # чистый UUID
            if cls.UUID_RE.fullmatch(t):
                # первый — device_id, второй — family_did, и т.д.
                for slot in ("device_id", "family_device_id", "phone_id", "advertising_id"):
                    if slot not in acc:
                        acc[slot] = t
                        break
                continue
            # TOTP secret (base32, 16-32 симв)
            if cls.TOTP_RE.match(t):
                acc["totp_secret"] = t
                continue
            # прокси (URL или user:pass@ip:port или ip:port)
            if "://" in t or "@" in t:
                acc["proxy"] = t
                continue
            # proxy type
            if t.lower() in ("http", "https", "socks4", "socks5"):
                acc["proxy_type"] = t.lower()
                continue
            # UA (короткая проверка)
            if t.startswith("Instagram "):
                cls._merge_ua(acc, t)
                continue

        # ─── попытка «поженить» email + email_password если email найден ───
        if acc.get("email") and not acc.get("email_password"):
            # ищем в rest токен идущий сразу после email — если он не выглядит как что-то распознанное
            try:
                idx = rest.index(acc["email"])
                if idx + 1 < len(rest):
                    candidate = rest[idx + 1]
                    if candidate and not any(
                        cls.ANDROID_ID_RE.match(candidate),
                        cls.UUID_RE.fullmatch(candidate),
                        cls.TOTP_RE.match(candidate),
                    ):
                        acc["email_password"] = candidate
            except ValueError:
                pass

        return acc
storage/ios_importer.py
"""
Импортёр iOS IAM-аккаунтов.
Формат: login:password:totp_seed:base64_url_safe_JSON

Внутри base64 blob'а — полный слепок настоящего iPhone:
  username, password, totp_seed, email
  session: authorization (Bearer IGT:2:...), ds_user_id, mid
  device: guid, family_device_id, phone_id, device_id, pigeon_session,
          fb_anon_id, waterfall_id, reg_flow_id, aac_jid, machine_id, cloud_trust
  app: ig_version, app_id, app_version, build_number, bloks_version
  ua_profile: device_model (iPhone10,4), os_version, os_build, locale, country,
              tz_name, scale, resolution, ios_ver
  cookies: []

Такой акк НЕ ДОЛЖЕН перелогиниваться — у него уже есть Bearer-токен и
живая сессия. Валидировать один раз через /accounts/current_user/, дальше
только заливать. Login-flow только если Bearer протух (401).
"""
import base64
import json
import re
from datetime import datetime, timezone


class IOSAccountImporter:
    # login:pass:TOTP(base32 16-32):base64_urlsafe_JSON
    _LINE_RE = re.compile(
        r"^([^:\s]+):([^:]+):([A-Z2-7]{16,64}):([A-Za-z0-9+/=_\-]{200,})\s*$"
    )
    _IPHONE_MODEL_RE = re.compile(r"^iPhone\d+,\d+$")

    # iPhoneN,M → commercial model, chip
    _IPHONE_MAP = {
        "iPhone11,8": ("iPhone XR",          "A12 Bionic"),
        "iPhone12,1": ("iPhone 11",          "A13 Bionic"),
        "iPhone12,3": ("iPhone 11 Pro",      "A13 Bionic"),
        "iPhone12,5": ("iPhone 11 Pro Max",  "A13 Bionic"),
        "iPhone13,1": ("iPhone 12 mini",     "A14 Bionic"),
        "iPhone13,2": ("iPhone 12",          "A14 Bionic"),
        "iPhone13,3": ("iPhone 12 Pro",      "A14 Bionic"),
        "iPhone13,4": ("iPhone 12 Pro Max",  "A14 Bionic"),
        "iPhone14,2": ("iPhone 13 Pro",      "A15 Bionic"),
        "iPhone14,3": ("iPhone 13 Pro Max",  "A15 Bionic"),
        "iPhone14,4": ("iPhone 13 mini",     "A15 Bionic"),
        "iPhone14,5": ("iPhone 13",          "A15 Bionic"),
        "iPhone14,6": ("iPhone SE 3rd gen",  "A15 Bionic"),
        "iPhone14,7": ("iPhone 14",          "A15 Bionic"),
        "iPhone14,8": ("iPhone 14 Plus",     "A15 Bionic"),
        "iPhone15,2": ("iPhone 14 Pro",      "A16 Bionic"),
        "iPhone15,3": ("iPhone 14 Pro Max",  "A16 Bionic"),
        "iPhone15,4": ("iPhone 15",          "A16 Bionic"),
        "iPhone15,5": ("iPhone 15 Plus",     "A16 Bionic"),
        "iPhone16,1": ("iPhone 15 Pro",      "A17 Pro"),
        "iPhone16,2": ("iPhone 15 Pro Max",  "A17 Pro"),
        "iPhone17,1": ("iPhone 16 Pro",      "A18 Pro"),
        "iPhone17,2": ("iPhone 16 Pro Max",  "A18 Pro"),
        "iPhone17,3": ("iPhone 16",          "A18"),
        "iPhone17,4": ("iPhone 16 Plus",     "A18"),
    }

    # tz_name (IANA) → offset секунды. Только реально встречающееся в IAM-дампах.
    _TZ_OFFSETS = {
        "America/New_York":       -18000, "America/Detroit":     -18000,
        "America/Chicago":        -21600, "America/Denver":      -25200,
        "America/Phoenix":        -25200, "America/Los_Angeles": -28800,
        "America/Anchorage":      -32400, "Pacific/Honolulu":    -36000,
        "America/Toronto":        -18000, "America/Vancouver":   -28800,
        "America/Mexico_City":    -21600, "America/Sao_Paulo":   -10800,
        "America/Buenos_Aires":   -10800, "America/Bogota":      -18000,
        "Europe/London":               0, "Europe/Dublin":            0,
        "Europe/Lisbon":               0,
        "Europe/Berlin":            3600, "Europe/Paris":          3600,
        "Europe/Madrid":            3600, "Europe/Rome":           3600,
        "Europe/Amsterdam":         3600, "Europe/Warsaw":         3600,
        "Europe/Stockholm":         3600, "Europe/Oslo":           3600,
        "Europe/Copenhagen":        3600, "Europe/Helsinki":       7200,
        "Europe/Athens":            7200, "Europe/Bucharest":      7200,
        "Europe/Kiev":              7200, "Europe/Istanbul":      10800,
        "Europe/Moscow":           10800, "Asia/Yekaterinburg":   18000,
        "Asia/Novosibirsk":        25200, "Asia/Vladivostok":     36000,
        "Asia/Tokyo":              32400, "Asia/Seoul":           32400,
        "Asia/Shanghai":           28800, "Asia/Hong_Kong":       28800,
        "Asia/Singapore":          28800, "Asia/Bangkok":         25200,
        "Asia/Jakarta":            25200, "Asia/Manila":          28800,
        "Asia/Kolkata":            19800, "Asia/Karachi":         18000,
        "Asia/Dubai":              14400, "Asia/Riyadh":          10800,
        "Australia/Sydney":        36000, "Australia/Melbourne":  36000,
        "Australia/Perth":         28800, "Pacific/Auckland":     43200,
        "Africa/Johannesburg":      7200, "Africa/Cairo":          7200,
        "Africa/Lagos":             3600, "Africa/Nairobi":       10800,
    }

    # ── Публичный API ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
    # login:password:TOTP:cookies_string
    _COOKIE_LINE_RE = re.compile(
        r"^([^:\s]+):([^:]+):([A-Z2-7]{16,64}):(.+;.*sessionid=[^;]+.*)\s*$",
        re.IGNORECASE
    )

    @classmethod
    def detect(cls, line: str) -> bool:
        """True для ЛЮБОГО iOS формата — base64 IAM или cookie-format."""
        line = line.strip()
        return bool(cls._LINE_RE.match(line)) or \
               bool(cls._COOKIE_LINE_RE.match(line))

    @classmethod
    def parse_line(cls, line: str) -> dict | None:
        """Универсальный парсер iOS: base64 сначала, потом cookie fallback."""
        line = line.strip()
        m = cls._LINE_RE.match(line)
        if m:
            return cls._parse_base64_line(m)
        m2 = cls._COOKIE_LINE_RE.match(line)
        if m2:
            return cls._parse_cookie_line(m2)
        return None

    @classmethod
    def _parse_base64_line(cls, m) -> dict | None:
        """iOS IAM формат: login:pass:TOTP:base64(JSON) — с полным device fingerprint."""
        login, password, totp, blob = m.groups()

        try:
            data = cls._b64decode(blob)
            payload = json.loads(data.decode("utf-8", "ignore"))
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[ios-import] {login}: bad base64/JSON — {e}")
            return None

        acc = {
            "login":        payload.get("username") or login,
            "password":     payload.get("password") or password,
            "totp_secret":  payload.get("totp_seed") or totp,
            "email":        payload.get("email") or "",
            "platform":     "ios",
            "user_agent":   "",
        }

        # ── session ──
        sess = payload.get("session") or {}
        cookies: dict[str, str] = {}
        auth = sess.get("authorization") or ""
        if auth:
            cookies["Authorization"] = auth
        ds_uid = sess.get("ds_user_id")
        if ds_uid:
            cookies["ds_user_id"] = str(ds_uid)
        mid = sess.get("mid")
        if mid:
            cookies["mid"] = mid
            cookies["X-MID"] = mid
        claim = sess.get("www_claim") or sess.get("wwwClaim") or ""
        if claim:
            cookies["X-IG-WWW-Claim"] = claim
        csrf = sess.get("csrf")
        if csrf:
            cookies["csrftoken"] = csrf
        for c in payload.get("cookies") or []:
            if isinstance(c, dict) and c.get("name") and c.get("value"):
                cookies[c["name"]] = c["value"]
        if cookies:
            acc["cookies"] = cookies

        # ── device ──
        dev = payload.get("device") or {}
        acc["device_id"]        = dev.get("guid") or ""
        acc["family_device_id"] = dev.get("family_device_id") or ""
        acc["phone_id"]         = dev.get("phone_id") or ""
        acc["advertising_id"]   = dev.get("device_id") or ""

        # ── app ──
        app = payload.get("app") or {}
        acc["ig_version"]      = app.get("app_version") or app.get("ig_version") or "446.0.0"
        acc["ig_version_code"] = app.get("build_number") or "0"
        acc["bloks_version"]   = app.get("bloks_version") or ""
        acc["ig_app_id"]       = app.get("app_id") or "124024574287414"

        # ── ua_profile ──
        ua = payload.get("ua_profile") or {}
        model = ua.get("device_model") or ""
        if not cls._IPHONE_MODEL_RE.match(model):
            model = "iPhone14,5"
        commercial, chip = cls._IPHONE_MAP.get(model, ("iPhone", "A15 Bionic"))
        os_ver = (ua.get("os_ver_dotted") or ua.get("os_version") or "16.4.1").strip()
        os_build = ua.get("os_build") or ""
        locale  = ua.get("locale")  or "en_US"
        country = ua.get("country") or (locale.split("_")[1] if "_" in locale else "US")
        language = locale[:2]
        tz_name   = ua.get("tz_name") or "America/New_York"
        tz_offset = cls._TZ_OFFSETS.get(tz_name, -18000)
        scale      = ua.get("scale")      or "3.00"
        resolution = ua.get("resolution") or "1170x2532"

        acc.update({
            "manufacturer":    "Apple",
            "brand":           "Apple",
            "model":           model,
            "device_name":     model,
            "hardware":        chip,
            "cpu":             "arm64",
            "chip_commercial": chip,
            "gpu":             "Apple GPU",
            "os_release":      os_ver,
            "os_api":          "",
            "build_id":        os_build,
            "build_incremental": "",
            "build_fingerprint": f"Apple/{model}/{model}:iOS {os_ver}/{os_build}",
            "locale":          locale,
            "language":        language,
            "country":         country,
            "timezone_offset": tz_offset,
            "resolution":      resolution,
            "dpi":             "460dpi",
            "scale":           scale,
            "carrier_name":    "",
            "carrier_mcc":     "",
            "carrier_mnc":     "",
            "android_id":      "",
            "supported_abis":  ["arm64"],
            "radio_version":   "",
        })

        ios_ver_ua = os_ver.replace(".", "_")
        acc["user_agent"] = (
            f"Instagram {acc['ig_version']} ({model}; iOS {ios_ver_ua}; "
            f"{locale}; {language}; scale={scale}; {resolution}; "
            f"{acc['ig_version_code']})"
        )

        acc["is_ios_iam"] = 1
        acc["import_source"] = "ios_iam_v1"
        return acc
    @staticmethod
    def _b64decode(s: str) -> bytes:
        """base64 с восстановлением padding, поддерживает url-safe."""
        s = s.strip()
        # url-safe → стандарт
        s = s.replace("-", "+").replace("_", "/")
        pad = (-len(s)) % 4
        if pad:
            s += "=" * pad
        return base64.b64decode(s)
    @classmethod
    def _parse_cookie_line(cls, m) -> dict | None:
        """
        iOS cookie формат: login:pass:TOTP:csrftoken=X;sessionid=Y;...
        Device profile НЕ содержится — будет сгенерирован из seed=login при первом заливе.
        Использует ТОТ ЖЕ session (sessionid + ds_user_id + mid), что и base64 формат.
        """
        login, password, totp, cookies_blob = m.groups()

        cookies: dict[str, str] = {}
        for pair in cookies_blob.split(";"):
            pair = pair.strip()
            if "=" not in pair:
                continue
            k, v = pair.split("=", 1)
            k, v = k.strip(), v.strip()
            # убираем внешние кавычки типа rur="ATG..."
            if v.startswith('"') and v.endswith('"'):
                v = v[1:-1]
            if k and v:
                cookies[k] = v

        if not cookies.get("sessionid"):
            print(f"[ios-cookie] {login}: нет sessionid — пропуск")
            return None

        # X-MID для header
        if cookies.get("mid"):
            cookies["X-MID"] = cookies["mid"]

        acc = {
            "login":        login,
            "password":     password,
            "totp_secret":  totp,
            "email":        "",
            "platform":     "ios",
            "cookies":      cookies,
            "is_ios_iam":   1,
            "import_source": "ios_cookie_v1",
            # device fingerprint НЕ задан — DeviceProfile.generate() сделает
            # на лету при первом запросе (seed=login → стабильный fingerprint)
        }
        return acc

def generate_starter_pool(dst: str):
    """Раздувает базу до ~10к профилей вариациями DPI/локалей/подверсий."""
    android_pool = []
    for m, b, model, dev, cpu, rel, api, dpi, res in ANDROID:
        # для каждого устройства 3-5 вариаций DPI/разрешения
        for dpi_var in (dpi, dpi.replace("480", "420"), dpi.replace("440", "480")):
            android_pool.append({
                "manufacturer": m, "brand": b, "model": model, "device": dev,
                "cpu": cpu, "android_release": rel, "android_ver": api,
                "dpi": dpi_var, "resolution": res,
            })

    ios_pool = [{"model": m, "resolution": r, "chip": c} for m, r, c in IOS]

    # дублируем для набора количества (реальная софтина хранит уникальные, но нам нужно ≥1000 распределений)
    from pathlib import Path
    Path(dst).parent.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    with open(dst, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump({"android": android_pool, "ios": ios_pool}, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    generate_starter_pool("assets/devices.json")
    print("done")

def pick_timezone_for_locale(locale: str, geo_tz_offset: int | None = None) -> int:
    """
    Если известен реальный TZ страны прокси — используем его.
    Иначе — случайный из возможных для локали.
    """
    import random
    options = LOCALE_TZ.get(locale, [0])
    if geo_tz_offset is not None:
        # ищем ближайший
        return min(options, key=lambda x: abs(x - geo_tz_offset))
    return random.choice(options)


def pick_carrier(locale: str) -> tuple[str, str, str]:
    import random
    carriers = LOCALE_CARRIER.get(locale, [("310", "260", "T-Mobile")])
    return random.choice(carriers)


def region_suffix_for_locale(locale: str) -> str:
    """Регион для build.incremental Xiaomi/OPPO/HONOR."""
    country = LOCALE_COUNTRY.get(locale, "US")
    return {
        "RU": "RUXM", "UA": "RUXM", "BY": "RUXM",
        "IN": "INXM",
        "CN": "CNXM", "HK": "CNXM", "TW": "CNXM",
        "US": "MIXM", "CA": "MIXM", "MX": "MIXM", "BR": "MIXM",
        "TR": "TREEA", "SA": "MEAEA", "AE": "MEAEA",
        "ID": "IDXM", "TH": "IDXM", "VN": "IDXM", "MY": "IDXM",
    }.get(country, "EUEEA")
